### [1] [Reconstructing Spiking Neural Networks Using a Single Neuron with Autapses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24692)
*Wuque Cai,Hongze Sun,Quan Tang,Shifeng Mao,Zhenxing Wang,Jiayi He,Duo Chen,Dezhong Yao,Daqing Guo*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 提出TDA-SNN框架，使用单个LIF神经元和原型学习训练策略重构SNN，显著减少神经元数量和状态存储，但增加时间延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高性能SNN依赖密集多层架构，通信和状态存储成本高；受自突触启发，探索单神经元时间复用作为紧凑计算单元

Method: 提出时间延迟自突触SNN框架，使用单个LIF神经元和原型学习训练策略，通过重组内部时间状态实现储备池、MLP和卷积类架构

Result: 在序列、事件和图像基准测试中，储备池和MLP设置表现有竞争力，卷积结果显示明显时空权衡；相比标准SNN，大幅减少神经元数量和状态内存，增加单神经元信息容量

Conclusion: 时间复用单神经元模型作为紧凑计算单元在类脑计算中具有潜力，但极端单神经元设置需要额外时间延迟作为代价

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) are promising for neuromorphic computing, but high-performing models still rely on dense multilayer architectures with substantial communication and state-storage costs. Inspired by autapses, we propose time-delayed autapse SNN (TDA-SNN), a framework that reconstructs SNNs with a single leaky integrate-and-fire neuron and a prototype-learning-based training strategy. By reorganizing internal temporal states, TDA-SNN can realize reservoir, multilayer perceptron, and convolution-like spiking architectures within a unified framework. Experiments on sequential, event-based, and image benchmarks show competitive performance in reservoir and MLP settings, while convolutional results reveal a clear space--time trade-off. Compared with standard SNNs, TDA-SNN greatly reduces neuron count and state memory while increasing per-neuron information capacity, at the cost of additional temporal latency in extreme single-neuron settings. These findings highlight the potential of temporally multiplexed single-neuron models as compact computational units for brain-inspired computing.

</details>

### [2] [A formalization of the Gelfond-Schneider theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24823)
*Michail Karatarakis,Freek Wiedijk*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 在Lean 4证明助手中形式化希尔伯特第七问题及其解决方案——盖尔丰德-施奈德定理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特第七问题是希尔伯特23个问题之一，涉及超越数理论。盖尔丰德-施奈德定理是该问题的解决方案，是超越数理论的重要基石，连接了代数数论和复分析。在Lean 4中形式化这一经典定理有助于验证其证明的正确性，并为数学形式化库提供重要工具。

Method: 使用Lean 4证明助手对盖尔丰德-施奈德定理进行形式化证明。该定理表述为：若α和β是代数数，且α≠0,1，β是无理数，则α^β是超越数。形式化过程涉及代数数论、超越数理论和复分析的相关概念在证明助手环境中的实现。

Result: 成功在Lean 4中形式化了盖尔丰德-施奈德定理及其证明，验证了该定理在证明助手框架下的正确性。这为超越数理论的形式化验证提供了重要基础，并扩展了Lean数学库在数论领域的能力。

Conclusion: 盖尔丰德-施奈德定理的形式化是数学定理验证的重要进展，展示了现代证明助手处理复杂数论问题的能力。这项工作不仅验证了一个经典定理，还为未来更多超越数理论结果的形式化奠定了基础，促进了数学形式化研究的发展。

Abstract: We formalize Hilbert's Seventh Problem and its solution, the Gelfond-Schneider theorem, in the Lean 4 proof assistant. The theorem states that if $α$ and $β$ are algebraic numbers with $α\neq 0,1$ and $β$ irrational, then $α^β$ is transcendental. Originally proven independently by Gelfond and Schneider in 1934, this result is a cornerstone of transcendental number theory, bridging algebraic number theory and complex analysis.

</details>


### [3] [A Dual-Threshold Probabilistic Knowing Value Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24865)
*Shanxia Wang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种双阈值概率知道值逻辑，用于不确定多智能体环境，统一处理命题的概率阈值态度和项值的高置信度态度，特别适用于隐私敏感场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该框架的动机源于隐私敏感场景，其中攻击者可能对候选敏感值赋予高后验概率，但无法保证其真实性。需要一种形式化方法来同时处理概率认知逻辑和经典知道值逻辑，以捕捉对命题的概率阈值态度和对项值的高置信度态度。

Method: 主要方法是分离命题算子和值导向算子的阈值域：$K_i^θ$覆盖整个有理阈值区间，而知道值算子$Kv_i^η(t)$限制在$(\frac{1}{2},1]$的高阈值区间。采用基于类型空间分布和赋值配置映射的两层构造，解决概率质量分配和值敏感约束的联合实现问题。

Result: 建立了该框架的可靠公理系统，通过两层构造解决了联合实现问题，并为高阈值片段获得了结构化的弱完备性定理。当$η>\frac{1}{2}$时，两个不同的值不能同时满足阈值，从而自动获得唯一性。

Conclusion: 该双阈值概率知道值逻辑框架成功地将概率认知逻辑与经典知道值逻辑连接起来，为不确定多智能体环境中的隐私敏感分析提供了形式化工具，特别适用于攻击者具有高置信度但非事实性价值锁定的场景。

Abstract: We introduce a dual-threshold probabilistic knowing value logic for uncertain multi-agent settings. The framework captures within a single formalism both probabilistic-threshold attitudes toward propositions and high-confidence attitudes toward term values, thereby connecting probabilistic epistemic logic with classical knowing value logic. It is especially motivated by privacy-sensitive scenarios in which an attacker assigns high posterior probability to a candidate sensitive value without guaranteeing that it is the true one.
  The main idea is to separate the threshold domains of propositional and value-oriented operators. While $K_i^θ$ ranges over the full rational threshold interval, the knowing-value operator $Kv_i^η(t)$ is restricted to $(\frac{1}{2},1]$. This high-threshold restriction has a structural effect: once $η>\frac{1}{2}$, two distinct values cannot both satisfy the threshold, so uniqueness becomes automatic. Over probabilistic models with countably additive measures, $Kv_i^η(t)$ is interpreted as non-factive high-confidence value locking.
  We establish sound axiomatic systems for the framework and develop a two-layer construction based on type-space distributions and assignment-configuration mappings. This resolves the joint realization problem arising from probabilistic mass allocation and value-sensitive constraints, and yields a structured weak-completeness theorem for the high-threshold fragment.

</details>


### [4] [Normal forms in cubical type theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24923)
*Xu Huang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文档规范了立方类型理论中的正规形式定义，以更传统的方式呈现已有的归一化证明中的定义


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方类型理论中的正规形式定义虽然已存在于归一化证明中，但需要以更传统、明确的方式呈现以供参考

Method: 采用传统风格明确规范立方类型理论中的正规形式定义，而非隐藏在复杂的证明结构中

Result: 提供了立方类型理论中正规形式的明确规范定义，可作为独立参考文档使用

Conclusion: 本文档填补了立方类型理论中正规形式定义缺乏明确规范参考的空白，为后续研究和应用提供了便利

Abstract: This note documents the specification of normal forms in cubical type theory. The definition is already present in the proof of normalization for cubical type theory, but we present it in a more traditional style explicitly for reference.

</details>


### [5] [Absolute convergence and Taylor expansion in web based models of Linear Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25215)
*Christine Tasson,Aymeric Walch*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了微分λ演算的泰勒展开理论，使其适用于包含部分和且系数可为非正的web模型，并提供了统一的构造证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泰勒展开公理适用于许多具有部分和的模型，但未能涵盖经典的Köthe空间和有限性空间等web模型。需要扩展理论以覆盖这些重要模型。

Method: 1. 提出具有部分和的web模型的通用构造方法，涵盖从相干空间到概率相干空间、有限性空间和Köthe空间等模型；2. 将泰勒展开理论推广到系数可为非正的模型；3. 使用通用web模型构造为所有上述web模型提供统一的泰勒展开证明。

Result: 成功构建了包含部分和的web模型通用框架，将泰勒展开理论扩展到系数可为非正的模型，并统一证明了相干空间、概率相干空间、有限性空间和Köthe空间等经典web模型都具备泰勒展开性质。

Conclusion: 本文通过通用构造方法扩展了微分λ演算的泰勒展开理论，使其能够统一处理包括Köthe空间和有限性空间在内的多种web模型，为程序定量分析与微分运算的对应关系提供了更广泛的理论基础。

Abstract: The differential $λ$-calculus studies how the quantitative aspects of programs correspond to differentiation and to Taylor expansion inside models of linear logic. Recent work has generalized the axioms of Taylor expansion so they apply to many models that only feature partial sums. However, that work does not cover the classic web based models of K{ö}the spaces and finiteness spaces .  First, we provide a generic construction of web based models with partial sums. It captures models, ranging from coherence spaces to probabilistic coherence spaces, finiteness spaces and K{ö}the spaces. Second, we generalize the theory of Taylor expansion to models in which coefficients can be non-positive. We then use our generic web model construction to provide a unified proof that all the aforementioned web based models feature such Taylor expansion.

</details>


### [6] [A Linear-Size Block-Partition Fibonacci Encoding for Gödel Numbering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25307)
*Zoltán Sóstai*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 基于斐波那契数列块划分的字符串编码方法，将有限字母表上的字符串编码为自然数，编码长度与信息论下界匹配，避免了Rosko配对方法的指数级膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种将有限字母表上的字符串高效编码为自然数的方法，要求编码是单射的、可恢复字符串长度，且编码数字的位数增长与信息论下界匹配。同时要避免现有方法（如Rosko的二进制无进位配对）可能导致的指数级位数增长。

Method: 基于斐波那契数列的块划分编码：将斐波那契数列划分为块，每个字符串位置从专用块中选择一个斐波那契数，块间保留未使用的索引以保证非相邻性。编码数为所选斐波那契数的和，利用Zeckendorf定理保证选择的唯一性。

Result: 编码是单射的，字符串长度可从编码中恢复，最坏情况下编码数字的位数增长为Θ(m)（m为字符串长度），与信息论下界匹配。证明了Rosko的二进制无进位配对嵌套使用会导致Θ(2^m)的指数级位数增长，而块划分构造完全避免了这一问题。

Conclusion: 提出的基于斐波那契数列块划分的编码方法在理论上是高效的，达到了信息论下界，并且避免了现有方法的指数级膨胀问题，为字符串到自然数的编码提供了一种优化的解决方案。

Abstract: We construct an encoding of finite strings over a fixed finite alphabet as natural numbers, based on a block partition of the Fibonacci sequence. Each position in the string selects one Fibonacci number from a dedicated block, with unused indices between blocks guaranteeing non-adjacency. The encoded number is the sum of the selected Fibonacci numbers, and Zeckendorf's theorem guarantees that this sum uniquely determines the selection. The encoding is injective, the string length is recoverable from the code, and the worst-case digit count of the encoded number grows as $Θ(m)$ for strings of length $m$, matching the information-theoretic lower bound up to a constant factor. We also prove that the natural right-nested use of Rosko's (2025) binary carryless pairing for sequence encoding has worst-case $Θ(2^m)$ digit growth, an exponential blowup that the block-partition construction avoids entirely.

</details>


### [7] [Stone Duality for Monads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25710)
*Richard Garner,Alyssa Renata,Nicolas Wu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文构建了排名单子范畴与局部范畴内部范畴范畴之间的反变幂等伴随，建立了单子与局部范畴之间的对偶关系，称为"单子的Stone对偶"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究计算单子（Moggi意义下的计算概念）与局部范畴（理解为转移系统）之间的深层联系，建立类似Stone对偶的数学结构，统一处理计算语义与几何表示。

Method: 构造反变幂等伴随：左伴随将单子T映射到其局部行为范畴LB(T)，作为与T交互的"通用转移系统"；右伴随将局部范畴LC映射到单子Γ(LC)，其中(Γ(LC))A是源映射的A索引局部截面族集合。研究该伴随的不动点。

Result: 伴随的不动点包括：(1)超仿射-一元单子（允许只读操作预测输出）；(2)充足局部范畴（源映射是局部同胚，对象局部是强零维的）。该对偶限制到经典Stone对偶：布尔代数B作为B-划分单子对应只有恒等态射的局部范畴。

Conclusion: 建立了"单子的Stone对偶"，揭示了计算单子与局部范畴之间的深刻对应关系，统一了代数和拓扑视角，为计算语义和范畴逻辑提供了新的几何解释框架。

Abstract: We introduce a contravariant idempotent adjunction between (i) the category of ranked monads on $\mathsf{Set}$; and (ii) the category of internal categories and internal retrofunctors in the category of locales. The left adjoint takes a monad $T$-viewed as a notion of computation, following Moggi-to its localic behaviour category $\mathsf{LB}T$. This behaviour category is understood as "the universal transition system" for interacting with $T$: its "objects" are states and the "morphisms" are transitions. On the other hand, the right adjoint takes a localic category $\mathsf{LC}$-similarly understood as a transition system-to the monad $Γ\mathsf{LC}$ where $(Γ\mathsf{LC})A$ is the set of $A$-indexed families of local sections to the source map which jointly partition the locale of objects. The fixed points of this adjunction consist of (i) hyperaffine-unary monads, i.e., those monads where term $t$ admits a read-only operation $\bar{t}$ predicting the output of $t$; and (ii) ample localic categories, i.e., whose source maps are local homeomorphisms and whose locale of objects are strongly zero-dimensional. The hyperaffine-unary monads arise in earlier works by Johnstone and Garner as a syntactic characterization of those monads with Cartesian closed Eilenberg-Moore categories. This equivalence is the Stone duality for monads; so-called because it further restricts to the classical Stone duality by viewing a Boolean algebra $B$ as a monad of $B$-partitions and the corresponding Stone space as a localic category with only identity morphisms.

</details>

### [8] [Scalable Air-to-Ground Wireless Channel Modeling Using Environmental Context and Generative Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24620)
*Jingyi Tian,Lin Cai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于真实环境数据（数字高程模型和土地覆盖信息）与射线追踪的环境感知空对地无线信道建模方法，通过扩散模型实现高效预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道卫星快速运动导致传播信道快速变化，现有研究大多依赖假设的统计信道分布，忽略了实际地理环境的影响，特别是在低仰角时信号易受地形遮挡和环境效应影响

Method: 利用真实环境数据（DEM和土地覆盖信息）结合射线追踪确定LOS/NLOS状态和信号反射路径，将遮挡和反射剖面与衍射损耗、植被吸收和大气衰减模型结合，使用RT生成样本训练可扩展扩散模型以高效预测信道性能

Result: 通过蜂窝和LEO卫星链路的实测数据验证了所提模型在真实环境中的有效性，能够支持实时决策

Conclusion: 提出的环境感知信道建模方法能够更准确地表征真实地理环境中的信道行为，通过扩散模型实现计算效率与预测精度的平衡

Abstract: The fast motion of Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites causes the propagation channel to vary rapidly, and its behavior is strongly shaped by the surrounding environment, especially at low elevation angles where signals are highly susceptible to terrain blockage and other environmental effects. Existing studies mostly rely on assumed statistical channel distributions and therefore ignore the influence of the actual geographic environment. In this paper, we propose an environment-aware channel modeling method for air-to-ground wireless links. We leverage real environmental data, including digital elevation models (DEMs) and land cover information, together with ray tracing (RT) to determine whether a link is line-of-sight (LOS) or non-line-of-sight (NLOS) and to identify possible reflection paths of the signal. The resulting obstruction and reflection profiles are then combined with models of diffraction loss, vegetation absorption, and atmospheric attenuation to quantitatively characterize channel behavior in realistic geographic environments. Since RT is computationally intensive, we use RT-generated samples and environmental features to train a scalable diffusion model that can efficiently predict channel performance for arbitrary satellite and ground terminal positions, thereby supporting real-time decision-making. In the experiments, we validate the proposed model with measurement data from both cellular and LEO satellite links, demonstrating its effectiveness in realistic environments.

</details>

### [9] [Model2Kernel: Model-Aware Symbolic Execution For Safe CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24595)
*Mengting He,Shihao Xia,Haomin Jia,Wenfei Wu,Linhai Song*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Model2Kernel：首个用于自动验证LLM推理中CUDA内核内存安全性的实用系统，通过模型感知动态分析和CUDA专用符号执行发现353个未知bug


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理系统依赖CUDA内核实现核心transformer操作，但这些内核由于模型依赖的张量布局、复杂内存索引和大规模线程级并行而容易产生内存安全bug，现有技术无法有效检测LLM推理系统中的CUDA内存bug

Method: 1) 模型感知动态分析：确定每个模型如何调用内核，并将内核参数分类为模型架构固定或用户控制；2) CUDA专用符号执行：使用动态张量内存和线程标识符的新抽象来精确定位内核中的内存bug

Result: 在vLLM、Hugging Face和近期LLM研究论文的CUDA内核和模型评估中，Model2Kernel发现了353个先前未知的bug，仅产生9个误报，证明了其有效性

Conclusion: Model2Kernel是首个实用的CUDA内核内存安全验证系统，能够有效检测LLM推理系统中的内存bug，解决了现有技术无法处理可变长度内核输入和依赖不可用硬件的问题

Abstract: The widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs) has made GPU-accelerated inference a critical part of modern computing infrastructure. Production inference systems rely on CUDA kernels to implement core transformer operations, yet these kernels are highly susceptible to memory-safety bugs due to model-dependent tensor layouts, intricate memory indexing, and massive thread-level parallelism. Such bugs can corrupt model weights, crash inference services, or even enable adversarial attacks. Existing techniques either depend on unavailable hardware, incur high overhead, or fail to handle kernel inputs with variable lengths, and none can effectively detect CUDA memory bugs in LLM inference systems. This paper presents Model2Kernel, the first practical system for automatically verifying the memory safety of CUDA kernels used in LLM inference. Model2Kernel performs model-aware dynamic analysis to determine how each model invokes kernels and to classify kernel arguments as either fixed by the model architecture or controlled by model users. Using this information, Model2Kernel then applies CUDA-specialized symbolic execution, supported by new abstractions for dynamic tensor memory and thread identifiers, to accurately pinpoint memory bugs in kernels. In the evaluation on CUDA kernels and models from vLLM, Hugging Face, and recent LLM research papers, Model2Kernel discovers 353 previously unknown bugs while producing only nine false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [10] [ReSyn: A Generalized Recursive Regular Expression Synthesis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24624)
*Seongmin Kim,Hyunjoon Cheon,Su-Hyeon Kim,Yo-Sub Han,Sang-Ki Ko*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: ReSyn是一个合成器无关的分治框架，通过将复杂的正则表达式合成问题分解为可管理的子问题来解决现有PBE系统在真实世界复杂正则表达式上的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE系统依赖简化的基准测试，无法捕捉真实世界正则表达式的高结构复杂性（如深层嵌套和频繁的Union操作），导致性能下降。

Method: 提出ReSyn：合成器无关的分治框架，将复杂合成问题分解为可管理的子问题；引入Set2Regex：参数高效的合成器，捕捉示例的排列不变性。

Result: 实验结果表明，ReSyn显著提高了各种合成器的准确性，其与Set2Regex的组合在具有挑战性的真实世界基准测试中建立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: ReSyn框架通过分治策略有效解决了真实世界复杂正则表达式合成问题，结合Set2Regex合成器实现了性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Existing Programming-By-Example (PBE) systems often rely on simplified benchmarks that fail to capture the high structural complexity-such as deeper nesting and frequent Unions-of real-world regexes. To overcome the resulting performance drop, we propose ReSyn, a synthesizer-agnostic divide-and-conquer framework that decomposes complex synthesis problems into manageable sub-problems. We also introduce Set2Regex, a parameter-efficient synthesizer capturing the permutation invariance of examples. Experimental results demonstrate that ReSyn significantly boosts accuracy across various synthesizers, and its combination with Set2Regex establishes a new state-of-the-art on challenging real-world benchmark.

</details>


### [11] [Numerical Superoptimization for Library Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24812)
*Jonas Regehr,Mitch Briles,Zachary Tatlock,Pavel Panchekha*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: GrowLibm将数值超优化器重新用于数值库学习，通过挖掘候选数学原语、按反事实效用排序并剪枝冗余项，为科学应用识别出紧凑可重用的原语，显著提升内核速度和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值软件依赖于快速准确的数学原语实现。虽然现代超优化器可以针对给定原语集优化浮点内核，但更根本的问题仍未解决：哪些新原语值得实现？这涉及到数值库学习问题：给定浮点内核工作负载，识别哪些数学原语的专家实现最能提升速度和精度。

Method: GrowLibm将Herbie超优化器重新用作数值库学习器。它从超优化器的中间搜索结果中挖掘候选原语，使用反事实效用（如果某个原语可用，工作负载能改善多少）对候选进行排序，并剪枝冗余候选。该方法利用超优化器已有的机制：搜索过程枚举候选原语，等价性过程可以泛化和去重候选，成本模型可以估计反事实效用。

Result: 在三个科学应用（PROJ、CoolProp、Basilisk）中，GrowLibm识别出紧凑、可重用的原语，这些原语可以使用标准数值技术有效实现。当Herbie扩展这些专家实现后，内核速度在固定精度下提升高达2.2倍，最大可达到精度也得到改善，其中一个案例从56.0%提升到93.5%。原型LLVM匹配器在优化IR中识别学习到的原语，在五个PROJ投影中恢复了26个替换点，端到端应用性能提升高达5%。

Conclusion: 数值超优化器具备适合数值库学习问题的机制。GrowLibm通过重新利用Herbie超优化器，能够有效识别值得实现的数学原语，显著提升科学应用的性能和精度。该方法为数值库设计提供了系统化方法，并展示了在编译器优化中应用学习原语的潜力。

Abstract: Numerical software depends on fast, accurate implementations of mathematical primitives like sin, exp, and log. Modern superoptimizers can optimize floating-point kernels against a given set of such primitives, but a more fundamental question remains open: which new primitives are worth implementing in the first place?
  We formulate this as numerical library learning: given a workload of floating-point kernels, identify the mathematical primitives whose expert implementations would most improve speed and accuracy. Our key insight is that numerical superoptimizers already have the machinery well-suited to this problem. Their search procedures happen to enumerate candidate primitives, their equivalence procedures can generalize and deduplicate candidates, and their cost models can estimate counterfactual utility: how much the workload would improve if a given primitive were available.
  We present GrowLibm, which repurposes the Herbie superoptimizer as a numerical library learner. GrowLibm mines candidate primitives from the superoptimizer's intermediate search results, ranks them by counterfactual utility, and prunes redundant candidates. Across three scientific applications (PROJ, CoolProp, and Basilisk), GrowLibm identifies compact, reusable primitives that can be implemented effectively using standard numerical techniques. When Herbie is extended with these expert implementations, kernel speed improves by up to 2.2x at fixed accuracy, and maximum achievable accuracy also improves, in one case from 56.0% to 93.5%. We also prototype an LLVM matcher that recognizes learned primitives in optimized IR, recovering 26 replacement sites across five PROJ projections and improving end-to-end application performance by up to 5%.

</details>


### [12] [Evaluating adaptive and generative AI-based feedback and recommendations in a knowledge-graph-integrated programming learning system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24940)
*Lalita Na Nongkhai,Jingyun Wang,Adam Wynn,Takahiko Mendori*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个结合大语言模型与检索增强生成的框架，集成到自适应学习系统中，用于评估代码、生成反馈和推荐练习。实验比较了三种教学模式（自适应、GenAI、混合模式）的学习效果，发现混合模式在所有关键指标上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索如何有效整合大语言模型与检索增强生成技术来提升编程学习支持系统的效果，特别是通过结合知识图谱和用户交互历史来增强反馈质量，并比较不同教学模式对学习者表现的影响。

Method: 方法包括：1）设计开发集成LLM与RAG的框架，结合知识图谱和用户交互历史；2）将该框架整合到现有的自适应学习支持系统中；3）进行实验研究，比较自适应、GenAI和混合GenAI-自适应三种教学模式；4）分析4956个代码提交中的四个关键日志特征；5）通过问卷调查收集学习者感知数据。

Result: 结果显示：1）接受GenAI模式反馈的学习者比自适应模式有显著更多的正确代码和更少的缺失关键编程逻辑的提交；2）混合GenAI-自适应模式在所有日志特征上表现最佳，获得最多的正确提交和最少的错误或不完整尝试；3）问卷反馈表明GenAI生成的反馈被广泛认为有帮助，所有模式在易用性和有用性方面都获得积极评价。

Conclusion: 结论表明混合GenAI-自适应模式在编程学习支持方面优于纯自适应和纯GenAI模式，结合了两种方法的优势，为学习者提供了更有效的反馈和学习体验。该框架展示了LLM与RAG技术在教育应用中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces the design and development of a framework that integrates a large language model (LLM) with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approach leveraging both a knowledge graph and user interaction history. The framework is incorporated into a previously developed adaptive learning support system to assess learners' code, generate formative feedback, and recommend exercises. Moerover, this study examines learner preferences across three instructional modes; adaptive, Generative AI (GenAI), and hybrid GenAI-adaptive. An experimental study was conducted to compare the learning performance and perception of the learners, and the effectiveness of these three modes using four key log features derived from 4956 code submissions across all experimental groups. The analysis results show that learners receiving feedback from GenAI modes had significantly more correct code and fewer code submissions missing essential programming logic than those receiving feedback from adaptive mode. In particular, the hybrid GenAI-adaptive mode achieved the highest number of correct submissions and the fewest incorrect or incomplete attempts, outperforming both the adaptive-only and GenAI-only modes. Questionnaire responses further indicated that GenAI-generated feedback was widely perceived as helpful, while all modes were rated positively for ease of use and usefulness. These results suggest that the hybrid GenAI-adaptive mode outperforms the other two modes across all measured log features.

</details>


### [13] [On Representability of Multiple-Valued Functions by Linear Lambda Terms Typed with Second-order Polymorphic Type System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25337)
*Satoshi Matsuoka*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 多值函数可通过二阶多态类型系统中的线性λ项表示，提供电路风格和归纳风格两种表示方法，并讨论了优化技术和应用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何将多值函数（multiple-valued function）在类型系统中进行形式化表示，探索不同类型的表示风格及其优化方法，为多值逻辑在编程语言和形式化验证中的应用提供理论基础。

Method: 使用二阶多态类型系统中的线性λ项来表示多值函数，提出了两种表示风格：1) 电路风格 - 模拟开关理论中的组合电路；2) 归纳风格 - 采用更传统的数学归纳方法。同时讨论了这些表示的优化技术。

Result: 证明了任何多值函数都可以通过二阶多态类型系统中的线性λ项表示，提供了两种不同风格的表示方法，并展示了优化技术。通过案例研究证明了该方法在多个领域具有潜在应用价值。

Conclusion: 线性λ项在二阶多态类型系统中为多值函数提供了有效的形式化表示框架，两种表示风格各有优势，优化技术可提高表示效率，该方法在多值逻辑和形式化方法领域具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We show that any multiple-valued function can be represented by a linear lambda term typed in a second-order polymorphic type system, using two distinct styles. The first is a circuit style, which mimics combinational circuits in switching theory. The second is an inductive style, which follows a more traditional mathematical approach. We also discuss several optimizations for these representations. Furthermore, we present a case study that demonstrates the potential applications of our approach across various domains.

</details>


### [14] [Decidable By Construction: Design-Time Verification for Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25414)
*Houston Haynes*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种在AI模型设计时而非训练后验证数值稳定性、计算正确性和物理一致性的框架，基于有限生成阿贝尔群约束，通过类型系统组合实现零开销可靠性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习假设模型正确性必须在训练后强制执行，但数值稳定性、计算正确性和物理一致性等属性实际上可以在设计时验证，这对于高杠杆决策支持和科学约束环境尤为重要。

Method: 构建基于有限生成阿贝尔群约束的框架，组合三个现有成果：1) 携带任意注释的维度类型系统；2) 从类型签名推断Clifford代数等级和几何乘积稀疏性的程序超图；3) 通过前向模式共效应分析和精确posit累加在训练中保持不变量的自适应域模型架构。

Result: 该框架在形式上将Hindley-Milner统一置于与通用归纳相同的理论基础，证明在阿贝尔群上的统一计算了Solomonoff通用先验的可计算限制下的最大后验假设，从而为类型推断提供了信息论基础。

Conclusion: 相比四种当代AI可靠性方法，该框架通过构造消除了跨部署、层和推理请求的复合开销，实现了在设计时以边际计算成本验证关键模型属性。

Abstract: A prevailing assumption in machine learning is that model correctness must be enforced after the fact. We observe that the properties determining whether an AI model is numerically stable, computationally correct, or consistent with a physical domain do not necessarily demand post hoc enforcement. They can be verified at design time, before training begins, at marginal computational cost, with particular relevance to models deployed in high-leverage decision support and scientifically constrained settings. These properties share a specific algebraic structure: they are expressible as constraints over finitely generated abelian groups $\mathbb{Z}^n$, where inference is decidable in polynomial time and the principal type is unique. A framework built on this observation composes three prior results (arXiv:2603.16437, arXiv:2603.17627, arXiv:2603.18104): a dimensional type system carrying arbitrary annotations as persistent codata through model elaboration; a program hypergraph that infers Clifford algebra grade and derives geometric product sparsity from type signatures alone; and an adaptive domain model architecture preserving both invariants through training via forward-mode coeffect analysis and exact posit accumulation. We believe this composition yields a novel information-theoretic result: Hindley-Milner unification over abelian groups computes the maximum a posteriori hypothesis under a computable restriction of Solomonoff's universal prior, placing the framework's type inference on the same formal ground as universal induction. We compare four contemporary approaches to AI reliability and show that each imposes overhead that can compound across deployments, layers, and inference requests. This framework eliminates that overhead by construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [15] [A Unified Memory Perspective for Probabilistic Trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25692)
*Xueji Zhao,Likai Pei,Jianbo Liu,Kai Ni,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将确定性数据访问视为随机采样的特例，提出统一数据访问视角，揭示随机性需求降低数据访问效率并可能导致熵受限操作，定义内存级评估标准，分析传统架构局限并探索概率存内计算方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可信人工智能日益依赖概率计算实现鲁棒性、可解释性、安全性和隐私性。实际系统中，这些工作负载在模型、数据路径和系统功能间交错进行确定性数据访问和重复随机采样，将性能瓶颈从算术单元转移到必须同时提供数据和随机性的内存系统。

Method: 提出统一数据访问视角，将确定性访问视为随机采样的极限情况，使两种模式能在共同框架内分析。基于此视角定义内存级评估标准：统一操作、分布可编程性、效率、对硬件非理想性的鲁棒性以及并行兼容性。使用这些标准分析传统架构局限，并研究新兴的概率存内计算方法。

Result: 统一视角揭示：增加随机性需求会降低有效数据访问效率，并可能驱动系统进入熵受限操作。传统架构在满足概率计算的内存需求方面存在局限，而集成采样与内存访问的概率存内计算方法为可扩展可信AI硬件提供了有前景的途径。

Conclusion: 通过统一数据访问视角分析概率计算的内存需求，提出了评估内存系统的关键标准，并指出概率存内计算是实现可扩展可信AI硬件的有前景方向。该框架为设计满足概率计算独特需求的内存系统提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Trustworthy artificial intelligence increasingly relies on probabilistic computation to achieve robustness, interpretability, security and privacy. In practical systems, such workloads interleave deterministic data access with repeated stochastic sampling across models, data paths and system functions, shifting performance bottlenecks from arithmetic units to memory systems that must deliver both data and randomness. Here we present a unified data-access perspective in which deterministic access is treated as a limiting case of stochastic sampling, enabling both modes to be analyzed within a common framework. This view reveals that increasing stochastic demand reduces effective data-access efficiency and can drive systems into entropy-limited operation. Based on this insight, we define memory-level evaluation criteria, including unified operation, distribution programmability, efficiency, robustness to hardware non-idealities and parallel compatibility. Using these criteria, we analyze limitations of conventional architectures and examine emerging probabilistic compute-in-memory approaches that integrate sampling with memory access, outlining pathways toward scalable hardware for trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [16] [Can an Actor-Critic Optimization Framework Improve Analog Design Optimization?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24714)
*Sounak Dutta,Fin Amin,Sushil Panda,Jonathan Rabe,Yuejiang Wen,Paul Franzon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于模拟电路尺寸优化的演员-评论家优化框架（ACOF），通过分离提议和评估角色来模拟设计师的决策过程，显著提升了优化效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟电路设计面临的主要挑战是：即使对器件尺寸或偏置进行微小调整也需要昂贵的仿真周期，高质量解决方案通常只占据巨大搜索空间中非常狭窄的部分。现有优化器虽然减轻了一些负担，但缺乏设计师在决定下一步搜索方向时所使用的那种判断力。

Method: 提出了演员-评论家优化框架（ACOF），将优化过程分解为两个角色：演员负责提议设计空间中有前景的区域，评论家则评估这些选择、强制执行设计合法性，并在进展受阻时重新定向搜索。这种结构保持了与标准基于仿真流程的兼容性，同时使搜索过程更加审慎、稳定和可解释。

Result: 在测试电路中，ACOF将前10个性能指标（FoM）平均提升了38.9%，优于最强的竞争基线；平均减少了24.7%的遗憾值；在单个电路上实现了70.5%的FoM峰值增益和42.2%的遗憾值降低。

Conclusion: 通过将迭代推理与仿真驱动搜索相结合，该框架为跨挑战性设计空间的自动化模拟电路尺寸优化提供了一条更加透明的路径，使搜索过程更加审慎、稳定和可解释。

Abstract: Analog design often slows down because even small changes to device sizes or biases require expensive simulation cycles, and high-quality solutions typically occupy only a narrow part of a very large search space. While existing optimizers reduce some of this burden, they largely operate without the kind of judgment designers use when deciding where to search next. This paper presents an actor-critic optimization framework (ACOF) for analog sizing that brings that form of guidance into the loop. Rather than treating optimization as a purely black-box search problem, ACOF separates the roles of proposal and evaluation: an actor suggests promising regions of the design space, while a critic reviews those choices, enforces design legality, and redirects the search when progress is hampered. This structure preserves compatibility with standard simulator-based flows while making the search process more deliberate, stable, and interpretable. Across our test circuits, ACOF improves the top-10 figure of merit by an average of 38.9% over the strongest competing baseline and reduces regret by an average of 24.7%, with peak gains of 70.5% in FoM and 42.2% lower regret on individual circuits. By combining iterative reasoning with simulation-driven search, the framework offers a more transparent path toward automated analog sizing across challenging design spaces.

</details>


### [17] [DyMRL: Dynamic Multispace Representation Learning for Multimodal Event Forecasting in Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24636)
*Feng Zhao,Kangzheng Liu,Teng Peng,Yu Yang,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DyMRL：一种动态多空间表示学习方法，用于高效获取和融合多模态时序知识，以提升事件预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注静态设置，忽视了多模态知识的动态获取与融合。具体问题包括：1）知识获取层面难以学习不同模态（特别是动态结构模态）的时间敏感信息；2）知识融合层面难以学习演化的多模态融合特征，静态共注意力机制无法捕捉不同模态对历史事件变化的贡献差异。

Method: DyMRL采用动态多空间表示学习方法：1）将欧几里得、双曲和复数空间的时间特定结构特征整合到关系消息传递框架中，学习深度表示，反映人类智能的联想思维、高阶抽象和逻辑推理；2）采用先进的双重融合-演化注意力机制，以对称方式在不同时间戳为不同模态动态分配学习权重。利用预训练模型赋予时间敏感的视觉和语言智能。

Result: 构建了四个多模态时序知识图谱基准数据集进行实验评估。大量实验表明，DyMRL在事件预测性能上优于最先进的动态单模态和静态多模态基线方法。

Conclusion: DyMRL通过动态获取和融合多模态时序知识，有效解决了现有方法在时间敏感信息学习和演化特征融合方面的局限性，显著提升了事件预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate representation of multimodal knowledge is crucial for event forecasting in real-world scenarios. However, existing studies have largely focused on static settings, overlooking the dynamic acquisition and fusion of multimodal knowledge. 1) At the knowledge acquisition level, how to learn time-sensitive information of different modalities, especially the dynamic structural modality. Existing dynamic learning methods are often limited to shallow structures across heterogeneous spaces or simple unispaces, making it difficult to capture deep relation-aware geometric features. 2) At the knowledge fusion level, how to learn evolving multimodal fusion features. Existing knowledge fusion methods based on static coattention struggle to capture the varying historical contributions of different modalities to future events. To this end, we propose DyMRL, a Dynamic Multispace Representation Learning approach to efficiently acquire and fuse multimodal temporal knowledge. 1) For the former issue, DyMRL integrates time-specific structural features from Euclidean, hyperbolic, and complex spaces into a relational message-passing framework to learn deep representations, reflecting human intelligences in associative thinking, high-order abstracting, and logical reasoning. Pretrained models endow DyMRL with time-sensitive visual and linguistic intelligences. 2) For the latter concern, DyMRL incorporates advanced dual fusion-evolution attention mechanisms that assign dynamic learning emphases equally to different modalities at different timestamps in a symmetric manner. To evaluate DyMRL's event forecasting performance through leveraging its learned multimodal temporal knowledge in history, we construct four multimodal temporal knowledge graph benchmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DyMRL outperforms state-of-the-art dynamic unimodal and static multimodal baseline methods.

</details>


### [18] [Experiential Reflective Learning for Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24639)
*Marc-Antoine Allard,Arnaud Teinturier,Victor Xing,Gautier Viaud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ERL框架通过经验反思学习生成可迁移的启发式规则，提升LLM智能体在专业环境中的适应能力和任务成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型智能体难以适应专业环境，且无法利用过往经验，每次任务都从零开始，缺乏从经验中学习的能力

Method: 提出经验反思学习框架：1) 反思任务轨迹和结果生成启发式规则；2) 测试时基于当前任务检索相关启发式规则；3) 将规则注入智能体上下文指导执行

Result: 在Gaia2基准测试中，ERL比ReAct基线成功率提升7.8%，任务完成可靠性显著提高，优于先前经验学习方法；选择性检索是关键，启发式规则比few-shot轨迹提示更具可迁移性

Conclusion: 通过单次尝试经验反思提取可迁移启发式规则，能够实现有效的智能体自我改进，为LLM智能体适应专业环境提供了实用框架

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled the development of autonomous agents capable of complex reasoning and multi-step problem solving. However, these agents struggle to adapt to specialized environments and do not leverage past interactions, approaching each new task from scratch regardless of their accumulated experience. We introduce Experiential Reflective Learning (ERL), a simple self-improvement framework that enables rapid environment adaptation through experiential learning. ERL reflects on task trajectories and outcomes to generate heuristics, capturing actionable lessons that transfer across tasks. At test time, relevant heuristics are retrieved based on the current task and injected into the agent's context to guide execution. On the Gaia2 benchmark, ERL improves success rate by 7.8% over a ReAct baseline, with large gains in task completion reliability, and outperforms prior experiential learning methods. Through systematic ablations, we find that selective retrieval is essential and that heuristics provide more transferable abstractions than few-shot trajectory prompting. These results demonstrate that reflecting on single-attempt experiences to extract transferable heuristics enables effective agent self-improvement.

</details>


### [19] [Amplified Patch-Level Differential Privacy for Free via Random Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24695)
*Kaan Durmaz,Jan Schuchardt,Sebastian Schmidt,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 随机裁剪作为数据增强技术，其内在随机性在差分隐私训练中未被充分研究，本文发现当敏感内容空间局部化时，随机裁剪能概率性地排除敏感内容，从而增强隐私保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉中常用的随机裁剪数据增强技术，其内在随机性在差分隐私机器学习训练中的作用尚未被探索。当图像中的敏感内容（如人脸、车牌）空间局部化时，随机裁剪能够概率性地将这些敏感内容排除在模型输入之外，这为差分隐私训练引入了额外的随机性来源。

Method: 引入视觉数据的补丁级邻接关系，为结合随机裁剪的差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）推导紧致的隐私边界。分析量化补丁包含概率，展示其如何与小批量采样组合产生更低的有效采样率。

Result: 理论分析表明补丁级隐私放大改善了隐私-效用权衡，实验验证了该方法在多个分割架构和数据集上的有效性，证明对齐隐私计算与领域结构及现有随机性源可以在不增加成本的情况下提供更强的隐私保证。

Conclusion: 随机裁剪的内在随机性为差分隐私训练提供了额外的隐私放大机制，通过将隐私计算与领域结构对齐，并利用现有的随机性源，可以在不改变模型架构或训练过程的情况下获得更强的隐私保证。

Abstract: Random cropping is one of the most common data augmentation techniques in computer vision, yet the role of its inherent randomness in training differentially private machine learning models has thus far gone unexplored. We observe that when sensitive content in an image is spatially localized, such as a face or license plate, random cropping can probabilistically exclude that content from the model's input. This introduces a third source of stochasticity in differentially private training with stochastic gradient descent, in addition to gradient noise and minibatch sampling. This additional randomness amplifies differential privacy without requiring changes to model architecture or training procedure. We formalize this effect by introducing a patch-level neighboring relation for vision data and deriving tight privacy bounds for differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) when combined with random cropping. Our analysis quantifies the patch inclusion probability and shows how it composes with minibatch sampling to yield a lower effective sampling rate. Empirically, we validate that patch-level amplification improves the privacy-utility trade-off across multiple segmentation architectures and datasets. Our results demonstrate that aligning privacy accounting with domain structure and additional existing sources of randomness can yield stronger guarantees at no additional cost.

</details>


### [20] [Training LLMs for Multi-Step Tool Orchestration with Constrained Data Synthesis and Graduated Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24709)
*Cheng Jiayang,Xin Liu,Zhihan Zhang,Haoyang Wen,Zixuan Zhang,Qingyu Yin,Shiyang Li,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Chao Zhang,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个解决多步工具编排挑战的强化学习框架，包含真实API响应缓存环境和分级奖励设计，显著提升复杂函数调用序列执行的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步工具编排（LLMs需要按正确顺序调用多个依赖API并传播中间输出）仍然具有挑战性。现有模型在完整序列执行上经常失败，参数值错误占失败的主要部分。训练模型处理此类工作流面临两个障碍：现有环境专注于简单的每轮函数调用和模拟数据，二元奖励无法为部分正确性提供信号。

Method: 1. 构建基于大规模真实API响应缓存的强化学习环境，支持数据合成管道，可采样具有可控复杂度的有效多步编排轨迹，生成效率显著高于无约束方法。2. 提出分级奖励设计，将正确性分解为原子有效性（在递增粒度上的单个函数调用正确性）和编排（尊重依赖关系的正确工具序列）。

Result: 在ComplexFuncBench上，该方法在轮次准确性方面表现出显著改进。消融研究证实两个奖励组件都是必需的：单独使用任一组件的性能都会显著下降。

Conclusion: 该框架通过真实API响应缓存环境和分级奖励设计，有效解决了多步工具编排的训练挑战，显著提升了LLMs在复杂函数调用序列执行中的性能。

Abstract: Multi-step tool orchestration, where LLMs must invoke multiple dependent APIs in the correct order while propagating intermediate outputs, remains challenging. State-of-the-art models frequently fail on full sequence execution, with parameter value errors accounting for a significant portion of failures. Training models to handle such workflows faces two obstacles: existing environments focus on simple per-turn function calls with simulated data, and binary rewards provide no signal for partial correctness.
  We present a framework addressing both challenges. First, we construct a reinforcement learning environment backed by a large-scale cache of real API responses, enabling a data synthesis pipeline that samples valid multi-step orchestration traces with controllable complexity and significantly higher generation efficiency than unconstrained methods. Second, we propose a graduated reward design that decomposes correctness into atomic validity (individual function call correctness at increasing granularity) and orchestration (correct tool sequencing with dependency respect). On ComplexFuncBench, our approach demonstrates substantial improvements in turn accuracy. Ablation studies confirm both reward components are essential: using either alone significantly degrades performance.

</details>


### [21] [Grokking as a Falsifiable Finite-Size Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24746)
*Yuda Bi,Chenyu Zhang,Qiheng Wang,Vince D Calhoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究为深度学习中的"顿悟"现象提供了可检验的相变理论框架，通过引入群阶p作为扩展变量和谱头尾对比作为序参量，证实了顿悟具有相变特征而非平滑交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顿悟现象常被类比为相变，但缺乏可检验的有限尺寸输入。研究旨在为顿悟的相变描述提供可证伪的定量框架，区分其是真正的相变还是平滑交叉。

Method: 将群阶p作为扩展变量，使用保留的谱头尾对比作为序参量。应用凝聚态物理诊断链：粗网格扫描和密集近临界加法审计，通过类Binder交叉揭示有限尺寸边界，比较敏感性。

Result: 类Binder交叉显示共享有限尺寸边界，敏感性比较强烈反对平滑交叉解释（ΔAIC=16.8）。顿悟的相变语言可作为定量有限尺寸主张进行检验，而非仅作为类比。

Conclusion: 顿悟现象确实表现出相变特征，可通过有限尺寸分析进行定量检验。虽然相变阶数尚未确定，但研究为深度学习中的相变理论提供了可证伪的框架。

Abstract: Grokking -- the delayed onset of generalization after early memorization -- is often described with phase-transition language, but that claim has lacked falsifiable finite-size inputs. Here we supply those inputs by treating the group order $p$ of $\mathbb{Z}_p$ as an admissible extensive variable and a held-out spectral head-tail contrast as a representation-level order parameter, then apply a condensed-matter-style diagnostic chain to coarse-grid sweeps and a dense near-critical addition audit. Binder-like crossings reveal a shared finite-size boundary, and susceptibility comparison strongly disfavors a smooth-crossover interpretation ($Δ\mathrm{AIC}=16.8$ in the near-critical audit). Phase-transition language in grokking can therefore be tested as a quantitative finite-size claim rather than invoked as analogy alone, although the transition order remains unresolved at present.

</details>


### [22] [Light Cones For Vision: Simple Causal Priors For Visual Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24753)
*Manglam Kartik,Neel Tushar Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Worldline Slot Attention模型将物体建模为时空世界线中的持久轨迹，使用洛伦兹几何结构显著提升层次物体发现性能，相比欧几里得几何实现6倍改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视觉模型将物体视为欧几里得空间中的独立点，无法捕捉层次结构（如部分与整体的关系）。需要一种能够编码层次结构的几何表示方法

Method: 提出Worldline Slot Attention，将物体建模为时空世界线中的持久轨迹，每个物体在不同层次级别有多个槽位，共享相同空间位置但具有不同时间坐标。比较了欧几里得和洛伦兹几何结构的效果

Result: 欧几里得世界线准确率仅0.078（低于随机机会0.33），而洛伦兹世界线在三个数据集上达到0.479-0.661准确率，实现6倍改进，在20+独立运行中可复现。洛伦兹几何也优于双曲嵌入

Conclusion: 层次物体发现需要编码非对称因果关系的几何结构，这是欧几里得空间缺乏但洛伦兹光锥自然具备的归纳偏置。仅用11K参数即可实现

Abstract: Standard vision models treat objects as independent points in Euclidean space, unable to capture hierarchical structure like parts within wholes. We introduce Worldline Slot Attention, which models objects as persistent trajectories through spacetime worldlines, where each object has multiple slots at different hierarchy levels sharing the same spatial position but differing in temporal coordinates. This architecture consistently fails without geometric structure: Euclidean worldlines achieve 0.078 level accuracy, below random chance (0.33), while Lorentzian worldlines achieve 0.479-0.661 across three datasets: a 6x improvement replicated over 20+ independent runs. Lorentzian geometry also outperforms hyperbolic embeddings showing visual hierarchies require causal structure (temporal dependency) rather than tree structure (radial branching). Our results demonstrate that hierarchical object discovery requires geometric structure encoding asymmetric causality, an inductive bias absent from Euclidean space but natural to Lorentzian light cones, achieved with only 11K parameters. The code is available at: https://github.com/iclrsubmissiongram/loco.

</details>


### [23] [Transformers in the Dark: Navigating Unknown Search Spaces via Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24780)
*Jungtaek Kim,Thomas Zeng,Ziqian Lin,Minjae Lee,Chungpa Lee,Jy-yong Sohn,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Transformer架构能否近似搜索算法，通过引入"未知树搜索与老虎机反馈"框架，证明Transformer理论上能够实现不同搜索策略，并能通过训练泛化到未见条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然将大语言模型与外部搜索算法结合能增强问题解决能力，但外部组件的需求使整体流程复杂化。因此研究LLM或其底层Transformer架构能否近似搜索算法，以简化问题解决过程。

Method: 引入"未知树搜索与老虎机反馈"简化框架，其中树扩展和反馈信号由外部指定。在此设置下，从零开始训练Transformer模型来近似不同搜索策略，并研究其泛化能力。还通过微调预训练LLM在搜索轨迹上，探索任务聚焦训练的效果。

Result: Transformer理论上足够表达以实现不同搜索策略，并能从零训练来近似这些策略。模型展现出泛化到更长视野或更深树等未见条件的能力。任务聚焦的持续训练能解锁预训练LLM的完整能力。

Conclusion: LLM和Transformer架构能够近似搜索算法，这为简化问题解决流程提供了可能，无需依赖外部搜索组件。任务聚焦训练是解锁预训练模型搜索能力的关键。

Abstract: Effective problem solving with Large Language Models (LLMs) can be enhanced when they are paired with external search algorithms. By viewing the space of diverse ideas and their follow-up possibilities as a tree structure, the search algorithm can navigate such a search space and guide the LLM toward better solutions more efficiently. While the search algorithm enables an effective balance between exploitation and exploration of a tree-structured space, the need for an external component can complicate the overall problem-solving process. We therefore pose the following question: Can LLMs or their underlying Transformer architectures approximate a search algorithm? To answer this question, we first introduce a simplified framework in which tree extensions and feedback signals are externally specified, allowing for controlled evaluation of search capabilities. We call this setting unknown tree search with bandit feedback. Within this setting, we show that Transformers are theoretically expressive enough to implement distinct search strategies and can be trained from scratch to approximate those strategies. Our Transformer models exhibit the possibility of generalizing to unseen conditions such as longer horizons or deeper trees. Furthermore, we demonstrate that continued task-focused training unlocks the complete capabilities of a pretrained LLM, by fine-tuning the LLM on search trajectories.

</details>


### [24] [Uncertainty-Guided Label Rebalancing for CPS Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25670)
*John Ayotunde,Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Lionel C. Briand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: U-Balance：一种利用行为不确定性来重新平衡CPS安全监测中极端类别不平衡数据集的监督学习方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS安全监测面临极端类别不平衡问题（安全事件罕见），传统重平衡技术在时间序列遥测数据上效果不佳，而CPS行为不确定性（决策中的怀疑程度）与安全结果相关但未被探索

Method: 1. 训练基于GatedMLP的不确定性预测器，将遥测窗口转换为分布运动学特征并输出不确定性分数；2. 应用不确定性引导的标签重平衡机制，将高不确定性的"安全"标签窗口概率性重新标记为"不安全"；3. 在重平衡数据集上训练安全预测器

Result: 在46:1安全-不安全比的UAV基准测试中，U-Balance达到0.806 F1分数，比最强基线提升14.3个百分点，同时保持有竞争力的推理效率；验证了行为不确定性与安全性的中度显著相关性

Conclusion: U-Balance通过利用行为不确定性进行数据重平衡，有效解决了CPS安全监测中的极端类别不平衡问题，uLNR机制是比直接融合更有效的利用不确定性信息策略

Abstract: Safety monitoring is essential for Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). However, unsafe events are rare in real-world CPS operations, creating an extreme class imbalance that degrades safety predictors. Standard rebalancing techniques perform poorly on time-series CPS telemetry, either generating unrealistic synthetic samples or overfitting on the minority class. Meanwhile, behavioral uncertainty in CPS operations, defined as the degree of doubt or uncertainty in CPS decisions , is often correlated with safety outcomes but unexplored in safety monitoring. To that end, we propose U-Balance, a supervised approach that leverages behavioral uncertainty to rebalance imbalanced datasets prior to training a safety predictor. U-Balance first trains a GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor that summarizes each telemetry window into distributional kinematic features and outputs an uncertainty score. It then applies an uncertainty-guided label rebalancing (uLNR) mechanism that probabilistically relabels \textit{safe}-labeled windows with unusually high uncertainty as \textit{unsafe}, thereby enriching the minority class with informative boundary samples without synthesizing new data. Finally, a safety predictor is trained on the rebalanced dataset for safety monitoring. We evaluate U-Balance on a large-scale UAV benchmark with a 46:1 safe-to-unsafe ratio. Results confirm a moderate but significant correlation between behavioral uncertainty and safety. We then identify uLNR as the most effective strategy to exploit uncertainty information, compared to direct early and late fusion. U-Balance achieves a 0.806 F1 score, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.3 percentage points, while maintaining competitive inference efficiency. Ablation studies confirm that both the GatedMLP-based uncertainty predictor and the uLNR mechanism contribute significantly to U-Balance's effectiveness.

</details>


### [25] [A Practical Guide Towards Interpreting Time-Series Deep Clinical Predictive Models: A Reproducibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24828)
*Yongda Fan,John Wu,Andrea Fitzpatrick,Naveen Baskaran,Jimeng Sun,Adam Cross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个评估临床预测任务中可解释性方法的综合基准，分析了注意力机制、黑盒解释器等在不同模型架构和临床任务中的表现，并提供了改进临床预测流程可解释性的指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策需要明确的解释依据，因此模型可解释性对于部署前的深度临床模型审计至关重要。随着模型架构和可解释性方法的扩展，需要回答关键问题：注意力等架构特征是否改善可解释性？可解释性方法是否能在不同临床任务中泛化？现有基准缺乏可扩展性、可重复性，且未能系统研究可解释性在临床任务与模型架构交互中的变化。

Method: 提出了一个全面的基准测试框架，评估多种可解释性方法在不同临床预测任务和模型架构中的表现。通过PyHealth开源框架实现，支持可重复性和可扩展性。具体分析了注意力机制、KernelSHAP、LIME等黑盒解释器以及其他可解释性方法在时间序列临床预测任务中的性能。

Result: 分析发现：(1) 正确利用注意力机制是忠实解释模型预测的高效方法；(2) KernelSHAP和LIME等黑盒解释器在时间序列临床预测任务中计算不可行；(3) 多种可解释性方法可靠性不足，难以信任。基于这些发现，提出了改进临床预测流程可解释性的具体指南。

Conclusion: 该研究为临床预测模型的可解释性评估提供了系统基准，揭示了不同可解释性方法在临床任务中的实际表现和局限性。通过PyHealth开源框架的发布，支持了研究的可重复性和社区扩展。研究结果为改进临床预测流程的可解释性提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Clinical decisions are high-stakes and require explicit justification, making model interpretability essential for auditing deep clinical models prior to deployment. As the ecosystem of model architectures and explainability methods expands, critical questions remain: Do architectural features like attention improve explainability? Do interpretability approaches generalize across clinical tasks? While prior benchmarking efforts exist, they often lack extensibility and reproducibility, and critically, fail to systematically examine how interpretability varies across the interplay of clinical tasks and model architectures. To address these gaps, we present a comprehensive benchmark evaluating interpretability methods across diverse clinical prediction tasks and model architectures. Our analysis reveals that: (1) attention when leveraged properly is a highly efficient approach for faithfully interpreting model predictions; (2) black-box interpreters like KernelSHAP and LIME are computationally infeasible for time-series clinical prediction tasks; and (3) several interpretability approaches are too unreliable to be trustworthy. From our findings, we discuss several guidelines on improving interpretability within clinical predictive pipelines. To support reproducibility and extensibility, we provide our implementations via PyHealth, a well-documented open-source framework: https://github.com/sunlabuiuc/PyHealth.

</details>


### [26] [Flow matching on homogeneous spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24829)
*Francesco Ruscelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个将流匹配扩展到齐次空间（李群商空间）的通用框架，通过将数据分布提升到李群上简化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配方法在齐次空间上实现复杂，需要处理复杂的几何结构、定义预度量或测地线计算，限制了应用效率和简洁性

Method: 通过将齐次空间上的数据分布提升到其底层李群上，将问题转化为李群上的流匹配任务，再进一步简化为李代数上的欧几里得流匹配，避免直接处理齐次空间的复杂几何

Result: 相比黎曼流匹配，该方法消除了定义和计算预度量或测地线的需求，实现了更简单、更快、完全内蕴的框架

Conclusion: 提出的框架为齐次空间上的流匹配提供了一种简洁高效的解决方案，通过李群和李代数的转换避免了复杂的几何处理，具有理论和实践优势

Abstract: We propose a general framework to extend Flow Matching to homogeneous spaces, i.e. quotients of Lie groups. Our approach reformulates the problem as a flow matching task on the underlying Lie group by lifting the data distributions. This strategy avoids the potentially complicated geometry of homogeneous spaces by working directly on Lie groups, which in turn enables us reduce the problem to a Euclidean flow matching task on Lie algebras. In contrast to Riemannian Flow Matching, our method eliminates the need to define and compute premetrics or geodesics, resulting in a simpler, faster, and fully intrinsic framework.

</details>


### [27] [Reaching Beyond the Mode: RL for Distributional Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24844)
*Isha Puri,Mehul Damani,Idan Shenfeld,Marzyeh Ghassemi,Jacob Andreas,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多答案强化学习方法，用于训练语言模型在推理时对多个答案进行分布推理，避免将答案分布坍缩到单一主导模式，从而更有效地处理存在多个有效答案或不确定性的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常将答案分布坍缩到单一模式，这对于基准评估（假设只有一个正确答案）可能适用，但许多现实任务（如医疗诊断、模糊问答、信息不完整场景）存在多个有效答案或不可约的不确定性。需要模型能生成多个合理假设并给出置信度估计，而无需计算密集的重复采样。

Method: 采用多答案强化学习方法，修改RL目标使模型能在单次前向传播中显式生成多个候选答案，将推理时搜索的某些方面内化到模型的生成过程中。

Result: 在问答、医疗诊断和编程基准测试中，相比单答案训练的基线模型，观察到改进的多样性、覆盖率和集合级校准分数。使用该方法训练的模型生成多个答案所需的token更少，在编程任务中准确性也显著更高。

Conclusion: 多答案RL为推理时扩展程序（如best-of-k）提供了一种原则性且计算高效的替代方案，使语言模型能更好地处理存在多个有效答案或不确定性的现实任务。

Abstract: Given a question, a language model (LM) implicitly encodes a distribution over possible answers. In practice, post-training procedures for LMs often collapse this distribution onto a single dominant mode. While this is generally not a problem for benchmark-style evaluations that assume one correct answer, many real-world tasks inherently involve multiple valid answers or irreducible uncertainty. Examples include medical diagnosis, ambiguous question answering, and settings with incomplete information. In these cases, we would like LMs to generate multiple plausible hypotheses, ideally with confidence estimates for each one, and without computationally intensive repeated sampling to generate non-modal answers. This paper describes a multi-answer reinforcement learning approach for training LMs to perform distributional reasoning over multiple answers during inference. We modify the RL objective to enable models to explicitly generate multiple candidate answers in a single forward pass, internalizing aspects of inference-time search into the model's generative process. Across question-answering, medical diagnostic, and coding benchmarks, we observe improved diversity, coverage, and set-level calibration scores compared to single answer trained baselines. Models trained with our approach require fewer tokens to generate multiple answers than competing approaches. On coding tasks, they are also substantially more accurate. These results position multi-answer RL as a principled and compute-efficient alternative to inference-time scaling procedures such as best-of-k. Code and more information can be found at https://multi-answer-rl.github.io/.

</details>


### [28] [Learning to Staff: Offline Reinforcement Learning and Fine-Tuned LLMs for Warehouse Staffing Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24883)
*Kalle Kujanpää,Yuying Zhu,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了两种机器学习方法优化半自动化仓库分拣系统的实时人员配置决策：基于Transformer的离线强化学习方法和基于LLM的抽象状态描述方法，均能提升吞吐量或匹配历史基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是优化半自动化仓库分拣系统的实时人员配置决策，支持不同抽象层次的运营决策，权衡不同方法的优劣，探索AI辅助运营决策的可行路径。

Method: 方法一：使用详细历史状态表示训练自定义Transformer策略，采用离线强化学习在匹配的模拟环境中优化。方法二：探索LLM在抽象、人类可读状态描述上的应用，系统比较提示技术、自动提示优化和微调策略，特别是监督微调结合模拟器生成偏好的直接偏好优化。

Result: 离线强化学习方法在学习的模拟器中实现了2.4%的吞吐量提升，这在大型仓库运营中意味着显著节省。LLM方法中，仅提示不足，但监督微调结合直接偏好优化在手工制作的模拟器中达到或略超历史基线性能。

Conclusion: 两种方法都为AI辅助运营决策提供了可行路径：离线强化学习在任务特定架构上表现优异，LLM支持人类可读输入并能结合迭代反馈循环纳入管理者偏好。

Abstract: We investigate machine learning approaches for optimizing real-time staffing decisions in semi-automated warehouse sortation systems. Operational decision-making can be supported at different levels of abstraction, with different trade-offs. We evaluate two approaches, each in a matching simulation environment. First, we train custom Transformer-based policies using offline reinforcement learning on detailed historical state representations, achieving a 2.4% throughput improvement over historical baselines in learned simulators. In high-volume warehouse operations, improvements of this size translate to significant savings. Second, we explore LLMs operating on abstracted, human-readable state descriptions. These are a natural fit for decisions that warehouse managers make using high-level operational summaries. We systematically compare prompting techniques, automatic prompt optimization, and fine-tuning strategies. While prompting alone proves insufficient, supervised fine-tuning combined with Direct Preference Optimization on simulator-generated preferences achieves performance that matches or slightly exceeds historical baselines in a hand-crafted simulator. Our findings demonstrate that both approaches offer viable paths toward AI-assisted operational decision-making. Offline RL excels with task-specific architectures. LLMs support human-readable inputs and can be combined with an iterative feedback loop that can incorporate manager preferences.

</details>


### [29] [CVA: Context-aware Video-text Alignment for Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24934)
*Sungho Moon,Seunghun Lee,Jiwan Seo,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CVA框架通过查询感知上下文多样化、上下文不变边界判别损失和上下文增强Transformer编码器，解决了视频时序定位中背景上下文干扰问题，在多个基准测试中实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决视频时序定位中的关键挑战：实现时间敏感的视文对齐，同时保持对无关背景上下文的鲁棒性。传统方法在查询无关的混合中容易产生"假阴性"问题，需要更有效的上下文处理方法。

Method: 1. 查询感知上下文多样化(QCD)：基于视频-文本相似性构建替换片段池，仅混合语义无关内容，防止查询无关混合导致的假阴性；2. 上下文不变边界判别(CBD)损失：对比损失强制在挑战性时间边界处保持语义一致性，使表示对上下文变化和困难负样本鲁棒；3. 上下文增强Transformer编码器(CTE)：分层架构结合窗口自注意力、双向交叉注意力和可学习查询，捕获多尺度时间上下文。

Result: 在QVHighlights和Charades-STA等主要VTG基准测试中达到最先进性能。Recall@1(R1)分数相比SOTA方法提升约5个点，显著缓解了假阴性问题。

Conclusion: CVA框架通过数据中心和架构增强的协同作用，有效解决了视频时序定位中的上下文干扰问题。查询感知上下文多样化、上下文不变边界判别损失和上下文增强Transformer编码器的组合提供了鲁棒且时间敏感的视文对齐方案。

Abstract: We propose Context-aware Video-text Alignment (CVA), a novel framework to address a significant challenge in video temporal grounding: achieving temporally sensitive video-text alignment that remains robust to irrelevant background context. Our framework is built on three key components. First, we propose Query-aware Context Diversification (QCD), a new data augmentation strategy that ensures only semantically unrelated content is mixed in. It builds a video-text similarity-based pool of replacement clips to simulate diverse contexts while preventing the ``false negative" caused by query-agnostic mixing. Second, we introduce the Context-invariant Boundary Discrimination (CBD) loss, a contrastive loss that enforces semantic consistency at challenging temporal boundaries, making their representations robust to contextual shifts and hard negatives. Third, we introduce the Context-enhanced Transformer Encoder (CTE), a hierarchical architecture that combines windowed self-attention and bidirectional cross-attention with learnable queries to capture multi-scale temporal context. Through the synergy of these data-centric and architectural enhancements, CVA achieves state-of-the-art performance on major VTG benchmarks, including QVHighlights and Charades-STA. Notably, our method achieves a significant improvement of approximately 5 points in Recall@1 (R1) scores over state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its effectiveness in mitigating false negatives.

</details>


### [30] [Optimal High-Probability Regret for Online Convex Optimization with Two-Point Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25029)
*Haishan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了在线凸优化中两点反馈的强凸损失高概率遗憾界问题，首次实现了O(d(logT+log(1/δ))/μ)的最优界


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线凸优化中，两点反馈下的强凸损失高概率遗憾界问题一直未解决，主要挑战在于带反馈梯度估计器的重尾特性使得标准集中分析困难

Method: 通过新的技术方法解决了带反馈梯度估计器的重尾特性问题，实现了高概率遗憾界的分析

Result: 首次获得了μ-强凸损失的O(d(logT+log(1/δ))/μ)高概率遗憾界，该结果在时间范围T和维度d上都是极小极大最优的

Conclusion: 本文完全解决了在线凸优化中两点反馈下强凸损失的高概率遗憾界问题，填补了该领域的空白

Abstract: We consider the problem of Online Convex Optimization (OCO) with two-point bandit feedback in an adversarial environment.
  In this setting, a player attempts to minimize a sequence of adversarially generated convex loss functions, while only observing the value of each function at two points.
  While it is well-known that two-point feedback allows for gradient estimation, achieving tight high-probability regret bounds for strongly convex functions still remained open as highlighted by \citet{agarwal2010optimal}. The primary challenge lies in the heavy-tailed nature of bandit gradient estimators, which makes standard concentration analysis difficult.
  In this paper, we resolve this open challenge by providing the first high-probability regret bound of $O(d(\log T + \log(1/δ))/μ)$ for $μ$-strongly convex losses. Our result is minimax optimal with respect to both the time horizon $T$ and the dimension $d$.

</details>


### [31] [Epistemic Compression: The Case for Deliberate Ignorance in High-Stakes AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25033)
*Steffen Lukas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"认知压缩"原则：在高风险领域，模型鲁棒性源于模型复杂度与数据有效期的匹配，而非参数规模扩展。通过"制度指数"区分稳定与不稳定制度，指导模型架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在稳定环境中表现优异，但在医学、金融、政策等高风险领域存在"保真度悖论"：模型容量增加反而放大噪声而非捕捉信号。这不仅是数据问题，更是结构性问题。

Method: 提出"认知压缩"原则，通过架构设计而非后验权重惩罚来强制简约性。引入"制度指数"区分"转移制度"（不稳定、数据贫乏）和"稳定制度"（不变、数据丰富），指导模型复杂度选择。

Result: 在15个高风险领域的探索性综合研究中，制度指数与经验上优越的建模策略在86.7%的案例中一致（13/15）。

Conclusion: 高风险AI需要从单纯扩展规模转向原则性简约设计。模型鲁棒性源于模型复杂度与数据有效期的匹配，而非参数规模扩展。

Abstract: Foundation models excel in stable environments, yet often fail where reliability matters most: medicine, finance, and policy. This Fidelity Paradox is not just a data problem; it is structural. In domains where rules change over time, extra model capacity amplifies noise rather than capturing signal. We introduce Epistemic Compression: the principle that robustness emerges from matching model complexity to the shelf life of the data, not from scaling parameters. Unlike classical regularization, which penalizes weights post hoc, Epistemic Compression enforces parsimony through architecture: the model structure itself is designed to reduce overfitting by making it architecturally costly to represent variance that exceeds the evidence in the data. We operationalize this with a Regime Index that separates Shifting Regime (unstable, data-poor; simplicity wins) from Stable Regime (invariant, data-rich; complexity viable). In an exploratory synthesis of 15 high-stakes domains, this index was concordant with the empirically superior modeling strategy in 86.7% of cases (13/15). High-stakes AI demands a shift from scaling for its own sake to principled parsimony.

</details>
