Abstract: We introduce Intern-S1-Pro, the first one-trillion-parameter scientific multimodal foundation model. Scaling to this unprecedented size, the model delivers a comprehensive enhancement across both general and scientific domains. Beyond stronger reasoning and image-text understanding capabilities, its intelligence is augmented with advanced agent capabilities. Simultaneously, its scientific expertise has been vastly expanded to master over 100 specialized tasks across critical science fields, including chemistry, materials, life sciences, and earth sciences. Achieving this massive scale is made possible by the robust infrastructure support of XTuner and LMDeploy, which facilitates highly efficient Reinforcement Learning (RL) training at the 1-trillion parameter level while ensuring strict precision consistency between training and inference. By seamlessly integrating these advancements, Intern-S1-Pro further fortifies the fusion of general and specialized intelligence, working as a Specializable Generalist, demonstrating its position in the top tier of open-source models for general capabilities, while outperforming proprietary models in the depth of specialized scientific tasks.

</details>


### [33] [The Order Is The Message](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25047)
*Jordan LeDoux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过控制实验发现，在模算术任务中，仅改变训练样本顺序（而非内容）就能显著影响模型学习效果，某些固定顺序策略能在远低于IID顺序样本复杂度下实现高精度，而对抗性顺序则完全抑制学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究训练数据顺序对模型学习的影响，特别是在模算术任务中，探索是否仅通过改变样本顺序（而非内容）就能显著影响模型的学习效率和泛化能力，以及这种现象对机器学习理论和安全的影响

Method: 在模算术任务（p=9973）上进行控制实验，仅改变训练样本顺序而保持所有其他因素不变。比较三种顺序策略：两种固定顺序策略、IID随机顺序基线、以及对抗性结构化顺序。训练集仅占输入空间的0.3%，远低于IID顺序下的样本复杂度下界

Result: 两种固定顺序策略分别在487和659个epoch后达到99.5%的测试准确率，而IID基线在5000个epoch后仅达到0.30%。对抗性顺序完全抑制学习。泛化模型可靠地构建了傅里叶表示，其基频与顺序结构的傅里叶对偶对应，编码了单个训练样本中不存在的信息，且该基频在所有随机种子、初始化和训练集组成中一致出现

Conclusion: 训练样本顺序对模型学习具有决定性影响，能够绕过传统样本复杂度限制。这种现象对训练效率、grokking现象的解释以及机器学习安全具有重要意义，特别是这种通过顺序而非内容传递信息的通道能够逃避所有内容层面的审计

Abstract: In a controlled experiment on modular arithmetic ($p = 9973$), varying only example ordering while holding all else constant, two fixed-ordering strategies achieve 99.5\% test accuracy by epochs 487 and 659 respectively from a training set comprising 0.3\% of the input space, well below established sample complexity lower bounds for this task under IID ordering. The IID baseline achieves 0.30\% after 5{,}000 epochs from identical data. An adversarially structured ordering suppresses learning entirely. The generalizing model reliably constructs a Fourier representation whose fundamental frequency is the Fourier dual of the ordering structure, encoding information present in no individual training example, with the same fundamental emerging across all seeds tested regardless of initialization or training set composition. We discuss implications for training efficiency, the reinterpretation of grokking, and the safety risks of a channel that evades all content-level auditing.

</details>


### [34] [SIGMA: Structure-Invariant Generative Molecular Alignment for Chemical Language Models via Autoregressive Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25062)
*Xinyu Wang,Fei Dou,Jinbo Bi,Minghu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGMA通过token级对比学习对齐线性化分子序列中的结构等价表示，结合IsoBeam搜索消除推理中的同构冗余，解决分子图生成中的轨迹发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性化字符串表示作为可扩展自回归分子生成的基础，但存在模态不匹配问题：单个分子图对应多个不同序列。这种歧义导致轨迹发散——结构相同的部分图因其线性化历史不同而在潜在表示中漂移。

Method: 提出SIGMA方法，不改变线性表示，而是通过token级对比目标使模型严格识别几何对称性，显式对齐共享相同后缀的前缀的潜在状态。同时引入IsoBeam搜索，在推理过程中通过动态剪枝等价路径消除同构冗余。

Result: 在标准基准测试上的实证评估表明，SIGMA在序列可扩展性和图保真度之间架起桥梁，相比强基线在多参数优化中实现了更优的样本效率和结构多样性。

Conclusion: SIGMA解决了线性化分子生成中的轨迹发散问题，通过对比学习对齐结构等价表示，结合高效推理策略，实现了可扩展且结构保真的分子生成。

Abstract: Linearized string representations serve as the foundation of scalable autoregressive molecular generation; however, they introduce a fundamental modality mismatch where a single molecular graph maps to multiple distinct sequences. This ambiguity leads to \textit{trajectory divergence}, where the latent representations of structurally equivalent partial graphs drift apart due to differences in linearization history. To resolve this without abandoning the efficient string formulation, we propose Structure-Invariant Generative Molecular Alignment (SIGMA). Rather than altering the linear representation, SIGMA enables the model to strictly recognize geometric symmetries via a token-level contrastive objective, which explicitly aligns the latent states of prefixes that share identical suffixes. Furthermore, we introduce Isomorphic Beam Search (IsoBeam) to eliminate isomorphic redundancy during inference by dynamically pruning equivalent paths. Empirical evaluations on standard benchmarks demonstrate that SIGMA bridges the gap between sequence scalability and graph fidelity, yielding superior sample efficiency and structural diversity in multi-parameter optimization compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [35] [Process-Aware AI for Rainfall-Runoff Modeling: A Mass-Conserving Neural Framework with Hydrological Process Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25093)
*Mohammad A. Farmani,Hoshin V. Gupta,Ali Behrangi,Muhammad Jawad,Sadaf Moghisi,Guo-Yue Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究通过逐步嵌入水文过程物理表示，评估了质量守恒感知机（MCP）在降雨径流建模中的预测性能和可解释性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在水文应用中虽然能实现高预测精度，但通常缺乏物理可解释性。质量守恒感知机（MCP）提供了一个物理感知的AI框架，能够在强制守恒原理的同时从数据中学习水文过程关系。

Method: 从最小MCP公式开始，逐步引入有界土壤储水、状态相关导水率、可变孔隙度、入渗能力、地表积水、垂直排水和非线性水位动态等物理过程表示。在覆盖美国大陆五个水文气候区域的15个流域中，以日径流预测为目标，评估了这一系列过程感知MCP模型的层次结构。

Result: 逐步增强MCP单元内部物理结构通常能改善预测性能。这些过程表示的影响具有强烈的水文气候依赖性：垂直排水显著提高了干旱和雪主导流域的模型技能，但在降雨主导区域降低了性能；地表积水的影响相对较小。性能最佳的MCP配置在保持明确物理可解释性的同时，接近了长短期记忆基准模型的预测技能。

Conclusion: 将水文过程约束嵌入AI架构中，为实现可解释和过程感知的降雨径流建模提供了一条有前景的途径。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve high predictive accuracy in hydrological applications but often lack physical interpretability. The Mass-Conserving Perceptron (MCP) provides a physics-aware artificial intelligence (AI) framework that enforces conservation principles while allowing hydrological process relationships to be learned from data. In this study, we investigate how progressively embedding physically meaningful representations of hydrological processes within a single MCP storage unit improves predictive skill and interpretability in rainfall-runoff modeling. Starting from a minimal MCP formulation, we sequentially introduce bounded soil storage, state-dependent conductivity, variable porosity, infiltration capacity, surface ponding, vertical drainage, and nonlinear water-table dynamics. The resulting hierarchy of process-aware MCP models is evaluated across 15 catchments spanning five hydroclimatic regions of the continental United States using daily streamflow prediction as the target. Results show that progressively augmenting the internal physical structure of the MCP unit generally improves predictive performance. The influence of these process representations is strongly hydroclimate dependent: vertical drainage substantially improves model skill in arid and snow-dominated basins but reduces performance in rainfall-dominated regions, while surface ponding has comparatively small effects. The best-performing MCP configurations approach the predictive skill of a Long Short-Term Memory benchmark while maintaining explicit physical interpretability. These results demonstrate that embedding hydrological process constraints within AI architectures provides a promising pathway toward interpretable and process-aware rainfall-runoff modeling.

</details>


### [36] [Gap Safe Screening Rules for Fast Training of Robust Support Vector Machines under Feature Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25221)
*Tan-Hau Nguyen,Thu-Le Tran,Kien Trung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为鲁棒支持向量机(R-SVMs)开发安全样本筛选规则，通过识别不确定性集完全位于间隔超平面任一侧的训练样本，在不影响最优解的情况下降低训练复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒支持向量机通过最坏情况鲁棒公式处理特征噪声，提高了可靠性但增加了计算成本。需要在不影响最优解的情况下降低训练复杂度

Method: 基于拉格朗日对偶性而非常用的Fenchel-Rockafellar对偶性，为R-SVMs开发安全样本筛选规则。首先建立理想筛选规则，然后通过将基于GAP的安全区域适应到鲁棒设置中，推导出实用规则

Result: 实验表明，所提出的方法显著减少了训练时间，同时保持了分类准确性。这是首次将安全筛选技术应用于监督机器学习中的最坏情况鲁棒模型

Conclusion: 成功开发了R-SVMs的安全样本筛选规则，有效降低了鲁棒模型的训练复杂度，为鲁棒机器学习模型的高效优化提供了新方法

Abstract: Robust Support Vector Machines (R-SVMs) address feature noise by adopting a worst-case robust formulation that explicitly incorporates uncertainty sets into training. While this robustness improves reliability, it also leads to increased computational cost. In this work, we develop safe sample screening rules for R-SVMs that reduce the training complexity without affecting the optimal solution. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to apply safe screening techniques to worst-case robust models in supervised machine learning. Our approach safely identifies training samples whose uncertainty sets are guaranteed to lie entirely on either side of the margin hyperplane, thereby reducing the problem size and accelerating optimization. Owing to the nonstandard structure of R-SVMs, the proposed screening rules are derived from the Lagrangian duality rather than the Fenchel-Rockafellar duality commonly used in recent methods. Based on this analysis, we first establish an ideal screening rule, and then derive a practical rule by adapting GAP-based safe regions to the robust setting. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method significantly reduces training time while preserving classification accuracy.

</details>


### [37] [Offline Decision Transformers for Neural Combinatorial Optimization: Surpassing Heuristics on the Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25241)
*Hironori Ohigashi,Shinichiro Hamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将离线强化学习框架Decision Transformer应用于组合优化问题，通过学习启发式解数据集来超越原始启发式算法性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化方法依赖在线强化学习，部署困难且未能充分利用数十年积累的算法知识，需要更有效的学习框架

Method: 应用离线RL框架Decision Transformer，集成Pointer Network处理节点选择的实例相关可变动作空间，采用期望分位数回归进行乐观条件化Return-to-Go

Result: 该方法在实验中持续产生比训练所用的四种经典启发式算法更高质量的旅行商问题路径

Conclusion: 离线强化学习有潜力解锁并超越现有领域知识中嵌入的性能，为组合优化提供新的有效方法

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization problems like the Traveling Salesman Problem are critical in industry yet NP-hard. Neural Combinatorial Optimization has shown promise, but its reliance on online reinforcement learning (RL) hampers deployment and underutilizes decades of algorithmic knowledge. We address these limitations by applying the offline RL framework, Decision Transformer, to learn superior strategies directly from datasets of heuristic solutions; it aims to not only to imitate but to synthesize and outperform them. Concretely, we (i) integrate a Pointer Network to handle the instance-dependent, variable action space of node selection, and (ii) employ expectile regression for optimistic conditioning of Return-to-Go, which is crucial for instances with widely varying optimal values. Experiments show that our method consistently produces higher-quality tours than the four classical heuristics it is trained on, demonstrating the potential of offline RL to unlock and exceed the performance embedded in existing domain knowledge.

</details>


### [38] [How Pruning Reshapes Features: Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Weight-Pruned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25325)
*Hector Borobia,Elies Seguí-Mas,Guillermina Tormo-Carbó*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统分析了非结构化剪枝对语言模型内部表示几何结构的影响，发现剪枝优先破坏高频通用特征而保留低频专用特征，Wanda剪枝比幅度剪枝能更好地保持特征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管权重剪枝是压缩大语言模型的标准技术，但其对学习到的内部表示的影响仍然缺乏系统研究。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究剪枝如何重塑语言模型的特征几何结构。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAEs）作为可解释性探针，在三个模型家族（Gemma 3 1B、Gemma 2 2B、Llama 3.2 1B）、两种剪枝方法（幅度剪枝和Wanda剪枝）和六个稀疏度级别（0-60%）下，研究五个研究问题：种子稳定性、特征存活率、SAE可迁移性、特征脆弱性和因果相关性。

Result: 最显著的发现是低频SAE特征（激活率低的特征）在剪枝后的存活率远高于高频特征，在17个实验条件中有11个显示出rho = -1.0的Spearman相关性。这表明剪枝起到了隐式特征选择的作用，优先破坏高频通用特征而保留专用低频特征。此外，Wanda剪枝比幅度剪枝能更好地保持特征结构（最多3.7倍），预训练的SAEs在Wanda剪枝模型上可保持有效性至50%稀疏度，几何特征存活率不能预测因果重要性。

Conclusion: 剪枝对语言模型内部表示具有非均匀影响，优先保留低频专用特征而非高频通用特征。这一发现对压缩下的可解释性具有重要意义，表明特征几何存活率与因果重要性之间存在解耦关系，Wanda剪枝在保持特征结构方面优于传统幅度剪枝。

Abstract: Weight pruning is a standard technique for compressing large language models, yet its effect on learned internal representations remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic study of how unstructured pruning reshapes the feature geometry of language models, using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as interpretability probes. Across three model families (Gemma 3 1B, Gemma 2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B), two pruning methods (magnitude and Wanda), and six sparsity levels (0--60%), we investigate five research questions spanning seed stability, feature survival, SAE transferability, feature fragility, and causal relevance. Our most striking finding is that rare SAE features--those with low firing rates--survive pruning far better than frequent ones, with within-condition Spearman correlations of rho = -1.0 in 11 of 17 experimental conditions. This counter-intuitive result suggests that pruning acts as implicit feature selection, preferentially destroying high-frequency generic features while preserving specialized rare ones. We further show that Wanda pruning preserves feature structure up to 3.7x better than magnitude pruning, that pre-trained SAEs remain viable on Wanda-pruned models up to 50% sparsity, and that geometric feature survival does not predict causal importance--a dissociation with implications for interpretability under compression.

</details>


### [39] [From Intent to Evidence: A Categorical Approach for Structural Evaluation of Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25342)
*Shuoling Liu,Zhiquan Tan,Kun Yi,Hui Wu,Yihan Li,Jiangpeng Yan,Liyuan Chen,Kai Chen,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于范畴论的形式化框架来评估深度研究智能体，通过四个可解释维度构建包含296个问题的机制感知基准测试，揭示了当前AI在复杂结构信息处理上的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度研究智能体的评估主要依赖临时性经验基准，这些启发式方法无法严格建模智能体行为，也不能充分压力测试长时程信息合成和歧义消解能力。需要建立形式化的理论框架来系统评估智能体的结构理解能力。

Method: 1. 使用范畴论形式化深度研究智能体行为，将研究流程建模为结构保持映射（函子）的组合；2. 基于该理论框架构建包含296个问题的机制感知基准测试，从四个可解释维度设计问题：遍历顺序连通链、验证V-结构拉回中的交集、对检索子结构施加拓扑排序、通过Yoneda探测进行本体证伪；3. 对11个领先模型进行严格评估。

Result: 1. 所有模型表现均处于较低基线，最先进模型平均准确率仅为19.9%，表明形式化结构压力测试具有极高难度；2. 发现当前AI能力存在明显二分现象：高级研究流程能成功重新定义动态拓扑排序并展现稳健的本体验证能力（在证伪幻觉前提方面与纯推理模型相当），但在多跳结构合成方面几乎普遍崩溃；3. 跨任务的巨大性能差异暴露了对脆弱启发式的依赖，而非系统性理解。

Conclusion: 虽然顶级自主智能体现在能够有机统一搜索和推理，但要实现对复杂结构信息的广义掌握仍然是一个严峻的开放挑战。该工作为深度研究智能体的评估提供了形式化理论框架和机制感知基准，揭示了当前AI在结构理解方面的系统性缺陷。

Abstract: Although deep research agents (DRAs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex information synthesis, their evaluation remains constrained by ad hoc empirical benchmarks. These heuristic approaches do not rigorously model agent behavior or adequately stress-test long-horizon synthesis and ambiguity resolution. To bridge this gap, we formalize DRA behavior through the lens of category theory, modeling deep research workflow as a composition of structure-preserving maps (functors). Grounded in this theoretical framework, we introduce a novel mechanism-aware benchmark with 296 questions designed to stress-test agents along four interpretable axes: traversing sequential connectivity chains, verifying intersections within V-structure pullbacks, imposing topological ordering on retrieved substructures, and performing ontological falsification via the Yoneda Probe. Our rigorous evaluation of 11 leading models establishes a persistently low baseline, with the state-of-the-art achieving only a 19.9\% average accuracy, exposing the difficulty of formal structural stress-testing. Furthermore, our findings reveal a stark dichotomy in the current AI capabilities. While advanced deep research pipelines successfully redefine dynamic topological re-ordering and exhibit robust ontological verification -- matching pure reasoning models in falsifying hallucinated premises -- they almost universally collapse on multi-hop structural synthesis. Crucially, massive performance variance across tasks exposes a lingering reliance on brittle heuristics rather than a systemic understanding. Ultimately, this work demonstrates that while top-tier autonomous agents can now organically unify search and reasoning, achieving a generalized mastery over complex structural information remains a formidable open challenge.\footnote{Our implementation will be available at https://github.com/tzq1999/CDR.

</details>


### [40] [Hessian-informed machine learning interatomic potential towards bridging theory and experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25373)
*Bangchen Yin,Jian Ouyang,Zhen Fan,Kailai Lin,Hanshi Hu,Dingshun Lv,Weiluo Ren,Hai Xiao,Ji Chen,Changsu Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hi-MLIP：一种能够可靠捕捉势能面曲率的机器学习原子间势，通过高效的HINT训练协议大幅减少昂贵Hessian标签需求，显著改善过渡态搜索和吉布斯自由能预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 势能面的局部曲率对于从第一性原理预测分子和材料的实验可观测量至关重要，但对于复杂系统来说仍然难以获得。需要开发能够可靠捕捉这种曲率的机器学习原子间势。

Method: 提出Hessian-informed机器学习原子间势（Hi-MLIP），开发高效的Hessian INformed Training（HINT）协议，包括Hessian预训练、构型采样、课程学习和随机投影Hessian损失等技术，实现Hessian标签需求降低2-4个数量级。

Result: Hi-MLIP显著改善过渡态搜索，使吉布斯自由能预测接近化学精度（尤其在数据稀缺情况下）。能够准确处理强非谐氢化物，再现声子重整化和超导临界温度，与实验结果高度一致，同时绕过非谐计算的计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该框架为增强机器学习原子间势的曲率感知能力提供了实用途径，在广泛系统中架起了模拟与实验可观测量之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Local curvature of potential energy surfaces is critical for predicting certain experimental observables of molecules and materials from first principles, yet it remains far beyond reach for complex systems. In this work, we introduce a Hessian-informed Machine Learning Interatomic Potential (Hi-MLIP) that captures such curvature reliably, thereby enabling accurate analysis of associated thermodynamic and kinetic phenomena. To make Hessian supervision practically viable, we develop a highly efficient training protocol, termed Hessian INformed Training (HINT), achieving two to four orders of magnitude reduction for the requirement of expensive Hessian labels. HINT integrates critical techniques, including Hessian pre-training, configuration sampling, curriculum learning and stochastic projection Hessian loss. Enabled by HINT, Hi-MLIP significantly improves transition-state search and brings Gibbs free-energy predictions close to chemical accuracy especially in data-scarce regimes. Our framework also enables accurate treatment of strongly anharmonic hydrides, reproducing phonon renormalization and superconducting critical temperatures in close agreement with experiment while bypassing the computational bottleneck of anharmonic calculations. These results establish a practical route to enhancing curvature awareness of machine learning interatomic potentials, bridging simulation and experimental observables across a wide range of systems.

</details>


### [41] [GlowQ: Group-Shared LOw-Rank Approximation for Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25385)
*Selim An,Il hong Suh,Yeseong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GlowQ提出了一种分组共享低秩近似方法，用于量化LLMs的精度恢复，通过缓存共享右因子减少参数和内存开销，同时保持层特定修正的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化技术（如BitsAndBytes、AWQ、GPTQ）在低比特表示（如4位）时会导致精度下降，而现有的低秩修正方法（如LQER、QERA、ASER）虽然能缓解这一问题，但需要恢复所有层并在每个解码器块中插入误差修正模块，这会增加延迟和内存开销。

Method: 提出GlowQ方法：1）采用分组共享低秩近似，为每个输入共享组缓存单个共享右因子；2）仅恢复提供最高精度收益的组或层；3）每个输入共享组计算一次高精度投影并跨模块重用；4）提出选择性变体GlowQ-S，仅在提供最大收益的地方应用缓存的共享模块。

Result: 相比强基线，GlowQ平均减少TTFB 5.6%，提高吞吐量9.6%，在WikiText-2上降低困惑度0.17%，下游任务准确率提高0.42个百分点。GlowQ-S进一步减少延迟，TTFB降低23.4%，吞吐量提高37.4%，同时平均准确率保持在0.2个百分点以内。

Conclusion: GlowQ通过分组共享低秩近似有效解决了量化LLMs的精度恢复问题，在显著减少延迟和内存开销的同时保持了模型精度，为高效部署大型语言模型提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Quantization techniques such as BitsAndBytes, AWQ, and GPTQ are widely used as a standard method in deploying large language models but often degrades accuracy when using low-bit representations, e.g., 4 bits. Low-rank correction methods (e.g., LQER, QERA, ASER) has been proposed to mitigate this issue, however, they restore all layers and insert error-correction modules into every decoder block, which increases latency and memory overhead. To address this limitation, we propose GlowQ, a group-shared low-rank approximation for quantized LLMs that caches a single shared right factor per input-sharing group and restores only the groups or layers that yield the highest accuracy benefit. GlowQ computes the high-precision projection once per input-sharing group and reuses it across its modules, reducing parameter and memory overhead, and retaining the expressivity of layer-specific corrections. We also propose a selective variant, GlowQ-S, that applies the cached shared module only where it provides the largest benefit. Compared with strong baselines, our approach reduces TTFB by (5.6%) and increases throughput by (9.6%) on average, while reducing perplexity on WikiText-2 by (0.17%) and increasing downstream accuracy by 0.42 percentage points. The selective model GlowQ-S further reduces latency, cutting TTFB by (23.4%) and increasing throughput by (37.4%), while maintaining accuracy within 0.2 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [42] [Maximum Entropy Behavior Exploration for Sim2Real Zero-Shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25464)
*Jiajun Hu,Nuria Armengol Urpi,Jin Cheng,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FB-MEBE：一种结合无监督行为探索和正则化批评器的在线零样本强化学习算法，用于四足机器人控制，无需下游任务知识即可收集多样化数据，生成可直接部署到硬件的自然策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本RL算法需要预收集相关且多样化的数据集，但在没有下游任务先验知识的情况下，无向探索往往产生低多样性数据，导致下游性能差且策略无法直接部署到硬件。

Method: 提出FB-MEBE算法：1）结合无监督行为探索策略，通过最大化已实现行为分布的熵来促进探索；2）引入正则化批评器，将恢复的策略塑造成更自然、物理上合理的行为；3）基于前向-后向算法框架，实现在线零样本RL。

Result: 在模拟下游任务中，FB-MEBE相比其他探索策略获得改进性能；生成的自然策略可直接部署到硬件而无需进一步微调；算法在真实机器人系统上验证有效。

Conclusion: FB-MEBE通过熵最大化的行为探索和正则化批评器，解决了零样本RL中数据集多样性和策略自然性问题，实现了可直接硬件部署的在线零样本强化学习。

Abstract: Zero-shot reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms aim to learn a family of policies from a reward-free dataset, and recover optimal policies for any reward function directly at test time. Naturally, the quality of the pretraining dataset determines the performance of the recovered policies across tasks. However, pre-collecting a relevant, diverse dataset without prior knowledge of the downstream tasks of interest remains a challenge. In this work, we study $\textit{online}$ zero-shot RL for quadrupedal control on real robotic systems, building upon the Forward-Backward (FB) algorithm. We observe that undirected exploration yields low-diversity data, leading to poor downstream performance and rendering policies impractical for direct hardware deployment. Therefore, we introduce FB-MEBE, an online zero-shot RL algorithm that combines an unsupervised behavior exploration strategy with a regularization critic. FB-MEBE promotes exploration by maximizing the entropy of the achieved behavior distribution. Additionally, a regularization critic shapes the recovered policies toward more natural and physically plausible behaviors. We empirically demonstrate that FB-MEBE achieves and improved performance compared to other exploration strategies in a range of simulated downstream tasks, and that it renders natural policies that can be seamlessly deployed to hardware without further finetuning. Videos and code available on our website.

</details>


### [43] [Not a fragment, but the whole: Map-based evaluation of data-driven Fire Danger Index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25469)
*Shahbaz Alvi,Italo Epicoco,Jose Maria Costa Saura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种与真实决策对齐的森林火灾预报模型评估新方法，系统评估火灾活动预测准确性和误报率，并证明ML模型集成能改善火灾识别和减少误报


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法在预测野火发生时，虽然利用了高分辨率数据和传统过程模型忽略的预测因子，但标准评估指标对火灾危险指数(FDI)预报的操作性能衡量有限，且评估时常未充分考虑误报率的重要性

Method: 重新审视每日FDI模型评估范式，提出与真实决策对齐的森林火灾预报模型评估新方法，系统评估火灾活动预测准确性和误报率，并采用机器学习模型集成方法

Result: 机器学习模型集成能够同时改善火灾识别能力和减少误报率

Conclusion: 需要开发与操作决策更一致的评估方法，机器学习模型集成是提升森林火灾预报性能的有效策略

Abstract: A growing body of literature has focused on predicting wildfire occurrence using machine learning methods, capitalizing on high-resolution data and fire predictors that canonical process-based frameworks largely ignore. Standard evaluation metrics for an ML classifier, while important, provide a potentially limited measure of the model's operational performance for the Fire Danger Index (FDI) forecast. Furthermore, model evaluation is frequently conducted without adequately accounting for false positive rates, despite their critical relevance in operational contexts. In this paper, we revisit the daily FDI model evaluation paradigm and propose a novel method for evaluating a forest fire forecasting model that is aligned with real-world decision-making. Furthermore, we systematically assess performance in accurately predicting fire activity and the false positives (false alarms). We further demonstrate that an ensemble of ML models improves both fire identification and reduces false positives.

</details>


### [44] [Causal-INSIGHT: Probing Temporal Models to Extract Causal Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25473)
*Benjamin Redden,Hui Wang,Shuyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Causal-INSIGHT是一个模型无关的事后解释框架，用于从训练好的时序预测器中提取模型隐含的、有向的、时滞影响结构，通过干预式输入钳制分析预测器的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多元时间序列中的有向时序交互对于解释复杂动态系统和基于其训练的预测模型至关重要。现有方法通常关注数据生成过程的因果结构推断，而本文关注从已训练预测器中提取模型依赖的影响结构。

Method: 提出Causal-INSIGHT框架：1）在推理时对固定预训练预测器应用系统性的、受干预启发的输入钳制；2）从预测器响应中构建有向时序影响信号；3）引入Qbic准则（基于稀疏性的图选择准则），在无需真实图标签的情况下平衡预测保真度和结构复杂度。

Result: 在合成、模拟和现实基准测试中，Causal-INSIGHT能够泛化到不同的骨干架构，保持竞争力的结构准确性，并在应用于现有预测器时显著改善时序延迟定位性能。

Conclusion: Causal-INSIGHT提供了一种有效的方法来提取预测器隐含的有向时序影响结构，为理解复杂动态系统中预测模型的依赖关系提供了实用的解释工具。

Abstract: Understanding directed temporal interactions in multivariate time series is essential for interpreting complex dynamical systems and the predictive models trained on them. We present Causal-INSIGHT, a model-agnostic, post-hoc interpretation framework for extracting model-implied (predictor-dependent), directed, time-lagged influence structure from trained temporal predictors. Rather than inferring causal structure at the level of the data-generating process, Causal-INSIGHT analyzes how a fixed, pre-trained predictor responds to systematic, intervention-inspired input clamping applied at inference time.
  From these responses, we construct directed temporal influence signals that reflect the dependencies the predictor relies on for prediction, and introduce Qbic, a sparsity-aware graph selection criterion that balances predictive fidelity and structural complexity without requiring ground-truth graph labels. Experiments across synthetic, simulated, and realistic benchmarks show that Causal-INSIGHT generalizes across diverse backbone architectures, maintains competitive structural accuracy, and yields significant improvements in temporal delay localization when applied to existing predictors.

</details>


### [45] [How Class Ontology and Data Scale Affect Audio Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25476)
*Manuel Milling,Andreas Triantafyllopoulos,Alexander Gebhard,Simon Rampp,Björn W. Schuller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了音频到音频迁移学习，发现预训练数据量和类别数的增加对迁移学习有积极影响，但预训练与下游任务的相似性影响更大，能促使模型学习可比特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管迁移学习在深度学习中广泛应用且效果明显，但其内部工作机制、何时有效以及效果如何等问题仍不明确。本研究旨在通过音频到音频迁移学习的系统性研究来深入理解迁移学习的工作原理。

Method: 在AudioSet的（基于本体的）子集上预训练多种模型状态，然后在三个计算机听觉任务上进行微调：声学场景识别、鸟类活动识别和语音命令识别。通过控制预训练数据的样本数量和类别数量，系统研究迁移学习效果。

Result: 增加预训练数据的样本数量和类别数量对迁移学习都有积极影响。然而，预训练任务与下游任务之间的相似性影响更大，这种相似性能够促使模型学习可比的特征表示。

Conclusion: 迁移学习的效果不仅取决于预训练数据的规模，更重要的是预训练任务与下游任务之间的相似性。任务相似性能够使模型学习到更具可比性的特征，从而在迁移学习中取得更好效果。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a crucial concept within deep learning that allows artificial neural networks to benefit from a large pre-training data basis when confronted with a task of limited data. Despite its ubiquitous use and clear benefits, there are still many open questions regarding the inner workings of transfer learning and, in particular, regarding the understanding of when and how well it works. To that extent, we perform a rigorous study focusing on audio-to-audio transfer learning, in which we pre-train various model states on (ontology-based) subsets of AudioSet and fine-tune them on three computer audition tasks, namely acoustic scene recognition, bird activity recognition, and speech command recognition. We report that increasing the number of samples and classes in the pre-training data both have a positive impact on transfer learning. This is, however, generally surpassed by similarity between pre-training and the downstream task, which can lead the model to learn comparable features.

</details>


### [46] [Interpretable PM2.5 Forecasting for Urban Air Quality: A Comparative Study of Operational Time-Series Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25495)
*Moazzam Umer Gondal,Hamad ul Qudous,Asma Ahmad Farhan,Sultan Alamri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了三种轻量级可解释模型（SARIMAX、Facebook Prophet、NeuralProphet）在北京PM2.5小时预测中的性能，发现在不同自适应机制下，Facebook Prophet和SARIMAX表现优于NeuralProphet，且在线残差校正能显著提升预测精度和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空气质量预测框架通常依赖复杂、数据密集且计算需求高的模型，本研究旨在探究轻量级、可解释的预测方法是否能在北京PM2.5小时预测中提供具有竞争力的性能。

Method: 采用泄漏感知预测工作流，结合时序数据划分、预处理、特征选择和外生驱动建模（Perfect Prognosis设置）。评估三种预测模型家族：SARIMAX、Facebook Prophet和NeuralProphet。在两种自适应机制下测试：周度前向滚动重新拟合和冻结模型预测结合在线残差校正。

Result: 在前向滚动重新拟合机制下，Facebook Prophet表现最佳（MAE 37.61，RMSE 50.10），且计算时间显著少于NeuralProphet。在冻结模型机制下，在线残差校正提升了Facebook Prophet和SARIMAX的性能，校正后的SARIMAX获得最低总体误差（MAE 32.50，RMSE 46.85）。NeuralProphet在两个机制下均表现较差且不稳定，残差校正对其无改善作用。校正后的Facebook Prophet在保持相近误差的同时，将运行时间从15分21.91秒大幅减少到46.60秒。

Conclusion: 轻量级加性预测策略在城市空气质量预测中仍具有高度竞争力，在准确性、可解释性和计算效率之间提供了实用的平衡，为实际部署提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Accurate short-term air-quality forecasting is essential for public health protection and urban management, yet many recent forecasting frameworks rely on complex, data-intensive, and computationally demanding models. This study investigates whether lightweight and interpretable forecasting approaches can provide competitive performance for hourly PM2.5 prediction in Beijing, China. Using multi-year pollutant and meteorological time-series data, we developed a leakage-aware forecasting workflow that combined chronological data partitioning, preprocessing, feature selection, and exogenous-driver modeling under the Perfect Prognosis setting. Three forecasting families were evaluated: SARIMAX, Facebook Prophet, and NeuralProphet. To assess practical deployment behavior, the models were tested under two adaptive regimes: weekly walk-forward refitting and frozen forecasting with online residual correction. Results showed clear differences in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. Under walk-forward refitting, Facebook Prophet achieved the strongest completed performance, with an MAE of $37.61$ and an RMSE of $50.10$, while also requiring substantially less execution time than NeuralProphet. In the frozen-model regime, online residual correction improved Facebook Prophet and SARIMAX, with corrected SARIMAX yielding the lowest overall error (MAE $32.50$; RMSE $46.85$). NeuralProphet remained less accurate and less stable across both regimes, and residual correction did not improve its forecasts. Notably, corrected Facebook Prophet reached nearly the same error as its walk-forward counterpart while reducing runtime from $15$ min $21.91$ sec to $46.60$ sec. These findings show that lightweight additive forecasting strategies can remain highly competitive for urban air-quality prediction, offering a practical balance between accuracy, interpretability, ...

</details>


### [47] [Revisiting On-Policy Distillation: Empirical Failure Modes and Simple Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25562)
*Yuqian Fu,Haohuan Huang,Kaiwen Jiang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了在长序列设置中基于采样token的在线蒸馏方法的脆弱性，提出了基于教师top-K局部支持匹配的改进方法，通过截断反向KL散度和top-p采样等技术提升优化稳定性和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型语言模型的后训练中，在线蒸馏方法通过评估教师模型对学生生成轨迹的反馈来优化学生模型。然而，在长序列设置中，常用的基于采样token的变体存在脆弱性问题：它将分布匹配简化为单token信号，并且随着轨迹偏离教师常见前缀而变得越来越不可靠。需要重新审视在线蒸馏的估计器和实现方法。

Method: 论文从理论和实证两方面分析在线蒸馏：理论上比较token级和序列级反向KL散度的偏差-方差权衡；实证上识别采样token在线蒸馏的三种失效模式（不平衡单token信号、不可靠的教师指导、tokenizer或特殊token不匹配）。提出基于教师top-K局部支持匹配的改进方法，实现为带有top-p轨迹采样和特殊token掩码的截断反向KL散度。

Result: 在单任务数学推理和多任务智能体+数学训练中，提出的目标函数相比采样token在线蒸馏表现出更稳定的优化过程和更好的下游性能。理论分析显示token级在线蒸馏相对于序列级反向KL散度存在偏差，但具有更紧的最坏情况方差界限；玩具实验验证了相同的权衡，更强的未来奖励耦合会产生更高的梯度方差和更不稳定的学习。

Conclusion: 论文重新审视了在线蒸馏方法，识别了采样token变体在长序列设置中的关键失效模式，并提出基于教师top-K局部支持匹配的改进目标函数。该方法通过更好的偏差-方差权衡和实现细节处理，在数学推理和智能体任务中实现了更稳定和有效的模型训练。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is appealing for large language model (LLM) post-training because it evaluates teacher feedback on student-generated rollouts rather than fixed teacher traces. In long-horizon settings, however, the common sampled-token variant is fragile: it reduces distribution matching to a one-token signal and becomes increasingly unreliable as rollouts drift away from prefixes the teacher commonly visits. We revisit OPD from the estimator and implementation sides. Theoretically, token-level OPD is biased relative to sequence-level reverse-KL, but it has a much tighter worst-case variance bound; our toy study shows the same tradeoff empirically, with stronger future-reward coupling producing higher gradient variance and less stable learning. Empirically, we identify three failure modes of sampled-token OPD: an imbalanced one-token signal, unreliable teacher guidance on student-generated prefixes, and distortions caused by tokenizer or special-token mismatch. We address these issues with teacher top-K local support matching, implemented as truncated reverse-KL with top-p rollout sampling and special-token masking. Across single-task math reasoning and multi-task agentic-plus-math training, this objective yields more stable optimization and better downstream performance than sampled-token OPD.

</details>


### [48] [On Neural Scaling Laws for Weather Emulation through Continual Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25687)
*Shashank Subramanian,Alexander Kiefer,Arnur Nigmetov,Amir Gholami,Dmitriy Morozov,Michael W. Mahoney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了科学机器学习中的神经缩放定律，特别是在天气预报领域，通过最小化的Swin Transformer架构和持续训练策略，展示了可预测的缩放趋势和优于标准学习率调度的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经缩放定律在自然语言处理和计算机视觉领域是构建基础模型的基石，但科学机器学习中的缩放行为研究不足。本文旨在分析天气预报模型的缩放行为，为高效资源分配提供诊断工具。

Method: 采用最小化、可扩展的通用Swin Transformer架构，使用恒定学习率的持续训练和周期性冷却作为高效训练策略。系统探索了不同模型和数据集大小在各种计算预算下的表现，构建IsoFLOP曲线。

Result: 模型遵循可预测的缩放趋势，性能优于标准余弦学习率调度。冷却阶段可用于改进下游性能，如实现更长的预测时间跨度和更清晰的频谱损失调整预测。识别了计算最优训练机制。

Conclusion: 神经缩放定律可作为科学机器学习中高效资源分配的重要诊断工具。通过外推这些趋势到更大规模，揭示了潜在性能极限，为天气预报模型的发展提供了指导。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws, which in some domains can predict the performance of large neural networks as a function of model, data, and compute scale, are the cornerstone of building foundation models in Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision. We study neural scaling in Scientific Machine Learning, focusing on models for weather forecasting. To analyze scaling behavior in as simple a setting as possible, we adopt a minimal, scalable, general-purpose Swin Transformer architecture, and we use continual training with constant learning rates and periodic cooldowns as an efficient training strategy. We show that models trained in this minimalist way follow predictable scaling trends and even outperform standard cosine learning rate schedules. Cooldown phases can be re-purposed to improve downstream performance, e.g., enabling accurate multi-step rollouts over longer forecast horizons as well as sharper predictions through spectral loss adjustments. We also systematically explore a wide range of model and dataset sizes under various compute budgets to construct IsoFLOP curves, and we identify compute-optimal training regimes. Extrapolating these trends to larger scales highlights potential performance limits, demonstrating that neural scaling can serve as an important diagnostic for efficient resource allocation. We open-source our code for reproducibility.

</details>

### [49] [Belief-Driven Multi-Agent Collaboration via Approximate Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium for Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24973)
*Weiwei Fang,Lin Li,Kaize Shi,Yu Yang,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: BEACOF是一个基于信念驱动的自适应协作框架，通过建模不完全信息动态博弈来解决静态交互拓扑导致的群体思维和死锁问题，实现更真实的社会模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的多智能体框架主要采用静态交互拓扑，无法捕捉现实世界中合作知识合成与竞争批判推理之间的动态振荡，导致不现实的"群体思维"或无效僵局，降低了社会模拟的可信度。

Method: 提出BEACOF（信念驱动自适应协作框架），受完美贝叶斯均衡启发，将社会交互建模为不完全信息动态博弈。智能体迭代地精炼对同伴能力的概率信念，并自主调整协作策略，在不确定性下确保序列理性决策。

Result: 在对抗性（司法）、开放性（社会）和混合（医疗）场景中验证，BEACOF能够防止协调失败，促进高质量解决方案的稳健收敛，展示了可靠社会模拟的优越潜力。

Conclusion: BEACOF通过动态博弈建模解决了协作类型选择与能力估计之间的循环依赖问题，为高保真社会模拟提供了更真实的交互框架，代码和数据集已公开。

Abstract: High-fidelity social simulation is pivotal for addressing complex Web societal challenges, yet it demands agents capable of authentically replicating the dynamic spectrum of human interaction. Current LLM-based multi-agent frameworks, however, predominantly adhere to static interaction topologies, failing to capture the fluid oscillation between cooperative knowledge synthesis and competitive critical reasoning seen in real-world scenarios. This rigidity often leads to unrealistic ``groupthink'' or unproductive deadlocks, undermining the credibility of simulations for decision support. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{BEACOF}, a \textit{belief-driven adaptive collaboration framework} inspired by Perfect Bayesian Equilibrium (PBE). By modeling social interaction as a dynamic game of incomplete information, BEACOF rigorously addresses the circular dependency between collaboration type selection and capability estimation. Agents iteratively refine probabilistic beliefs about peer capabilities and autonomously modulate their collaboration strategy, thereby ensuring sequentially rational decisions under uncertainty. Validated across adversarial (judicial), open-ended (social) and mixed (medical) scenarios, BEACOF prevents coordination failures and fosters robust convergence toward high-quality solutions, demonstrating superior potential for reliable social simulation. Source codes and datasets are publicly released at: https://github.com/WUT-IDEA/BEACOF.

</details>


### [50] [Ultra-fast Traffic Nowcasting and Control via Differentiable Agent-based Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25068)
*Fumiyasu Makinoshima,Yuya Yamaguchi,Eigo Segawa,Koichiro Niinuma,Sean Qian*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种可微分智能体交通仿真器，实现超快速模型校准、交通预测和控制，为交通数字孪生提供实用计算基础


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统细粒度交通仿真不可微分，依赖低效的无梯度优化，导致实际应用中的校准计算不可行，阻碍了交通数字孪生的发展

Method: 开发可微分智能体交通仿真器，采用多种可微分计算技术模拟个体车辆运动，包括随机决策和智能体间交互，确保整个仿真轨迹端到端可微分

Result: 在芝加哥路网（超1万校准参数）上仿真超100万辆车，速度达实时173倍；30分钟数据校准需455秒，1小时预测需21秒，控制问题求解需728秒，完整循环不到20分钟

Conclusion: 该工作为实现交通数字孪生提供了实用计算基础，使校准-预测-控制循环能在20分钟内完成，为干预措施留出40分钟提前期

Abstract: Traffic digital twins, which inform policymakers of effective interventions based on large-scale, high-fidelity computational models calibrated to real-world traffic, hold promise for addressing societal challenges in our rapidly urbanizing world. However, conventional fine-grained traffic simulations are non-differentiable and typically rely on inefficient gradient-free optimization, making calibration for real-world applications computationally infeasible. Here we present a differentiable agent-based traffic simulator that enables ultra-fast model calibration, traffic nowcasting, and control on large-scale networks. We develop several differentiable computing techniques for simulating individual vehicle movements, including stochastic decision-making and inter-agent interactions, while ensuring that entire simulation trajectories remain end-to-end differentiable for efficient gradient-based optimization. On the large-scale Chicago road network, with over 10,000 calibration parameters, our model simulates more than one million vehicles at 173 times real-time speed. This ultra-fast simulation, together with efficient gradient-based optimization, enables us to complete model calibration using the previous 30 minutes of traffic data in 455 s, provide a one-hour-ahead traffic nowcast in 21 s, and solve the resulting traffic control problem in 728 s. This yields a full calibration--nowcast--control loop in under 20 minutes, leaving about 40 minutes of lead time for implementing interventions. Our work thus provides a practical computational basis for realizing traffic digital twins.

</details>


### [51] [AD-CARE: A Guideline-grounded, Modality-agnostic LLM Agent for Real-world Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis with Multi-cohort Assessment, Fairness Analysis, and Reader Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25322)
*Wenlong Hou,Sheng Bi,Guangqian Yang,Lihao Liu,Ye Du,Hanxiao Xue,Juncheng Wang,Yuxiang Feng,Yue Xun,Nanxi Yu,Ning Mao,Mo Yang,Yi Wah Eva Cheung,Ling Long,Kay Chen Tan,Lequan Yu,Xiaomeng Ma,Shaozhen Yan,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AD-CARE是一个模态无关的智能体，能够从不完整、异构的多模态输入中生成符合临床指南的阿尔茨海默病诊断报告，无需填补缺失模态，显著提高诊断准确性并减少性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病诊断面临现实世界数据不完整、异构以及跨站点和人口统计学差异的挑战。现有大语言模型在AD应用中局限于回答狭窄问题，而非生成支持临床决策的综合诊断报告。

Method: AD-CARE采用模态无关的智能体架构，通过动态编排专业诊断工具并将临床指南嵌入LLM驱动的推理过程，从不完整、异构输入中生成透明、报告式输出，无需填补缺失模态。

Result: 在6个队列10,303个病例中，AD-CARE达到84.9%诊断准确率，相对基线方法提升4.2%-13.7%。减少种族和年龄亚组间的性能差异（指标离散度降低21%-68%和28%-51%），在读者研究中提高神经科医生和放射科医生准确率6%-11%，并缩短决策时间一半以上。

Conclusion: AD-CARE是一个可扩展、实际可部署的框架，能够集成到常规临床工作流程中，为阿尔茨海默病提供多模态决策支持，显著改善诊断准确性和公平性。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a growing global health challenge as populations age, and timely, accurate diagnosis is essential to reduce individual and societal burden. However, real-world AD assessment is hampered by incomplete, heterogeneous multimodal data and variability across sites and patient demographics. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in biomedicine, their use in AD has largely been confined to answering narrow, disease-specific questions rather than generating comprehensive diagnostic reports that support clinical decision-making. Here we expand LLM capabilities for clinical decision support by introducing AD-CARE, a modality-agnostic agent that performs guideline-grounded diagnostic assessment from incomplete, heterogeneous inputs without imputing missing modalities. By dynamically orchestrating specialized diagnostic tools and embedding clinical guidelines into LLM-driven reasoning, AD-CARE generates transparent, report-style outputs aligned with real-world clinical workflows. Across six cohorts comprising 10,303 cases, AD-CARE achieved 84.9% diagnostic accuracy, delivering 4.2%-13.7% relative improvements over baseline methods. Despite cohort-level differences, dataset-specific accuracies remain robust (80.4%-98.8%), and the agent consistently outperforms all baselines. AD-CARE reduced performance disparities across racial and age subgroups, decreasing the average dispersion of four metrics by 21%-68% and 28%-51%, respectively. In a controlled reader study, the agent improved neurologist and radiologist accuracy by 6%-11% and more than halved decision time. The framework yielded 2.29%-10.66% absolute gains over eight backbone LLMs and converges their performance. These results show that AD-CARE is a scalable, practically deployable framework that can be integrated into routine clinical workflows for multimodal decision support in AD.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [52] [FODMP: Fast One-Step Diffusion of Movement Primitives Generation for Time-Dependent Robot Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24806)
*Xirui Shi,Arya Ebrahimi,Yi Hu,Jun Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FODMP：通过单步一致性蒸馏将扩散模型蒸馏到ProDMP轨迹参数空间，在保留运动基元时间结构的同时实现高速推理，比现有方法快7-10倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人学习中的扩散模型存在明显权衡：动作分块扩散策略（如ManiCM）推理快但只能预测短运动片段，无法捕获时间依赖的运动基元；运动基元扩散（MPD）虽能生成时间结构化运动，但多步扩散过程导致推理延迟过高，不适用于实时控制

Method: 提出FODMP框架，将扩散模型蒸馏到概率动态运动基元（ProDMPs）的轨迹参数空间，使用单步解码器生成运动。通过单步一致性蒸馏消除推理瓶颈，同时保留运动基元的时间结构

Result: 在标准操作基准（MetaWorld、ManiSkill）上，FODMP比MPD快10倍，比动作分块扩散策略快7倍，同时匹配或超过其成功率。通过生成快速加减速运动基元，FODMP使机器人能够实时拦截快速飞行的球，而其他方法响应过慢

Conclusion: FODMP成功解决了扩散模型在机器人学习中的速度-表达能力权衡问题，实现了既能生成时间结构化运动基元又能高速推理的框架，适用于闭环视觉控制场景

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used for robot learning, but current designs face a clear trade-off. Action-chunking diffusion policies like ManiCM are fast to run, yet they only predict short segments of motion. This makes them reactive, but unable to capture time-dependent motion primitives, such as following a spring-damper-like behavior with built-in dynamic profiles of acceleration and deceleration. Recently, Movement Primitive Diffusion (MPD) partially addresses this limitation by parameterizing full trajectories using Probabilistic Dynamic Movement Primitives (ProDMPs), thereby enabling the generation of temporally structured motions. Nevertheless, MPD integrates the motion decoder directly into a multi-step diffusion process, resulting in prohibitively high inference latency that limits its applicability in real-time control settings. We propose FODMP (Fast One-step Diffusion of Movement Primitives), a new framework that distills diffusion models into the ProDMPs trajectory parameter space and generates motion using a single-step decoder. FODMP retains the temporal structure of movement primitives while eliminating the inference bottleneck through single-step consistency distillation. This enables robots to execute time-dependent primitives at high inference speed, suitable for closed-loop vision-based control. On standard manipulation benchmarks (MetaWorld, ManiSkill), FODMP runs up to 10 times faster than MPD and 7 times faster than action-chunking diffusion policies, while matching or exceeding their success rates. Beyond speed, by generating fast acceleration-deceleration motion primitives, FODMP allows the robot to intercept and securely catch a fast-flying ball, whereas action-chunking diffusion policy and MPD respond too slowly for real-time interception.

</details>


### [53] [Characterization of Constraints in Flexible Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24813)
*Samrat Bhattacharyya,Nabil Simaan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在线路径规划算法，用于在未知环境中安全自主操纵柔性约束物体，通过实时识别和表征感知到的柔性约束及全局刚度，实现同时探索、表征和安全操纵弹性系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中安全操纵柔性约束物体（如手术中的器官牵拉）需要实时识别约束特性，传统方法缺乏对柔性约束的实时表征能力，限制了机器人在复杂环境中的自主操作能力

Method: 基于局部力和位置信息的约束探索方法，在物体轨迹的多个位姿处考虑感知约束刚度，利用刚度特征向量信息，通过简单机械约束（如铰链和平面约束）的图谱来表征和识别全局刚度行为

Result: 通过仿真和实验验证了算法有效性，能够实时识别多种常见简单机械约束（如柔性铰链）并确定相关螺旋参数，证明了同时进行全局约束/刚度探索和安全操纵柔性约束物体的可行性

Conclusion: 该方法为实现柔性约束物体的安全协同操纵（如手术中的器官牵拉和操作）提供了可行途径，通过实时约束识别和刚度表征提高了自主操作的适应性和安全性

Abstract: This paper presents an online path planning algorithm for safe autonomous manipulation of a flexibly constrained object in an unknown environment. Methods for real time identification and characterization of perceived flexible constraints and global stiffness are presented. Used in tandem, these methods allow a robot to simultaneously explore, characterize, and manipulate an elastic system safely. Navigation without a-priori knowledge of the system is achieved using constraint exploration based on local force and position information. The perceived constraint stiffness is considered at multiple poses along an object's (system) trajectory. Using stiffness eigenvector information, global stiffness behavior is characterized and identified using an atlas of simple mechanical constraints, such as hinges and planar constraints. Validation of these algorithms is carried out by simulation and experimentally. The ability to recognize several common simple mechanical constraints (such as a flexible hinge) in real time, and to subsequently identify relevant screw parameters is demonstrated. These results suggest the feasibility of simultaneous global constrain/stiffness exploration and safe manipulation of flexibly constrained objects. We believe that this approach will eventually enable safe cooperative manipulation in applications such as organ retraction and manipulation during surgery

</details>


### [54] [Wireless bioelectronics for untethered biohybrid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24959)
*Hiroyuki Tetsuka,Minoru Hirano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了生物混合机器人控制策略的最新进展，包括无线电刺激、无线光刺激和神经肌肉整合，并展望了神经类器官-生物电子学集成以实现自主闭环生物混合机器人的未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物混合机器人将活体组织与工程人工结构集成，实现生物启发的驱动和行为。当前主要挑战在于如何在不依赖有线连接或浸入细胞培养基的笨重硬件的情况下传递刺激和控制信号。

Method: 通过无线生物电子学（射频磁场远程传递控制信号）和无线光电子学（嵌入光发射器远程控制光遗传修饰的肌肉机器人）实现远程刺激。进一步整合神经肌肉接头，利用生物信号转导实现通过无线频率和时间分复用选择性控制多个执行器。

Result: 总结了生物混合机器人控制策略的最新进展：无线电刺激、无线光刺激和神经肌肉整合。提出了跨领域设计原则，并展望了神经类器官-生物电子学集成的发展方向。

Conclusion: 生物混合机器人控制策略已取得显著进展，未来发展方向是实现神经类器官与生物电子学的协同集成，构建自主、闭环的生物混合机器人系统。

Abstract: Biohybrid robots integrate living tissues with engineered artificial structures to achieve organism-inspired actuation and behavior. A persistent challenge is delivering stimulation and control signals without relying on tethered wiring or bulky hardware immersed in cell-culture media. Wireless bioelectronics addresses this limitation by enabling the remote transfer of control signals, typically via radio-frequency magnetic fields, to locally stimulate muscle tissues at tissue-electrode interfaces. In parallel, wireless optoelectronics enables remote control of optogenetically modified, muscle-based robots by embedding light emitters that initiate muscle actuation through light-gated ion channels. Further advances incorporate neuromuscular junctions, leveraging biological signal transduction to enable selective control of multiple actuators through wireless frequency- and time-division multiplexing. This perspective article summarizes recent advances in control strategies for biohybrid robots, namely, wireless electrical stimulation, wireless optical stimulation, and neuromuscular integration. Then this describes cross-cutting design principles and highlights a future direction, namely, co-integration of neural organoid-bioelectronics toward autonomous, closed-loop biohybrid robots.

</details>


### [55] [Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation, Sequencing, and Optimal Trajectory Generation in Obstacle-Rich 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24908)
*Yunes Alqudsi,Murat Makaraci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IMD-TAPP是一个端到端框架，用于协调无人机团队在复杂三维环境中执行任务，通过联合优化任务分配、路径规划和轨迹生成来最小化任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂三维环境中协调无人机团队需要将离散的任务规划（决定哪个无人机服务哪个目标以及顺序）与连续时间的轨迹合成（确保避碰和动态可行性）有机结合。现有方法往往将这两个问题分开处理，导致次优解或安全违规。

Method: 1. 将工作空间离散化为3D导航图，通过图搜索计算机器人到目标和目标到目标的障碍感知旅行成本；2. 使用注入粒子群优化(IPSO)方案，结合多线性分配，探索耦合的任务分配/排序替代方案以最小化任务完成时间；3. 将生成的航点路径通过生成-优化例程转换为时间参数化的最小snap轨迹，迭代验证障碍物清除和机器人间距离，在安全裕度被违反时触发重新规划。

Result: 在复杂3D场景的广泛MATLAB模拟中，IMD-TAPP始终产生动态可行、无碰撞的轨迹，同时实现有竞争力的完成时间。在一个具有两个无人机服务多个目标的代表性案例研究中，该方法实现了136秒的最小任务时间，并在整个执行过程中保持所需的安全约束。

Conclusion: IMD-TAPP提供了一个有效的端到端框架，用于协调无人机团队在复杂三维环境中的任务执行，通过联合优化任务分配、路径规划和轨迹生成，在保证安全约束的同时最小化任务完成时间。

Abstract: Coordinating teams of aerial robots in cluttered three-dimensional (3D) environments requires a principled integration of discrete mission planning-deciding which robot serves which goals and in what order -- with continuous-time trajectory synthesis that enforces collision avoidance and dynamic feasibility. This paper introduces IMD-TAPP (Integrated Multi-Drone Task Allocation and Path Planning), an end-to-end framework that jointly addresses multi-goal allocation, tour sequencing, and safe trajectory generation for quadrotor teams operating in obstacle-rich spaces. IMD--TAPP first discretizes the workspace into a 3D navigation graph and computes obstacle-aware robot-to-goal and goal-to-goal travel costs via graph-search-based pathfinding. These costs are then embedded within an Injected Particle Swarm Optimization (IPSO) scheme, guided by multiple linear assignment, to efficiently explore coupled assignment/ordering alternatives and to minimize mission makespan. Finally, the resulting waypoint tours are transformed into time-parameterized minimum-snap trajectories through a generation-and-optimization routine equipped with iterative validation of obstacle clearance and inter-robot separation, triggering re-planning when safety margins are violated. Extensive MATLAB simulations across cluttered 3D scenarios demonstrate that IMD--TAPP consistently produces dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories while achieving competitive completion times. In a representative case study with two drones serving multiple goals, the proposed approach attains a minimum mission time of 136~s while maintaining the required safety constraints throughout execution.

</details>


### [56] [CROSS: A Mixture-of-Experts Reinforcement Learning Framework for Generalizable Large-Scale Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24930)
*Xibei Chen,Yifeng Zhang,Yuxiang Xiao,Mingfeng Fan,Maonan Wang,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CROSS：基于混合专家（MoE）的去中心化强化学习框架，用于可泛化的自适应交通信号控制，通过预测对比聚类识别交通模式，并通过场景自适应MoE模块实现策略专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的自适应交通信号控制方法通常对所有场景使用单一共享策略，其有限的表示能力难以捕捉多样化的交通动态并泛化到未见环境。需要解决大规模ATSC中不同交叉口拓扑和高度动态复杂交通需求模式带来的挑战。

Method: 提出CROSS框架：1）预测对比聚类（PCC）模块，通过预测短期状态转移来识别潜在交通模式，结合聚类和对比学习增强模式级表示；2）场景自适应MoE模块，在共享策略基础上集成多个专家，实现自适应专业化和更灵活的场景特定策略。

Result: 在SUMO模拟器上对合成和真实世界交通数据集进行广泛实验。与最先进的基线方法相比，CROSS通过改进对多样化交通场景的表示，实现了优越的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: CROSS框架通过结合预测对比聚类和场景自适应混合专家系统，有效解决了大规模自适应交通信号控制中的泛化问题，能够更好地表示多样化交通动态并适应未见环境。

Abstract: Recent advances in robotics, automation, and artificial intelligence have enabled urban traffic systems to operate with increasing autonomy towards future smart cities, powered in part by the development of adaptive traffic signal control (ATSC), which dynamically optimizes signal phases to mitigate congestion and optimize traffic. However, achieving effective and generalizable large-scale ATSC remains a significant challenge due to the diverse intersection topologies and highly dynamic, complex traffic demand patterns across the network. Existing RL-based methods typically use a single shared policy for all scenarios, whose limited representational capacity makes it difficult to capture diverse traffic dynamics and generalize to unseen environments. To address these challenges, we propose CROSS, a novel Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based decentralized RL framework for generalizable ATSC. We first introduce a Predictive Contrastive Clustering (PCC) module that forecasts short-term state transitions to identify latent traffic patterns, followed by clustering and contrastive learning to enhance pattern-level representation. We further design a Scenario-Adaptive MoE module that augments a shared policy with multiple experts, thus enabling adaptive specialization and more flexible scenario-specific strategies. We conduct extensive experiments in the SUMO simulator on both synthetic and real-world traffic datasets. Compared with state-of-the-art baselines, CROSS achieves superior performance and generalization through improved representation of diverse traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [57] [A Minimum-Energy Control Approach for Redundant Mobile Manipulators in Physical Human-Robot Interaction Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25259)
*Davide Tebaldi,Niccolò Paradisi,Fabio Pini,Luigi Biagiotti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于移动机械臂的控制方法，旨在最小化人机物理交互中整个系统的动能，并通过孔轴装配任务验证其有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂相比固定基座机械臂能执行更多任务，但增加了自由度，使控制设计更具挑战性且更需性能优化。在物理人机交互应用中，需要开发合适的控制方法来优化系统性能。

Method: 提出一种移动机械臂控制方法，该机械臂由移动基座和顶部安装的机械臂组成。控制目标是最小化整个移动机械臂系统在物理人机交互应用中的总动能。

Result: 通过孔轴装配任务进行实验测试，结果表明所提出的方法减少了整个机器人系统的总动能，相比基准方法提高了系统性能。

Conclusion: 提出的控制方法能有效优化移动机械臂在物理人机交互中的性能，通过最小化系统动能改善了整体系统表现。

Abstract: Research on mobile manipulation systems that physically interact with humans has expanded rapidly in recent years, opening the way to tasks which could not be performed using fixed-base manipulators. Within this context, developing suitable control methodologies is essential since mobile manipulators introduce additional degrees of freedom, making the design of control approaches more challenging and more prone to performance optimization. This paper proposes a control approach for a mobile manipulator, composed of a mobile base equipped with a robotic arm mounted on the top, with the objective of minimizing the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body mobile manipulator in physical human-robot interaction applications. The approach is experimentally tested with reference to a peg-in-hole task, and the results demonstrate that the proposed approach reduces the overall kinetic energy stored in the whole-body robotic system and improves the system performance compared with the benchmark method.

</details>


### [58] [COIN: Collaborative Interaction-Aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Self-Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24931)
*Yifeng Zhang,Jieming Chen,Tingguang Zhou,Tanishq Duhan,Jianghong Dong,Yuhong Cao,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COIN是一个用于多智能体自动驾驶系统的协作交互感知MARL框架，通过CIG-TD3算法和双级交互感知中心化评论家架构，在密集城市交通环境中实现了安全高效的协作驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体自动驾驶系统能够协调自动驾驶车辆以减少拥堵、提升安全和运行效率，但如何在密集场景中实现高效安全的协作仍然是一个重大挑战。现有的多智能体强化学习方法在动态MASD系统中处理复杂智能体交互时存在困难。

Method: 提出了COIN框架，包含：1) CIG-TD3算法，采用集中训练分散执行方式，联合优化个体导航目标和全局协作目标；2) 双级交互感知中心化评论家架构，捕捉局部成对交互和全局系统级依赖关系，实现更准确的全局价值估计和信用分配。

Result: 在密集城市交通环境中的大量仿真实验表明，COIN在各种系统规模下，在安全和效率方面始终优于其他先进基线方法。通过真实世界机器人演示进一步验证了其在复杂动态MASD场景中的优越性。

Conclusion: COIN框架通过创新的CIG-TD3算法和双级交互感知架构，有效解决了密集多智能体自动驾驶系统中的协作挑战，在安全性和效率方面表现出色，为未来智能交通系统提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Self-Driving (MASD) systems provide an effective solution for coordinating autonomous vehicles to reduce congestion and enhance both safety and operational efficiency in future intelligent transportation systems. Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has emerged as a promising approach for developing advanced end-to-end MASD systems. However, achieving efficient and safe collaboration in dynamic MASD systems remains a significant challenge in dense scenarios with complex agent interactions. To address this challenge, we propose a novel collaborative(CO-) interaction-aware(-IN) MARL framework, named COIN. Specifically, we develop a new counterfactual individual-global twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient (CIG-TD3) algorithm, crafted in a "centralized training, decentralized execution" (CTDE) manner, which aims to jointly optimize the individual objectives (navigation) and the global objectives (collaboration) of agents. We further introduce a dual-level interaction-aware centralized critic architecture that captures both local pairwise interactions and global system-level dependencies, enabling more accurate global value estimation and improved credit assignment for collaborative policy learning. We conduct extensive simulation experiments in dense urban traffic environments, which demonstrate that COIN consistently outperforms other advanced baseline methods in both safety and efficiency across various system sizes. These results highlight its superiority in complex and dynamic MASD scenarios, as further validated through real-world robot demonstrations. Supplementary videos are available at https://marmotlab.github.io/COIN/

</details>


### [59] [SABER: A Stealthy Agentic Black-Box Attack Framework for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24935)
*Xiyang Wu,Guangyao Shi,Qingzi Wang,Zongxia Li,Amrit Singh Bedi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SABER：一种基于智能体的黑盒攻击方法，通过生成最小但有效的指令编辑来评估VLA模型的鲁棒性，在有限编辑预算下显著降低机器人任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型使机器人能够遵循基于视觉观察的自然语言指令，但指令通道也引入了关键漏洞：微小的文本扰动可能改变下游机器人行为。系统鲁棒性评估需要一个黑盒攻击器，能够为不同VLA模型生成最小但有效的指令编辑。

Method: SABER采用以智能体为中心的方法，使用GRPO训练的ReAct攻击器，在有限编辑预算下通过字符级、词元级和提示级工具生成小而合理的对抗性指令编辑，诱导目标行为退化（任务失败、执行时间过长、约束违反增加）。

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，针对六个最先进的VLA模型，SABER将任务成功率降低20.6%，动作序列长度增加55%，约束违反率提高33%，同时比GPT基线减少21.1%的工具调用和54.7%的字符编辑。

Conclusion: 小而合理的指令编辑足以显著降低机器人执行性能，基于智能体的黑盒管道为红队测试机器人基础模型提供了实用、可扩展和自适应的方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to follow natural-language instructions grounded in visual observations, but the instruction channel also introduces a critical vulnerability: small textual perturbations can alter downstream robot behavior. Systematic robustness evaluation therefore requires a black-box attacker that can generate minimal yet effective instruction edits across diverse VLA models. To this end, we present SABER, an agent-centric approach for automatically generating instruction-based adversarial attacks on VLA models under bounded edit budgets. SABER uses a GRPO-trained ReAct attacker to generate small, plausible adversarial instruction edits using character-, token-, and prompt-level tools under a bounded edit budget that induces targeted behavioral degradation, including task failure, unnecessarily long execution, and increased constraint violations. On the LIBERO benchmark across six state-of-the-art VLA models, SABER reduces task success by 20.6%, increases action-sequence length by 55%, and raises constraint violations by 33%, while requiring 21.1% fewer tool calls and 54.7% fewer character edits than strong GPT-based baselines. These results show that small, plausible instruction edits are sufficient to substantially degrade robot execution, and that an agentic black-box pipeline offers a practical, scalable, and adaptive approach for red-teaming robotic foundation models.

</details>


### [60] [Learning Rollout from Sampling:An R1-Style Tokenized Traffic Simulation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24989)
*Ziyan Wang,Peng Chen,Ding Li,Chiwei Li,Qichao Zhang,Zhongpu Xia,Guizhen Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R1Sim：基于运动令牌熵模式的强化学习交通仿真方法，通过熵引导自适应采样和群体相对策略优化实现平衡的探索-利用权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于下一令牌预测（NTP）范式的交通仿真方法限制了在次优区域对潜在有价值运动令牌的主动探索，而熵模式为基于运动令牌不确定性的探索提供了有前景的视角

Method: 提出R1Sim令牌化交通仿真策略：1）引入熵引导自适应采样机制，关注高不确定性但高潜力的运动令牌；2）使用群体相对策略优化（GRPO）优化运动行为，配合安全感知奖励设计；3）通过多样化高不确定性采样和群体比较估计实现探索-利用平衡

Result: 在Waymo Sim Agent基准测试上的广泛实验表明，R1Sim相比最先进方法取得了有竞争力的性能，能够生成真实、安全且多样化的多智能体行为

Conclusion: R1Sim是基于运动令牌熵模式的强化学习在交通仿真中的首次尝试，通过熵引导采样和GRPO优化实现了更好的探索-利用平衡，为交通仿真提供了新的有效方法

Abstract: Learning diverse and high-fidelity traffic simulations from human driving demonstrations is crucial for autonomous driving evaluation. The recent next-token prediction (NTP) paradigm, widely adopted in large language models (LLMs), has been applied to traffic simulation and achieves iterative improvements via supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, such methods limit active exploration of potentially valuable motion tokens, particularly in suboptimal regions. Entropy patterns provide a promising perspective for enabling exploration driven by motion token uncertainty. Motivated by this insight, we propose a novel tokenized traffic simulation policy, R1Sim, which represents an initial attempt to explore reinforcement learning based on motion token entropy patterns, and systematically analyzes the impact of different motion tokens on simulation outcomes. Specifically, we introduce an entropy-guided adaptive sampling mechanism that focuses on previously overlooked motion tokens with high uncertainty yet high potential. We further optimize motion behaviors using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), guided by a safety-aware reward design. Overall, these components enable a balanced exploration-exploitation trade-off through diverse high-uncertainty sampling and group-wise comparative estimation, resulting in realistic, safe, and diverse multi-agent behaviors. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Sim Agent benchmark demonstrate that R1Sim achieves competitive performance compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [61] [UMBRELLA: Uncertainty-aware Multi-robot Reactive Coordination under Dynamic Temporal Logic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25395)
*Qisheng Zhao,Meng Guo,Hengxuan Du,Lars Lindemann,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人协作执行动态移动目标任务的规划框架，结合保形预测处理目标运动不确定性，使用MCTS搜索和CVaR优化目标，实现平均完工时间减少23%和方差减少71%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人系统方法通常假设静态任务特征或在环境变化时简单重新规划，无法有效处理动态移动目标的协作任务。需要解决目标运动预测不确定性和时空约束同时满足的挑战。

Method: 提出UMBRELLA框架：1) 使用保形预测(CP)显式建模目标运动预测不确定性；2) 结合线性时序逻辑(LTL)处理时空约束；3) 采用蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)在部分计划上进行搜索，结合不确定性感知的rollout；4) 引入CP-based指标引导和加速搜索；5) 以最小化平均完工时间的条件风险价值(CVaR)为目标；6) 对于在线任务，采用滚动时域规划方案动态调整分配。

Result: 大规模仿真和硬件实验表明，相比静态基线方法，平均完工时间减少23%，方差减少71%。框架始终确保任务间的时空约束，在线执行期间仅需部分同步协作任务。

Conclusion: UMBRELLA框架有效解决了多机器人协作处理动态移动目标任务的挑战，通过不确定性建模、时空约束满足和优化搜索策略，显著提升了系统性能，为动态环境下的多机器人协作提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can be extremely efficient for accomplishing team-wise tasks by acting concurrently and collaboratively. However, most existing methods either assume static task features or simply replan when environmental changes occur. This paper addresses the challenging problem of coordinating multi-robot systems for collaborative tasks involving dynamic and moving targets. We explicitly model the uncertainty in target motion prediction via Conformal Prediction(CP), while respecting the spatial-temporal constraints specified by Linear Temporal Logic (LTL). The proposed framework (UMBRELLA) combines the Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over partial plans with uncertainty-aware rollouts, and introduces a CP-based metric to guide and accelerate the search. The objective is to minimize the Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) of the average makespan. For tasks released online, a receding-horizon planning scheme dynamically adjusts the assignments based on updated task specifications and motion predictions. Spatial and temporal constraints among the tasks are always ensured, and only partial synchronization is required for the collaborative tasks during online execution. Extensive large-scale simulations and hardware experiments demonstrate substantial reductions in both the average makespan and its variance by 23% and 71%, compared with static baselines.

</details>


### [62] [$π$, But Make It Fly: Physics-Guided Transfer of VLA Models to Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25038)
*Johnathan Tucker,Denis Liu,Aiden Swann,Allen Ren,Javier Yu,Jiankai Sun,Brandon Kim,Lachlain McGranahan,Quan Vuong,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AirVLA系统将预训练的视觉-语言-动作模型从固定基座机械臂迁移到空中平台，通过载荷感知引导和Gaussian Splatting合成数据解决动力学差异和数据稀缺问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然VLA模型在固定基座机械臂上表现出色，但将其迁移到空中平台面临挑战，因为固定基座机械臂的准静态动力学与飞行器的欠驱动、高动态特性存在根本性不匹配

Method: 引入载荷感知引导机制，将载荷约束直接注入策略的流匹配采样过程；使用Gaussian Splatting管道合成导航训练数据以克服数据稀缺问题

Result: 合成数据使导航任务成功率从81%提升至100%；载荷感知引导将真实世界抓取放置任务成功率从23%提升至50%；在长时域组合任务中达到62%总体成功率

Conclusion: 预训练的机械臂VLA模型通过适当的数据增强和物理信息引导，可以迁移到空中操纵和导航任务，以及这些任务的组合

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models such as $π_0$ have demonstrated remarkable generalization across diverse fixed-base manipulators. However, transferring these foundation models to aerial platforms remains an open challenge due to the fundamental mismatch between the quasi-static dynamics of fixed-base arms and the underactuated, highly dynamic nature of flight. In this work, we introduce AirVLA, a system that investigates the transferability of manipulation-pretrained VLAs to aerial pick-and-place tasks. We find that while visual representations transfer effectively, the specific control dynamics required for flight do not. To bridge this "dynamics gap" without retraining the foundation model, we introduce a Payload-Aware Guidance mechanism that injects payload constraints directly into the policy's flow-matching sampling process. To overcome data scarcity, we further utilize a Gaussian Splatting pipeline to synthesize navigation training data. We evaluate our method through a cumulative 460 real-world experiments which demonstrate that this synthetic data is a key enabler of performance, unlocking 100% success in navigation tasks where directly fine-tuning on teleoperation data alone attains 81% success. Our inference-time intervention, Payload-Aware Guidance, increases real-world pick-and-place task success from 23% to 50%. Finally, we evaluate the model on a long-horizon compositional task, achieving a 62% overall success rate. These results suggest that pre-trained manipulation VLAs, with appropriate data augmentation and physics-informed guidance, can transfer to aerial manipulation and navigation, as well as the composition of these tasks.

</details>


### [63] [ThermoAct:Thermal-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models for Robotic Perception and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25044)
*Young-Chae Son,Dae-Kwan Ko,Yoon-Ji Choi,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合热信息的视觉-语言-动作框架，用于机器人任务执行，通过VLM作为高级规划器解析自然语言指令并分解为子任务，相比传统纯视觉方法能感知物理属性并提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作环境中，现有研究多关注视觉信息，而热数据对于提升机器人安全性和操作效率至关重要但被相对忽视，需要整合多模态传感器数据以实现更安全智能的任务执行。

Method: 提出新颖的视觉-语言-动作框架，集成热信息用于机器人任务执行。利用视觉语言模型作为高级规划器解析复杂自然语言指令并分解为简单子任务，实现高效数据收集和复杂操作的鲁棒推理。与传统纯视觉方法不同，本方法融合热信息使机器人能感知物理属性并主动确保环境安全。

Result: 真实世界任务场景的实验结果验证了所提框架的可行性，表明相比现有视觉系统，该方法有潜力提高任务成功率和安全性。

Conclusion: 融合热信息的视觉-语言-动作框架能有效提升机器人任务执行的安全性和智能性，为多模态传感器数据在人机协作中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: In recent human-robot collaboration environments, there is a growing focus on integrating diverse sensor data beyond visual information to enable safer and more intelligent task execution. Although thermal data can be crucial for enhancing robot safety and operational efficiency, its integration has been relatively overlooked in prior research. This paper proposes a novel Vision-Language-Action (VLA) framework that incorporates thermal information for robot task execution. The proposed system leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as a high-level planner to interpret complex natural language commands and decompose them into simpler sub-tasks. This approach facilitates efficient data collection and robust reasoning for complex operations. Unlike conventional methods that rely solely on visual data, our approach integrates thermal information, enabling the robot to perceive physical properties and proactively ensure environmental safety. Experimental results from real-world task scenarios validate the feasibility of our proposed framework, suggesting its potential to enhance task success rates and safety compared to existing vision-based systems.

</details>


### [64] [Drive My Way: Preference Alignment of Vision-Language-Action Model for Personalized Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25740)
*Zehao Wang,Huaide Jiang,Shuaiwu Dong,Yuping Wang,Hang Qiu,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Drive My Way (DMW) 是一个个性化的视觉-语言-动作驾驶框架，通过用户嵌入和自然语言指令来适应长期驾驶习惯和短期意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统要么优化通用目标，要么依赖固定驾驶模式，缺乏适应个体偏好或解释自然语言意图的能力。人类驾驶行为具有个性化特征，受长期习惯和短期意图共同影响。

Method: 提出DMW框架：1) 从多个真实驾驶员收集的个性化驾驶数据集中学习用户嵌入；2) 在规划时基于该嵌入条件化策略；3) 自然语言指令提供额外的短期指导。

Result: 在Bench2Drive基准测试的闭环评估中，DMW改善了风格指令适应能力。用户研究表明，其生成的行为可被识别为每个驾驶员自身的风格。

Conclusion: 个性化是以人为中心的自动驾驶的关键能力，DMW通过结合长期习惯学习和实时指令适应，实现了有效的个性化驾驶行为生成。

Abstract: Human driving behavior is inherently personal, which is shaped by long-term habits and influenced by short-term intentions. Individuals differ in how they accelerate, brake, merge, yield, and overtake across diverse situations. However, existing end-to-end autonomous driving systems either optimize for generic objectives or rely on fixed driving modes, lacking the ability to adapt to individual preferences or interpret natural language intent. To address this gap, we propose Drive My Way (DMW), a personalized Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving framework that aligns with users' long-term driving habits and adapts to real-time user instructions. DMW learns a user embedding from our personalized driving dataset collected across multiple real drivers and conditions the policy on this embedding during planning, while natural language instructions provide additional short-term guidance. Closed-loop evaluation on the Bench2Drive benchmark demonstrates that DMW improves style instruction adaptation, and user studies show that its generated behaviors are recognizable as each driver's own style, highlighting personalization as a key capability for human-centered autonomous driving. Our data and code are available at https://dmw-cvpr.github.io/.

</details>


### [65] [CTS-PLL: A Robust and Anytime Framework for Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25121)
*Junkai Jiang,Yitao Xu,Ruochen Li,Shaobing Xu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CTS-PLL：一个用于协作任务排序和多智能体路径规划的分层框架，通过锁智能体检测与释放机制以及基于大邻域搜索的优化，在密集环境中实现高成功率和解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTS-MAPF问题要求智能体在避免碰撞的同时完成一系列任务，由于其组合复杂性而面临重大挑战。现有方法在密集环境中的鲁棒性和解质量需要改进。

Method: CTS-PLL扩展了基于配置的CTS-MAPF规划范式，包含两个关键增强：1）利用完整规划方法进行局部重规划的锁智能体检测与释放机制；2）基于大邻域搜索（LNS）的随时优化程序。

Result: 在稀疏和密集基准测试中的广泛评估表明，CTS-PLL相比现有方法实现了更高的成功率和解质量，同时保持了有竞争力的运行时间效率。真实机器人实验进一步验证了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: CTS-PLL通过分层框架和优化机制，有效解决了CTS-MAPF问题在密集环境中的鲁棒性和解质量问题，为实际机器人应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The Collaborative Task Sequencing and Multi-Agent Path Finding (CTS-MAPF) problem requires agents to accomplish sequences of tasks while avoiding collisions, posing significant challenges due to its combinatorial complexity. This work introduces CTS-PLL, a hierarchical framework that extends the configuration-based CTS-MAPF planning paradigm with two key enhancements: a lock agents detection and release mechanism leveraging a complete planning method for local re-planning, and an anytime refinement procedure based on Large Neighborhood Search (LNS). These additions ensure robustness in dense environments and enable continuous improvement of solution quality. Extensive evaluations across sparse and dense benchmarks demonstrate that CTS-PLL achieves higher success rates and solution quality compared with existing methods, while maintaining competitive runtime efficiency. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate the feasibility of the approach in practice.

</details>


### [66] [Connectivity-Aware Representations for Constrained Motion Planning via Multi-Scale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25298)
*Suhyun Jeon,Yumin Lim,Woo-Jeong Baek,Hyeonseo Kim,Suhan Park,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于连通性感知表示学习的运动规划方法，通过多尺度流形学习和对比学习框架，在规划前选择位于连通区域的起始和目标构型，避免本质互不连通组件，提高规划成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束运动规划面临三个主要挑战：1) 起始和目标构型可能位于本质互不连通(EMD)组件中，导致规划低效或不可行；2) 约束将可行空间限制到低维子流形；3) 冗余性引入额外复杂性，单个末端执行器姿态对应无限多逆运动学解，可能形成离散的自运动流形。

Method: 提出连通性感知表示学习框架：1) 通过多尺度流形学习将关节构型嵌入到潜在空间，覆盖从局部到全局的邻域范围；2) 通过聚类生成伪标签；3) 使用对比学习框架进行监督学习，获得连通性感知度量；4) 在规划前使用该度量偏置选择位于连通区域的起始和目标构型。

Result: 在各种操作任务上的实验表明，该方法相比基线方法：1) 成功率达到1.9倍；2) 规划时间减少到0.43倍（即减少了57%）。

Conclusion: 通过连通性感知表示学习在规划前选择构型，能够有效避免EMD问题，显著提高约束运动规划的成功率和效率，为解决复杂约束下的运动规划问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The objective of constrained motion planning is to connect start and goal configurations while satisfying task-specific constraints. Motion planning becomes inefficient or infeasible when the configurations lie in disconnected regions, known as essentially mutually disconnected (EMD) components. Constraints further restrict feasible space to a lower-dimensional submanifold, while redundancy introduces additional complexity because a single end-effector pose admits infinitely many inverse kinematic solutions that may form discrete self-motion manifolds. This paper addresses these challenges by learning a connectivity-aware representation for selecting start and goal configurations prior to planning. Joint configurations are embedded into a latent space through multi-scale manifold learning across neighborhood ranges from local to global, and clustering generates pseudo-labels that supervise a contrastive learning framework. The proposed framework provides a connectivity-aware measure that biases the selection of start and goal configurations in connected regions, avoiding EMDs and yielding higher success rates with reduced planning time. Experiments on various manipulation tasks showed that our method achieves 1.9 times higher success rates and reduces the planning time by a factor of 0.43 compared to baselines.

</details>


### [67] [SafeGuard ASF: SR Agentic Humanoid Robot System for Autonomous Industrial Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25353)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Tuan Tran,Ben Wei Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeGuard ASF是一个部署人形机器人进行工业环境自主危险检测的框架，整合多模态感知、ReAct智能体推理框架和Unitree G1人形机器人平台上的学习运动策略，针对火灾烟雾、管道异常温度和入侵者检测三种危险场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无需人工操作的"黑灯工厂"兴起，需要能够检测和响应多种危险类型的自主安全系统。现有系统在工业环境中缺乏综合性的自主危险检测能力。

Method: 整合多模态感知（RGB-D成像）、基于ReAct的智能体推理框架和Unitree G1人形机器人平台上的学习运动策略。使用Unitree RL Lab和PPO算法训练多种运动策略，包括舞蹈运动跟踪和速度控制。提出ToolOrchestra动作框架，通过感知、推理和执行工具实现结构化决策。

Result: 感知流水线在火灾烟雾检测上达到94.2% mAP，延迟127ms。运动策略在80,000次训练迭代内稳定收敛。系统在仿真和真实环境中验证了自主巡逻、视觉感知下的人类检测和避障能力。

Conclusion: SafeGuard ASF框架成功展示了人形机器人在工业环境中进行自主危险检测的可行性，为"黑灯工厂"提供了一种全面的安全解决方案。

Abstract: The rise of unmanned ``dark factories'' operating without human presence demands autonomous safety systems capable of detecting and responding to multiple hazard types. We present SafeGuard ASF (Agentic Security Fleet), a comprehensive framework deploying humanoid robots for autonomous hazard detection in industrial environments. Our system integrates multi-modal perception (RGB-D imaging), a ReAct-based agentic reasoning framework, and learned locomotion policies on the Unitree G1 humanoid platform. We address three critical hazard scenarios: fire and smoke detection, abnormal temperature monitoring in pipelines, and intruder detection in restricted zones. Our perception pipeline achieves 94.2% mAP for fire or smoke detection with 127ms latency. We train multiple locomotion policies, including dance motion tracking and velocity control, using Unitree RL Lab with PPO, demonstrating stable convergence within 80,000 training iterations. We validate our system in both simulation and real-world environments, demonstrating autonomous patrol, human detection with visual perception, and obstacle avoidance capabilities. The proposed ToolOrchestra action framework enables structured decision-making through perception, reasoning, and actuation tools.

</details>


### [68] [A Mentalistic Interface for Probing Folk-Psychological Attribution to Non-Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25646)
*Giulio Pisaneschi,Pierpaolo Serio,Estelle Gerbier,Andrea Dan Ryals,Lorenzo Pollini,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个实验平台，用于研究对非人形机器人的意向状态归因，通过保持行为不变但改变解释框架来探究语言和框架如何影响意向立场的采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言和解释框架如何影响人们对机器人行为的意向状态归因，特别是在非人形机器人场景中，理解意向立场采纳的心理机制。

Method: 构建了一个结合模拟机器人、真实任务环境和基于大语言模型的解释层的实验平台，能够用意向性、目的论或机械论三种不同框架解释相同行为。

Result: 平台提供了一个受控的实验环境，可以在保持机器人行为恒定的情况下，系统性地改变解释框架，从而研究语言和框架对意向立场采纳的影响。

Conclusion: 该平台为研究机器人交互中的意向状态归因提供了有效的实验工具，有助于理解解释框架在塑造人们对机器人行为认知中的关键作用。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental platform for studying intentional-state attribution toward a non-humanoid robot. The system combines a simulated robot, realistic task environments, and large language model-based explanatory layers that can express the same behavior in mentalistic, teleological, or mechanistic terms. By holding behavior constant while varying the explanatory frame, the platform provides a controlled way to investigate how language and framing shape the adoption of the intentional stance in robotics.

</details>


### [69] [Integrating Deep RL and Bayesian Inference for ObjectNav in Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25366)
*João Castelo-Branco,José Santos-Victor,Alexandre Bernardino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合贝叶斯推理与深度强化学习的混合物体搜索框架，在室内环境中实现更高效可靠的物体搜索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决移动机器人在室内环境中自主物体搜索的挑战：部分可观测性、感知不确定性以及探索与导航效率的权衡。传统概率方法依赖人工设计的动作选择启发式，而深度强化学习存在收敛慢和可解释性差的问题

Method: 提出混合物体搜索框架，通过贝叶斯推理维护目标位置的空间信念图，使用校准的物体检测进行在线更新，并训练强化学习策略直接从该概率表示中选择导航动作

Result: 在Habitat 3.0的室内仿真环境中评估，相比基线策略，在两个室内环境中提高了成功率并减少了搜索工作量
