Conclusion: 贝叶斯信念估计与学习动作选择的结合在部分可观测条件下能够实现更高效可靠的物体搜索行为

Abstract: Autonomous object search is challenging for mobile robots operating in indoor environments due to partial observability, perceptual uncertainty, and the need to trade off exploration and navigation efficiency. Classical probabilistic approaches explicitly represent uncertainty but typically rely on handcrafted action-selection heuristics, while deep reinforcement learning enables adaptive policies but often suffers from slow convergence and limited interpretability. This paper proposes a hybrid object-search framework that integrates Bayesian inference with deep reinforcement learning. The method maintains a spatial belief map over target locations, updated online through Bayesian inference from calibrated object detections, and trains a reinforcement learning policy to select navigation actions directly from this probabilistic representation. The approach is evaluated in realistic indoor simulation using Habitat 3.0 and compared against developed baseline strategies. Across two indoor environments, the proposed method improves success rate while reducing search effort. Overall, the results support the value of combining Bayesian belief estimation with learned action selection to achieve more efficient and reliable objectsearch behavior under partial observability.

</details>


### [70] [IntentReact: Guiding Reactive Object-Centric Navigation via Topological Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25382)
*Yanmei Jiao,Anpeng Lu,Wenhan Hu,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang,Huajin Tang,Wen-an Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IntentReact：一种意图条件化的物体中心导航框架，通过低维方向信号（意图）连接全局拓扑规划和局部反应式控制，解决物体目标视觉导航中全局指导与局部感知的脱节问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物体级拓扑地图的方法在长距离导航中面临全局拓扑指导与局部感知驱动控制之间的脱节问题。局部决策仅依赖当前自我中心观察，缺乏视野外信息，导致机器人可能持续朝远离目标的方向移动，无法减少全局拓扑距离。

Method: 提出IntentReact框架，将全局拓扑指导编码为低维方向信号（意图），该意图条件化地指导学习到的路径点预测策略。通过紧凑接口连接全局拓扑规划和反应式物体中心控制，使机器人能够在局部观察误导时及时重新定向。

Result: 通过大量实验评估，相比先前的物体中心导航方法，该框架在导航成功率和执行质量方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: IntentReact通过意图条件化设计有效弥合了全局拓扑指导与局部反应式控制之间的鸿沟，在保持物体中心控制反应性和鲁棒性的同时，引导运动朝减少全局拓扑距离的方向发展。

Abstract: Object-goal visual navigation requires robots to reason over semantic structure and act effectively under partial observability. Recent approaches based on object-level topological maps enable long-horizon navigation without dense geometric reconstruction, but their execution remains limited by the gap between global topological guidance and local perception-driven control. In particular, local decisions are made solely from the current egocentric observation, without access to information beyond the robot's field of view. As a result, the robot may persist along its current heading even when initially oriented away from the goal, moving toward directions that do not decrease the global topological distance. In this work, we propose IntentReact, an intent-conditioned object-centric navigation framework that introduces a compact interface between global topological planning and reactive object-centric control. Our approach encodes global topological guidance as a low-dimensional directional signal, termed intent, which conditions a learned waypoint prediction policy to bias navigation toward topologically consistent progression. This design enables the robot to promptly reorient when local observations are misleading, guiding motion toward directions that decrease global topological distance while preserving the reactivity and robustness of object-centric control. We evaluate the proposed framework through extensive experiments, demonstrating improved navigation success and execution quality compared to prior object-centric navigation methods.

</details>


### [71] [System Design for Maintaining Internal State Consistency in Long-Horizon Robotic Tabletop Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25405)
*Guangyu Zhao,Ceyao Zhang,Chengdong Ma,Tao Wu,Yiyang Song,Haoxuan Ru,Yifan Zhong,Ruilin Yan,Lingfeng Li,Ruochong Li,Yu Li,Xuyuan Han,Yun Ding,Ruizhang Jiang,Xiaochuan Zhang,Yichao Li,Yuanpei Chen,Yaodong Yang,Yitao Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于长时程桌面游戏（以麻将为例）的机器人系统架构，通过显式状态维护、模块分区和恢复机制来保持内部状态一致性，避免错误传播导致交互失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程桌面游戏对机器人系统提出了独特挑战：微小的感知或执行错误会破坏累积的任务状态，在决策模块间传播，最终导致交互失败。需要系统级设计而非孤立组件改进来保持内部状态一致性。

Method: 提出集成架构：1) 显式维护感知、执行和交互状态；2) 将高层语义推理与时间关键的感知控制分离；3) 使用带触觉触发恢复的已验证动作原语防止状态过早损坏；4) 引入交互级监控机制检测回合违规和隐藏信息泄露。

Result: 展示了完整游戏操作，实证分析了故障模式、恢复效果、跨模块错误传播以及部署中观察到的硬件-算法权衡。显式分区、监控状态转换和恢复机制对维持长时间游戏的可执行一致性至关重要。

Conclusion: 显式分区、监控状态转换和恢复机制是维持长时间游戏可执行一致性的关键，而单体或未验证的流水线会导致端到端可靠性显著下降。该系统为研究长时程回合制交互的系统级设计原则提供了实证平台。

Abstract: Long-horizon tabletop games pose a distinct systems challenge for robotics: small perceptual or execution errors can invalidate accumulated task state, propagate across decision-making modules, and ultimately derail interaction. This paper studies how to maintain internal state consistency in turn-based, multi-human robotic tabletop games through deliberate system design rather than isolated component improvement. Using Mahjong as a representative long-horizon setting, we present an integrated architecture that explicitly maintains perceptual, execution, and interaction state, partitions high-level semantic reasoning from time-critical perception and control, and incorporates verified action primitives with tactile-triggered recovery to prevent premature state corruption. We further introduce interaction-level monitoring mechanisms to detect turn violations and hidden-information breaches that threaten execution assumptions. Beyond demonstrating complete-game operation, we provide an empirical characterization of failure modes, recovery effectiveness, cross-module error propagation, and hardware-algorithm trade-offs observed during deployment. Our results show that explicit partitioning, monitored state transitions, and recovery mechanisms are critical for sustaining executable consistency over extended play, whereas monolithic or unverified pipelines lead to measurable degradation in end-to-end reliability. The proposed system serves as an empirical platform for studying system-level design principles in long-horizon, turn-based interaction.

</details>


### [72] [Visualizing Impedance Control in Augmented Reality for Teleoperation: Design and User Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25418)
*Gijs van den Brandt,Femke van Beek,Elena Torta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AR可视化阻抗控制器目标位姿及其与机器人末端执行器位移的方法，为无触觉反馈的遥操作提供力反馈，提升接触丰富的操作性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本、仅有运动控制的遥操作接口缺乏触觉反馈，限制了接触丰富操作任务的性能，需要一种无需昂贵触觉硬件的力反馈方法

Method: 使用增强现实可视化阻抗控制器的目标位姿及其与机器人末端执行器的位移，通过视觉方式传达控制器产生的力，为操作者提供直观的实时反馈

Result: 在17名参与者的双臂操作研究中，AR可视化使力关键提升任务的完成时间减少24%，但对滑动任务（精确力控制较不重要）无显著影响

Conclusion: 通过AR使阻抗目标可见是改善接触丰富遥操作中人机交互的可行方法，无需昂贵触觉硬件即可提供有效的力反馈

Abstract: Teleoperation for contact-rich manipulation remains challenging, especially when using low-cost, motion-only interfaces that provide no haptic feedback. Virtual reality controllers enable intuitive motion control but do not allow operators to directly perceive or regulate contact forces, limiting task performance. To address this, we propose an augmented reality (AR) visualization of the impedance controller's target pose and its displacement from each robot end effector. This visualization conveys the forces generated by the controller, providing operators with intuitive, real-time feedback without expensive haptic hardware. We evaluate the design in a dual-arm manipulation study with 17 participants who repeatedly reposition a box with and without the AR visualization. Results show that AR visualization reduces completion time by 24% for force-critical lifting tasks, with no significant effect on sliding tasks where precise force control is less critical. These findings indicate that making the impedance target visible through AR is a viable approach to improve human-robot interaction for contact-rich teleoperation.

</details>


### [73] [Temporally Decoupled Diffusion Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25462)
*Xiang Li,Bikun Wang,John Zhang,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TDDM提出了一种时间解耦的扩散模型，通过噪声掩码范式将轨迹分割为不同噪声水平的片段，分别处理近期动态约束和远期导航目标，在nuPlan基准测试中达到或超过SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型将轨迹视为整体处理，忽略了异质的时间依赖性：近期规划受瞬时动力学约束，远期规划受导航目标约束。这种单一处理方式无法有效平衡即时安全与长期目标。

Method: 1) 提出时间解耦扩散模型(TDDM)，采用噪声掩码范式，将轨迹分割为不同噪声水平的片段；2) 设计时间解耦自适应层归一化(TD-AdaLN)注入片段特定时间步；3) 推理时使用非对称时间分类器自由引导，利用弱噪声远期先验指导近期路径生成。

Result: 在nuPlan基准测试中，TDDM达到或超过现有最佳基线，特别是在具有挑战性的Test14-hard子集上表现出色。

Conclusion: 通过时间解耦的噪声掩码范式，TDDM能够更好地处理动态城市环境中轨迹规划的异质时间依赖性，平衡即时安全与长期目标，在复杂场景中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic urban environments requires balancing immediate safety with long-term goals. While diffusion models effectively capture multi-modal decision-making, existing approaches treat trajectories as monolithic entities, overlooking heterogeneous temporal dependencies where near-term plans are constrained by instantaneous dynamics and far-term plans by navigational goals. To address this, we propose Temporally Decoupled Diffusion Model (TDDM), which reformulates trajectory generation via a noise-as-mask paradigm. By partitioning trajectories into segments with independent noise levels, we implicitly treat high noise as information voids and weak noise as contextual cues. This compels the model to reconstruct corrupted near-term states by leveraging internal correlations with better-preserved temporal contexts. Architecturally, we introduce a Temporally Decoupled Adaptive Layer Normalization (TD-AdaLN) to inject segment-specific timesteps. During inference, our Asymmetric Temporal Classifier-Free Guidance utilizes weakly noised far-term priors to guide immediate path generation. Evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark show TDDM approaches or exceeds state-of-the-art baselines, particularly excelling in the challenging Test14-hard subset.

</details>


### [74] [Towards Generalizable Robotic Data Flywheel: High-Dimensional Factorization and Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25583)
*Yuyang Xiao,Yifei Zhou,Haoran Wang,Wenxuan Ou,Yuxiao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: F-ACIL是一个启发式因子感知组合迭代学习框架，通过结构化数据分解和组合泛化来解决机器人学习中数据多样性和效率不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通用模型面临的主要瓶颈是缺乏足够多样化的数据以及有限的数据效率，而系统化的数据收集和整理策略尚未得到充分探索。任务多样性源于隐含因素，这些因素稀疏分布在多个维度且难以明确定义。

Method: 提出F-ACIL框架，将数据分布分解为结构化因子空间（如物体、动作、环境）。基于因子化表述，开发因子级数据收集和迭代训练范式，促进高维因子空间上的组合泛化，从而更有效地利用真实世界机器人演示数据。

Result: 通过大量真实世界实验，F-ACIL相比无策略方法，在减少5-10倍演示数据的情况下实现了超过45%的性能提升。结果表明结构化因子分解为真实世界机器人学习中的高效组合泛化提供了实用途径。

Conclusion: 结构化因子分解为机器人学习中的组合泛化提供了有效方法。F-ACIL能够激发更多关于构建可泛化机器人数据飞轮策略的系统性研究。

Abstract: The lack of sufficiently diverse data, coupled with limited data efficiency, remains a major bottleneck for generalist robotic models, yet systematic strategies for collecting and curating such data are not fully explored. Task diversity arises from implicit factors that are sparsely distributed across multiple dimensions and are difficult to define explicitly. To address this challenge, we propose F-ACIL, a heuristic factor-aware compositional iterative learning framework that enables structured data factorization and promotes compositional generalization. F-ACIL decomposes the data distribution into structured factor spaces such as object, action, and environment. Based on the factorized formulation, we develop a factor-wise data collection and an iterative training paradigm that promotes compositional generalization over the high-dimensional factor space, leading to more effective utilization of real-world robotic demonstrations. With extensive real-world experiments, we show that F-ACIL can achieve more than 45% performance gains with 5-10$\times$ fewer demonstrations comparing to that of which without the strategy. The results suggest that structured factorization offers a practical pathway toward efficient compositional generalization in real-world robotic learning. We believe F-ACIL can inspire more systematic research on building generalizable robotic data flywheel strategies. More demonstrations can be found at: https://f-acil.github.io/

</details>


### [75] [Accurate Surface and Reflectance Modelling from 3D Radar Data with Neural Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25623)
*Judith Treffler,Vladimír Kubelka,Henrik Andreasson,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经隐式表示的雷达点云3D建图方法，联合建模场景几何和视角相关的雷达强度，在低可见度环境下实现更平滑准确的表面重建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达在雾、烟、尘等低可见度环境下比相机和激光雷达更具优势，但雷达数据固有的稀疏性和噪声使得可靠的3D表面重建具有挑战性，需要专门的方法来处理这些特性

Method: 采用神经隐式方法进行雷达点云的3D建图，联合建模场景几何和视角相关的雷达强度；使用内存高效的混合特征编码学习连续的有符号距离场(SDF)进行表面重建，同时捕获雷达特定的反射特性

Result: 相比现有激光雷达重建方法应用于雷达数据，该方法能产生更平滑、更准确的3D表面重建，并能重建视角相关的雷达强度；当输入点云变得更稀疏时，神经隐式表示相比传统显式SDF和网格化技术能渲染出更真实的表面

Conclusion: 神经隐式表示特别适合处理稀疏雷达数据，能有效解决雷达点云3D重建的挑战，在低可见度环境下为自主系统提供鲁棒的场景表示

Abstract: Robust scene representation is essential for autonomous systems to safely operate in challenging low-visibility environments. Radar has a clear advantage over cameras and lidars in these conditions due to its resilience to environmental factors such as fog, smoke, or dust. However, radar data is inherently sparse and noisy, making reliable 3D surface reconstruction challenging. To address these challenges, we propose a neural implicit approach for 3D mapping from radar point clouds, which jointly models scene geometry and view-dependent radar intensities. Our method leverages a memory-efficient hybrid feature encoding to learn a continuous Signed Distance Field (SDF) for surface reconstruction, while also capturing radar-specific reflective properties. We show that our approach produces smoother, more accurate 3D surface reconstructions compared to existing lidar-based reconstruction methods applied to radar data, and can reconstruct view-dependent radar intensities. We also show that in general, as input point clouds get sparser, neural implicit representations render more faithful surfaces, compared to traditional explicit SDFs and meshing techniques.

</details>


### [76] [Fast-dVLA: Accelerating Discrete Diffusion VLA to Real-Time Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25661)
*Wenxuan Song,Jiayi Chen,Shuai Chen,Jingbo Wang,Pengxiang Ding,Han Zhao,Yikai Qin,Xinhu Zheng,Donglin Wang,Yan Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦辅助任务训练目标的方法，通过在小规模任务集上使用两种不同训练策略获得能力向量，与预训练参数合并形成能力增强的元模型，结合轻量正交正则化损失，在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的视觉语言动作（VLA）模型在标准监督微调（SFT）中往往难以有效提升性能并降低适应成本。现有的辅助训练方法虽然能提升性能并减少收敛步数，但会因额外损失函数带来显著计算开销。需要同时实现辅助训练的增强能力和标准SFT的简洁性。

Method: 将辅助任务训练的两个目标（增强通用能力和拟合任务特定动作分布）在参数空间解耦。首先在小规模任务集上使用两种不同训练策略使模型收敛，得到的参数差异被解释为辅助任务提供的能力向量。将这些向量与预训练参数合并形成能力增强的元模型。在标准SFT中增加轻量正交正则化损失，使合并模型达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在多种机器人任务中非常有效，能够在减少计算开销的同时达到与辅助微调基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了预训练VLA模型在标准SFT中性能提升有限和适应成本高的问题，通过解耦辅助任务训练目标并合并能力向量，实现了计算效率与性能的平衡，为机器人任务的高效适应提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel approach to address the challenge that pretrained VLA models often fail to effectively improve performance and reduce adaptation costs during standard supervised finetuning (SFT). Some advanced finetuning methods with auxiliary training objectives can improve performance and reduce the number of convergence steps. However, they typically incur significant computational overhead due to the additional losses from auxiliary tasks. To simultaneously achieve the enhanced capabilities of auxiliary training with the simplicity of standard SFT, we decouple the two objectives of auxiliary task training within the parameter space, namely, enhancing general capabilities and fitting task-specific action distributions. To deliver this goal, we only need to train the model to converge on a small-scale task set using two distinct training strategies. The difference between the resulting model parameters can then be interpreted as capability vectors provided by auxiliary tasks. These vectors are then merged with pretrained parameters to form a capability-enhanced meta model. Moreover, when standard SFT is augmented with a lightweight orthogonal regularization loss, the merged model attains performance comparable to auxiliary finetuned baselines with reduced computational overhead. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach is highly effective across diverse robot tasks. Project page: https://chris1220313648.github.io/Fast-dVLA/

</details>


### [77] [Can Users Specify Driving Speed? Bench2Drive-Speed: Benchmark and Baselines for Desired-Speed Conditioned Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25672)
*Yuqian Shao,Xiaosong Jia,Langechuan Liu,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了Bench2Drive-Speed基准测试，用于评估自动驾驶策略在用户指定速度条件下的性能，包括速度遵循和超车指令执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前端到端自动驾驶系统缺乏用户自定义功能，特别是无法让用户指定期望速度或超车/跟随指令，需要建立相关评估基准。

Method: 引入用户期望速度和超车/跟随指令作为模型输入；设计速度遵循分数和超车分数等量化指标；构建CustomizedSpeedDataset数据集（2100个带专家标注的片段）；探索两种监督策略：收集严格遵循速度要求的专家演示，或将常规驾驶数据中未来帧的速度作为目标速度进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，经过适当重新标注后，在常规驾驶数据上训练的模型性能与专家演示数据相当，说明无需额外复杂数据收集即可引入速度监督；目标速度跟随不会降低常规驾驶性能，但执行超车指令仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 建立了首个用于速度条件自动驾驶的基准测试，证明了使用重新标注的常规驾驶数据进行速度监督的可行性，但交互行为（如超车）的执行仍是未来研究挑战。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) has achieved remarkable progress. However, one practical and useful function has been long overlooked: users may wish to customize the desired speed of the policy or specify whether to allow the autonomous vehicle to overtake. To bridge this gap, we present Bench2Drive-Speed, a benchmark with metrics, dataset, and baselines for desired-speed conditioned autonomous driving. We introduce explicit inputs of users' desired target-speed and overtake/follow instructions to driving policy models. We design quantitative metrics, including Speed-Adherence Score and Overtake Score, to measure how faithfully policies follow user specifications, while remaining compatible with standard autonomous driving metrics. To enable training of speed-conditioned policies, one approach is to collect expert demonstrations that strictly follow speed requirements, an expensive and unscalable process in the real world. An alternative is to adapt existing regular driving data by treating the speed observed in future frames as the target speed for training. To investigate this, we construct CustomizedSpeedDataset, composed of 2,100 clips annotated with experts demonstrations, enabling systematic investigation of supervision strategies. Our experiments show that, under proper re-annotation, models trained on regular driving data perform comparably to on expert demonstrations, suggesting that speed supervision can be introduced without additional complex real-world data collection. Furthermore, we find that while target-speed following can be achieved without degrading regular driving performance, executing overtaking commands remains challenging due to the inherent difficulty of interactive behaviors. All code, datasets and baselines are available at https://github.com/Thinklab-SJTU/Bench2Drive-Speed

</details>


### [78] [Persistent Robot World Models: Stabilizing Multi-Step Rollouts via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25685)
*Jai Bardhan,Patrik Drozdik,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种强化学习后训练方案，通过对比RL目标训练世界模型在自回归展开上，结合多视图视觉保真度奖励，显著提升机器人世界模型的长期预测质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于动作条件的机器人世界模型主要针对短期预测优化，在自回归部署时误差会累积导致视觉质量迅速退化，需要解决长期预测的稳定性问题

Method: 1) 引入RL后训练方案，使用对比RL目标在扩散模型上，让模型在自身自回归展开而非真实历史上训练；2) 设计训练协议生成并比较同一展开状态的多个候选变长未来；3) 开发高效的多视图视觉保真度奖励，结合互补感知指标跨相机视图

Result: 在DROID数据集上建立新的最先进水平：LPIPS降低14%（外部相机），SSIM提升9.1%（腕部相机），赢得98%的配对比较，在盲人研究中获得80%偏好率

Conclusion: 通过强化学习后训练方案，显著提升了机器人世界模型在长期自回归展开中的视觉保真度，为解决传统短期预测模型的误差累积问题提供了有效方案

Abstract: Action-conditioned robot world models generate future video frames of the manipulated scene given a robot action sequence, offering a promising alternative for simulating tasks that are difficult to model with traditional physics engines. However, these models are optimized for short-term prediction and break down when deployed autoregressively: each predicted clip feeds back as context for the next, causing errors to compound and visual quality to rapidly degrade. We address this through the following contributions. First, we introduce a reinforcement learning (RL) post-training scheme that trains the world model on its own autoregressive rollouts rather than on ground-truth histories. We achieve this by adapting a recent contrastive RL objective for diffusion models to our setting and show that its convergence guarantees carry over exactly. Second, we design a training protocol that generates and compares multiple candidate variable-length futures from the same rollout state, reinforcing higher-fidelity predictions over lower-fidelity ones. Third, we develop efficient, multi-view visual fidelity rewards that combine complementary perceptual metrics across camera views and are aggregated at the clip level for dense, low-variance training signal. Fourth, we show that our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art for rollout fidelity on the DROID dataset, outperforming the strongest baseline on all metrics (e.g., LPIPS reduced by 14% on external cameras, SSIM improved by 9.1% on the wrist camera), winning 98% of paired comparisons, and achieving an 80% preference rate in a blind human study.

</details>


### [79] [Intelligent Navigation and Obstacle-Aware Fabrication for Mobile Additive Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25688)
*Yifei Li,Ruizhe Fu,Huihang Liu,Guha Manogharan,Feng Ju,Ilya Kovalenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种将导航与材料沉积耦合的通用移动打印配送平台，通过实时控制框架实现动态制造环境中移动增材制造机器人的自主决策，提高制造灵活性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增材制造虽能实现按需定制化生产，但其灵活性受固定设备布局限制。移动机器人集成可解决此问题，但动态制造环境中的干扰（如障碍物、不平坦地形）会影响导航稳定性和打印质量

Method: 开发通用移动打印配送平台，将导航与材料沉积过程耦合；建立实时控制框架，规划和控制机器人导航，确保安全运动、避障和路径稳定性；通过传感、移动和制造的闭环集成提供实时反馈

Result: 通过仿真和真实世界实验验证框架对轨迹变化和外部干扰的适应性；耦合导航与打印使移动增材制造机器人能够规划安全、自适应轨迹，提高制造灵活性和适应性

Conclusion: 所提出的耦合导航与打印方法解决了先前框架将这两个过程分离处理的局限性，使移动增材制造机器人能够在动态环境中自主决策，为大规模定制需求提供更灵活、适应性更强的制造解决方案

Abstract: As the demand for mass customization increases, manufacturing systems must become more flexible and adaptable to produce personalized products efficiently. Additive manufacturing (AM) enhances production adaptability by enabling on-demand fabrication of customized components directly from digital models, but its flexibility remains constrained by fixed equipment layouts. Integrating mobile robots addresses this limitation by allowing manufacturing resources to move and adapt to changing production requirements. Mobile AM Robots (MAMbots) combine AM with mobile robotics to produce and transport components within dynamic manufacturing environments. However, the dynamic manufacturing environments introduce challenges for MAMbots. Disturbances such as obstacles and uneven terrain can disrupt navigation stability, which in turn affects printing accuracy and surface quality. This work proposes a universal mobile printing-and-delivery platform that couples navigation and material deposition, addressing the limitations of earlier frameworks that treated these processes separately. A real-time control framework is developed to plan and control the robot's navigation, ensuring safe motion, obstacle avoidance, and path stability while maintaining print quality. The closed-loop integration of sensing, mobility, and manufacturing provides real-time feedback for motion and process control, enabling MAMbots to make autonomous decisions in dynamic environments. The framework is validated through simulations and real-world experiments that test its adaptability to trajectory variations and external disturbances. Coupled navigation and printing together enable MAMbots to plan safe, adaptive trajectories, improving flexibility and adaptability in manufacturing.

</details>


### [80] [SoftMimicGen: A Data Generation System for Scalable Robot Learning in Deformable Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25725)
*Masoud Moghani,Mahdi Azizian,Animesh Garg,Yuke Zhu,Sean Huver,Ajay Mandlekar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftMimicGen：用于可变形物体操作的自动化数据生成管道，通过高保真仿真环境生成合成数据，减少真实世界数据需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器人数据集收集困难且成本高昂，现有合成数据方法主要局限于刚体任务，而可变形物体操作在真实世界操作中占比很大，需要解决这一关键缺口

Method: 开发SoftMimicGen自动化数据生成管道，创建包含多种可变形物体（毛绒玩具、绳子、纸巾、毛巾）和操作行为（高精度穿线、动态挥动、折叠、拾放）的高保真仿真环境，支持四种机器人形态

Result: 在任务套件中生成数据集，训练出高性能策略，并系统分析了数据生成系统的性能

Conclusion: SoftMimicGen填补了可变形物体操作合成数据生成的空白，为机器人学习提供了可扩展的数据生成解决方案，推动了仿真数据范式在更广泛操作任务中的应用

Abstract: Large-scale robot datasets have facilitated the learning of a wide range of robot manipulation skills, but these datasets remain difficult to collect and scale further, owing to the intractable amount of human time, effort, and cost required. Simulation and synthetic data generation have proven to be an effective alternative to fuel this need for data, especially with the advent of recent work showing that such synthetic datasets can dramatically reduce real-world data requirements and facilitate generalization to novel scenarios unseen in real-world demonstrations. However, this paradigm has been limited to rigid-body tasks, which are easy to simulate. Deformable object manipulation encompasses a large portion of real-world manipulation and remains a crucial gap to address towards increasing adoption of the synthetic simulation data paradigm. In this paper, we introduce SoftMimicGen, an automated data generation pipeline for deformable object manipulation tasks. We introduce a suite of high-fidelity simulation environments that encompasses a wide range of deformable objects (stuffed animal, rope, tissue, towel) and manipulation behaviors (high-precision threading, dynamic whipping, folding, pick-and-place), across four robot embodiments: a single-arm manipulator, bimanual arms, a humanoid, and a surgical robot. We apply SoftMimicGen to generate datasets across the task suite, train high-performing policies from the data, and systematically analyze the data generation system. Project website: \href{https://softmimicgen.github.io}{softmimicgen.github.io}.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [81] [EPAR: Electromagnetic Pathways to Architectural Reliability in Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25671)
*Navnil Choudhury,Yizhuo Tan,Jiaqi Yu,Jakub Szefer,Kanad Basu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: EPAR是一个电磁到架构的框架，通过从物理设计重建有效哈密顿量、中介连接和控制脉冲响应，预测超导量子处理器布局的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着超导处理器规模扩大，需要理解物理布局如何影响量子比特相互作用，现有方法在电磁设计选择如何转化为执行级行为方面提供有限洞察。

Method: EPAR框架直接从物理设计预测鲁棒性，通过重建设计失真如何修改有效哈密顿量、重新路由中介连接并影响控制脉冲响应。

Result: 在所有测试布局中，EPAR的结构分数与双量子比特错误趋势100%一致，但揭示了具有相同校准错误率的边缘之间存在超过10倍的鲁棒性差异。

Conclusion: EPAR超越了传统度量标准，为编译器提供了改进且可操作的指导，能够更早地从物理设计预测架构可靠性。

Abstract: As superconducting processors scale, understanding how physical layout shapes qubit interactions is essential for architectural reliability. Existing methods offer limited insight into how electromagnetic design choices translate into execution-level behavior. We present EPAR, an electromagnetic-to-architecture framework that predicts robustness early directly from physical design by reconstructing how design distortion modifies the effective Hamiltonian, reroutes mediated connectivity, and influences control-pulse response. Across all tested layouts, EPAR's structural scores show 100% agreement with two-qubit error trends yet reveal over 10X robustness differences among edges with identical calibrated error rates, going beyond conventional metrics to provide improved and actionable compiler guidance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [Zero-Cost NDV Estimation from Columnar File Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24606)
*Claude Brisson*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种仅利用现有文件元数据（无需额外存储或数据访问）估计列中不同值数量的方法，适用于Parquet等列式文件格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NDV估计需要扫描数据或维护额外统计信息，成本高昂。本文旨在利用列式文件格式已有的元数据（如字典编码大小、分区统计信息）实现零开销的准确估计。

Method: 结合两种互补信号：1) 通过反解字典编码存储大小方程，在数据均匀分布时提供准确估计；2) 统计行组间不同最小/最大值，结合优惠券收集模型反解，适用于排序或分区数据。通过轻量级分布检测器在两种估计器间路由。

Result: 方法在Apache Parquet格式上验证有效，可推广至任何支持字典编码和分区级统计的格式（如ORC、F3）。实现了无需数据访问的准确NDV估计。

Conclusion: 该方法为查询优化、GPU内存分配和数据剖析等应用提供了高效、零开销的NDV估计方案，充分利用了列式文件格式的现有元数据特性。

Abstract: We present a method for estimating the number of distinct values (NDV) of a column in columnar file formats, using only existing file metadata--no extra storage, no data access. Two complementary signals are exploited: (1)~inverting the dictionary-encoded storage size equation yields accurate NDV estimates when distinct values are well-spread across row groups; (2)~counting distinct min/max values across row groups and inverting a coupon collector model provides robust estimates for sorted or partitioned data. A lightweight distribution detector routes between the two estimators. While demonstrated on Apache Parquet, the technique generalizes to any format with dictionary encoding and partition-level statistics, such as ORC and F3. Applications include cost-based query optimization, GPU memory allocation, and data profiling.

</details>


### [83] [TaCo: Data-adaptive and Query-aware Subspace Collision for High-dimensional Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24919)
*Jiuqi Wei,Zhenyu Liao,Ruoyu Han,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TaCo提出了一种数据自适应和查询感知的子空间碰撞方法，通过子空间导向的数据变换和查询感知策略，解决了传统子空间碰撞框架的数据无关和查询无关问题，显著提升了高维近似最近邻搜索的索引和查询性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子空间碰撞框架存在数据无关和查询无关的局限性，导致索引构建不平衡和查询开销浪费。需要设计数据自适应和查询感知的方法来优化高维近似最近邻搜索的性能。

Method: 1) 设计子空间导向的数据变换机制，通过对变换后数据的每个子空间计算熵的平均值，确保信息论意义上的平衡子空间划分，实现数据自适应子空间碰撞；2) 提出查询感知和可扩展的查询策略，动态分配每个查询的开销，加速子空间内的碰撞探测。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的子空间碰撞方法相比，TaCo实现了高达8倍的索引加速，内存占用减少至0.6倍，查询吞吐量提升超过1.5倍。即使与子空间碰撞范式之外的高级方法相比，TaCo也实现了最先进的索引性能，并在索引和查询效率之间提供了有效平衡。

Conclusion: TaCo通过数据自适应和查询感知的子空间碰撞方法，有效解决了传统子空间碰撞框架的局限性，在高维近似最近邻搜索中实现了优异的索引和查询性能平衡，为相似性搜索提供了新的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) in high-dimensional Euclidean spaces is a fundamental problem with broad applications. Subspace Collision is a newly proposed ANNS framework that provides a novel paradigm for similarity search and achieves superior indexing and query performance. However, the subspace collision framework remains data-agnostic and query-oblivious, resulting in imbalanced index construction and wasted query overhead. In this paper, we address these limitations from two aspects: first, we design a subspace-oriented data transformation mechanism by averaging the entropies computed over each subspace of the transformed data, which ensures balanced subspace partitioning (in an information theoretical sense) and enables data-adaptive subspace collision; second, we present query-aware and scalable query strategies that dynamically allocate overhead for each query and accelerate collision probing within subspaces. Building on these ideas, we propose a novel data-adaptive and query-aware subspace collision method, abbreviated as TaCo, which achieves efficient and accurate ANN search while maintaining an excellent balance between indexing and query performance. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that, when compared to state-of-the-art subspace collision methods, TaCo achieves up to 8x speedup in indexing and reduces to 0.6x memory footprint, while achieving over 1.5x query throughput. Moreover, TaCo achieves state-of-the-art indexing performance and provides an effective balance between indexing and query efficiency, even when compared with advanced methods beyond the subspace-collision paradigm. This paper was published in SIGMOD 2026.

</details>


### [84] [PDET-LSH: Scalable In-Memory Indexing for High-Dimensional Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with Quality Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24920)
*Jiuqi Wei,Xiaodong Lee,Botao Peng,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出DET-LSH和PDET-LSH方法，通过动态编码树改进LSH的索引效率，支持高效范围查询，显著提升索引和查询速度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LSH方法主要关注查询阶段的效率和准确性改进，但对索引阶段效率关注不足。现有方法通常基于数据导向的分区树进行索引，但在高维空间中直接分区多维空间耗时且性能下降

Method: 设计动态编码树(DE-Tree)提高索引效率并支持范围查询；基于DE-Tree提出DET-LSH方案，采用新型查询策略在多个独立DE-Tree中执行范围查询以减少漏检概率；进一步提出PDET-LSH，利用多核CPU并行化机会提升性能

Result: DET-LSH在保持最佳查询准确性的同时，索引时间提升6倍，查询时间提升2倍；PDET-LSH在保持相同查询准确性的同时，索引时间提升40倍，查询时间提升62倍；理论分析证明两种方法在查询准确性上具有概率保证

Conclusion: 提出的DET-LSH和PDET-LSH方法显著改进了LSH的索引效率，特别在大规模数据集上表现出色，同时保持了查询准确性的理论保证，为近似最近邻搜索提供了高效解决方案

Abstract: Locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) is a well-known solution for approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search with theoretical guarantees. Traditional LSH-based methods mainly focus on improving the efficiency and accuracy of query phase by designing different query strategies, but pay little attention to improving the efficiency of the indexing phase. They typically fine-tune existing data-oriented partitioning trees to index data points and support their query strategies. However, their strategy to directly partition the multidimensional space is time-consuming, and performance degrades as the space dimensionality increases. In this paper, we design an encoding-based tree called Dynamic Encoding Tree (DE-Tree) to improve the indexing efficiency and support efficient range queries. Based on DE-Tree, we propose a novel LSH scheme called DET-LSH. DET-LSH adopts a novel query strategy, which performs range queries in multiple independent index DE-Trees to reduce the probability of missing exact NN points. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while achieving best query accuracy, DET-LSH achieves up to 6x speedup in indexing time and 2x speedup in query time over the state-of-the-art LSH-based methods. In addition, to further improve the performance of DET-LSH, we propose PDET-LSH, an in-memory method adopting the parallelization opportunities provided by multicore CPUs. PDET-LSH exhibits considerable advantages in indexing and query efficiency, especially on large-scale datasets. Extensive experiments show that, while achieving the same query accuracy as DET-LSH, PDET-LSH offers up to 40x speedup in indexing time and 62x speedup in query answering time over the state-of-the-art LSH-based methods. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that DET-LSH and PDET-LSH offer probabilistic guarantees on query answering accuracy. This paper was published in TKDE.

</details>


### [85] [Enabling Homomorphic Analytical Operations on Compressed Scientific Data with Multi-stage Decompression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25206)
*Xuan Wu,Sheng Di,Tripti Agarwal,Kai Zhao,Xin Liang,Franck Cappello*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种支持多阶段解压缩和同态分析操作的误差控制有损压缩框架，旨在减少科学数据压缩后的访问延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的误差控制有损压缩器虽然能有效减少科学数据存储和传输压力，但解压缩过程会显著增加数据访问延迟。当分析操作本身耗时远低于解压缩时，这种延迟成为瓶颈。

Method: 设计了一个包含多阶段解压缩和同态分析操作的框架：1) 抽象出具有部分解压缩到多个中间数据表示的通用压缩流水线，并基于现有高性能压缩器实现四个实例；2) 为三类科学数据分析操作精心设计同态算法，支持直接在中间解压缩数据上操作；3) 使用五个真实科学数据集进行评估。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法在执行压缩科学数据的分析操作时，在所有三类目标分析操作类型上都实现了显著的速度提升。

Conclusion: 提出的误差控制有损压缩和分析框架通过多阶段解压缩和同态分析操作，有效减少了压缩科学数据的访问延迟，为科学数据分析提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Error-controlled lossy compressors have been widely used in scientific applications to reduce the unprecedented size of scientific data while keeping data distortion within a user-specified threshold. While they significantly mitigate the pressure for data storage and transmission, they prolong the time to access the data because decompression is required to transform the binary compressed data into meaningful floating-point numbers. This incurs noticeable overhead for common analytical operations on scientific data that extract or derive useful information, because the time cost of the operations could be much lower than that of decompression. In this work, we design an error-controlled lossy compression and analytical framework that features multi-stage decompression and homomorphic analytical operation algorithms on intermediate decompressed data for reduced data access latency. Our contributions are threefold. (1) We abstract a generic compression pipeline with partial decompression to multiple intermediate data representations and implement four instances based on state-of-the-art high-throughput scientific data compressors. (2) We carefully design homomorphic algorithms to enable direct operations on intermediate decompressed data for three types of analytical operations on scientific data. (3) We evaluate our approach using five real-world scientific datasets. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our method achieves significant speedups when performing analytical operations on compressed scientific data across all three targeted analytical operation types.

</details>


### [86] [Are LLMs Overkill for Databases?: A Study on the Finiteness of SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25568)
*Yue Li,David Mimno,Unso Eun Seo Jo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文通过分析376个数据库发现，SQL查询在实际应用中复杂度有限，70%的查询仅需13%的模板类型即可覆盖，表明SQL查询高度可预测且模式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索自然语言转SQL查询的实际复杂度，验证虽然数据库复杂度可以无限增长，但实际查询需求受人类实用需求限制，复杂度有限。

Method: 使用376个数据库样本进行分析，研究SQL查询复杂度与数据库表数量的关系，分析查询模板的频率分布模式。

Result: SQL查询复杂度有限，数据库表数量增加与查询复杂度增加之间没有明确的单调关系。SQL查询模板遵循类幂律分布，70%的查询仅需13%的模板类型即可覆盖。

Conclusion: 在数据库访问领域，LLM代码生成可能操作在狭窄、高度模式化的空间中，使用模板可能更安全、更经济且可审计。

Abstract: Translating natural language to SQL for data retrieval has become more accessible thanks to code generation LLMs. But how hard is it to generate SQL code? While databases can become unbounded in complexity, the complexity of queries is bounded by real life utility and human needs. With a sample of 376 databases, we show that SQL queries, as translations of natural language questions are finite in practical complexity. There is no clear monotonic relationship between increases in database table count and increases in complexity of SQL queries. In their template forms, SQL queries follow a Power Law-like distribution of frequency where 70% of our tested queries can be covered with just 13% of all template types, indicating that the high majority of SQL queries are predictable. This suggests that while LLMs for code generation can be useful, in the domain of database access, they may be operating in a narrow, highly formulaic space where templates could be safer, cheaper, and auditable.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [SolRugDetector: Investigating Rug Pulls on Solana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24625)
*Jiaxin Chen,Ziwei Li,Zigui Jiang,Ruihong He,Yantong Zhou,Jiajing Wu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文对Solana上的Rug Pulls进行了首次系统性实证研究，提出了SolRugDetector检测系统，在2025年上半年新发行的100,063个代币中识别出76,469个欺诈代币，揭示了Solana上Rug Pulls的短生命周期、价格驱动、严重经济损失和有组织群体行为等特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana因其高性能和低交易成本而快速增长，但极低的代币发行门槛导致了广泛的Rug Pulls。与依赖恶意智能合约的以太坊Rug Pulls不同，Solana上统一的SPL Token程序将欺诈行为转向链上操作（如市场操纵）。现有研究尚未对Solana上这些特定的Rug Pull模式进行系统性分析。

Method: 基于68个真实事件报告构建了包含117个确认Rug Pull代币的手动标注数据集，并描述了Solana上Rug Pulls的工作流程。在此基础上提出了SolRugDetector检测系统，仅使用链上交易和状态数据识别欺诈代币。对2025年上半年新发行的100,063个代币进行了大规模测量。

Result: 实验结果显示SolRugDetector在标注数据集上优于现有工具。大规模测量识别出76,469个Rug Pull代币。分析发现Solana上的Rug Pulls具有极短生命周期（平均仅2.3天）、强价格驱动动态、严重经济损失（平均损失率98.7%）和高度有组织的群体行为。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统分析了Solana上的Rug Pulls模式，提出的SolRugDetector能有效检测欺诈代币。研究发现Solana上的Rug Pulls呈现出独特特征，为理解Solana Rug Pull生态提供了重要见解，支持开发有效的链上防御机制。研究发布了标注数据集供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Solana has experienced rapid growth due to its high performance and low transaction costs, but the extremely low barrier to token issuance has also led to widespread Rug Pulls. Unlike Ethereum-based Rug Pulls that rely on malicious smart contracts, the unified SPL Token program on Solana shifts fraudulent behaviors toward on-chain operations such as market manipulation. However, existing research has not yet conducted a systematic analysis of these specific Rug Pull patterns on Solana. In this paper, we present a comprehensive empirical study of Rug Pulls on Solana. Based on 68 real-world incident reports, we construct and release a manually labeled dataset containing 117 confirmed Rug Pull tokens and characterize the workflow of Rug Pulls on Solana. Building on this analysis, we propose SolRugDetector, a detection system that identifies fraudulent tokens solely using on-chain transaction and state data. Experimental results show that SolRugDetector outperforms existing tools on the labeled dataset. We further conduct a large-scale measurement on 100,063 tokens newly issued in the first half of 2025 and identify 76,469 Rug Pull tokens. After validating the in-the-wild detection results, we release this dataset and analyze the Rug Pull ecosystem on Solana. Our analysis reveals that Rug Pulls on Solana exhibit extremely short lifecycles, strong price-driven dynamics, severe economic losses, and highly organized group behaviors. These findings provide insights into the Solana Rug Pull landscape and support the development of effective on-chain defense mechanisms.

</details>


### [88] [Bridging Code Property Graphs and Language Models for Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24837)
*Ahmed Lekssays*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CodeBadger是一个开源MCP服务器，将Joern的代码属性图引擎与LLM集成，解决LLM分析真实代码库安全漏洞时的限制，通过高级工具实现跨函数和文件的语义代码分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在分析真实代码库安全漏洞时面临三个关键挑战：token限制无法加载整个仓库、代码嵌入无法捕获过程间数据流、LLM难以生成复杂的静态分析查询。现有方法只能处理孤立代码片段，无法发现跨多个函数和文件的漏洞。

Method: 开发CodeBadger开源MCP服务器，集成Joern的代码属性图引擎与LLM。提供高级工具包括程序切片、污点跟踪、数据流分析和语义代码导航，使LLM能够有针对性地探索大型代码库而无需完整读取文件。

Result: 通过三个用例证明有效性：(1)在8000个方法的代码库中导航审计内存安全模式；(2)发现并利用libtiff中先前未报告的缓冲区溢出漏洞；(3)首次尝试即为libxml2中的整数溢出漏洞(CVE-2025-6021)生成正确补丁。

Conclusion: CodeBadger使LLM能够在整个代码库范围内进行语义推理，支持大规模漏洞发现、补丁生成和程序理解，解决了现有LLM在代码安全分析中的关键限制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) face critical challenges when analyzing security vulnerabilities in real world codebases: token limits prevent loading entire repositories, code embeddings fail to capture inter procedural data flows, and LLMs struggle to generate complex static analysis queries. These limitations force existing approaches to operate on isolated code snippets, missing vulnerabilities that span multiple functions and files. We introduce codebadger, an open source Model Context Protocol (MCP) server that integrates Joern's Code Property Graph (CPG) engine with LLMs. Rather than requiring LLMs to generate complex CPG queries, codebadger provides high level tools for program slicing, taint tracking, data flow analysis, and semantic code navigation, enabling targeted exploration of large codebases without exhaustive file reading. We demonstrate its effectiveness through three use cases: (1) navigating an 8,000 method codebase to audit memory safety patterns, (2) discovering and exploiting a previously unreported buffer overflow in libtiff, and (3) generating a correct patch for an integer overflow vulnerability (CVE-2025-6021) in libxml2 on the first attempt. codebadger enables LLMs to reason about code semantically across entire repositories, supporting vulnerability discovery, patching, and program comprehension at scale.

</details>


### [89] [AI Security in the Foundation Model Era: A Comprehensive Survey from a Unified Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24857)
*Zhenyi Wang,Siyu Luan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出统一闭环威胁分类法，将机器学习安全威胁按数据与模型间的双向交互分为四类，为分析基础模型安全提供系统性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习安全研究将各类威胁孤立看待，缺乏揭示其共同原理和相互依赖关系的统一框架。数据与模型作为ML两大基础资产已不再独立，一方的漏洞会直接危及另一方，需要系统性理解双向风险在ML流程中的传播机制。

Method: 提出统一闭环威胁分类法，从四个方向轴明确框定模型与数据的交互：数据→数据、数据→模型、模型→数据、模型→模型。该框架为分析基础模型安全提供原则性视角。

Result: 建立了包含四类安全威胁的分类体系：1) D→D：数据解密攻击、水印移除攻击；2) D→M：投毒攻击、有害微调攻击、越狱攻击；3) M→D：模型反演、成员推断攻击、训练数据提取攻击；4) M→M：模型提取攻击。这些类别既相互区别又相互关联。

Conclusion: 统一框架阐明了各类安全威胁之间的内在联系，为开发可扩展、可迁移、跨模态的安全策略奠定了基础，特别是在基础模型的安全防护领域具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: As machine learning (ML) systems expand in both scale and functionality, the security landscape has become increasingly complex, with a proliferation of attacks and defenses. However, existing studies largely treat these threats in isolation, lacking a coherent framework to expose their shared principles and interdependencies. This fragmented view hinders systematic understanding and limits the design of comprehensive defenses. Crucially, the two foundational assets of ML -- \textbf{data} and \textbf{models} -- are no longer independent; vulnerabilities in one directly compromise the other. The absence of a holistic framework leaves open questions about how these bidirectional risks propagate across the ML pipeline. To address this critical gap, we propose a \emph{unified closed-loop threat taxonomy} that explicitly frames model-data interactions along four directional axes. Our framework offers a principled lens for analyzing and defending foundation models. The resulting four classes of security threats represent distinct but interrelated categories of attacks: (1) Data$\rightarrow$Data (D$\rightarrow$D): including \emph{data decryption attacks and watermark removal attacks}; (2) Data$\rightarrow$Model (D$\rightarrow$M): including \emph{poisoning, harmful fine-tuning attacks, and jailbreak attacks}; (3) Model$\rightarrow$Data (M$\rightarrow$D): including \emph{model inversion, membership inference attacks, and training data extraction attacks}; (4) Model$\rightarrow$Model (M$\rightarrow$M): including \emph{model extraction attacks}. Our unified framework elucidates the underlying connections among these security threats and establishes a foundation for developing scalable, transferable, and cross-modal security strategies, particularly within the landscape of foundation models.

</details>


### [90] [Trusted-Execution Environment (TEE) for Solving the Replication Crisis in Academia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24878)
*Jiasun Li,Project Team*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于可信执行环境(TEE)的新框架，通过云TEE执行复制包并提交密码学执行证明，以低成本、可扩展方式解决学术界的复制危机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济学、金融学、社会科学和计算机科学等领域日益严重的复制危机削弱了学术研究的可信度。现有的机构解决方案（如工件评估和复制包）存在合格数据编辑短缺、处理专有数据集困难、流程效率低下以及依赖志愿劳动等关键限制。

Method: 提出利用可信执行环境(TEE)技术（以Intel TDX为例）的新框架：作者在基于云的TEE中执行复制包，并提交正确执行的密码学证明，期刊或会议无需重新运行代码即可高效验证。这重新分配了操作负担给作者，同时保护数据机密性并减少对稀缺编辑资源的依赖。

Result: 通过实地实验验证系统可行性：在TDX支持的云VM上复制已发表论文的试点研究显示，每个复制包平均成本为1.35-1.80美元，相对于标准VM计算开销最小，即使没有TEE经验的新手用户也能获得高成功率。正式分析表明TEE采用对作者具有激励兼容性，对期刊具有成本优势，并在认证市场中构成均衡。

Conclusion: TEE技术有潜力为学术界复制危机提供可持续、隐私保护和高效的解决方案，通过重新分配操作负担、保护数据机密性并减少对稀缺编辑资源的依赖，实现成本效益和可扩展性。

Abstract: The growing replication crisis across disciplines such as economics, finance, and other social sciences as well as computer science undermines the credibility of academic research. Current institutional solutions -- such as artifact evaluations and replication packages -- suffer from critical limitations, including shortages of qualified data editors, difficulties in handling proprietary datasets, inefficient processes, and reliance on voluntary labor. This paper proposes a novel framework leveraging new technological advances in trusted-execution environments (TEEs) -- exemplified by Intel Trust Domain Extensions (TDX) -- to address the replication crisis in a cost-effective and scalable manner. Under our approach, authors execute replication packages within a cloud-based TEE and submit cryptographic proofs of correct execution, for which journals or conferences can efficiently verify without re-running the code. This reallocates the operational burden to authors while preserving data confidentiality and eliminating reliance on scarce editorial resources. As a proof of concept, we validate the feasibility of this system through field experiments, reporting a pilot study replicating published papers on TDX-backed cloud VMs, finding average costs of \$1.35--\$1.80 per package with minimal computational overhead relative to standard VMs and high success rates even for novice users with no prior TEE experience. We also provide a conduct formal analysis showing that TEE adoption is incentive-compatible for authors, cost-dominant for journals, and constitutes an equilibrium in the certification market. The findings highlight the potential of TEE technology to provide a sustainable, privacy-preserving, and efficient mechanism to address the replication crisis in academia.

</details>


### [91] [LiteGuard: Efficient Task-Agnostic Model Fingerprinting with Enhanced Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24982)
*Guang Yang,Ziye Geng,Yihang Chen,Changqing Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LiteGuard：一种高效的任务无关模型指纹框架，通过检查点增强和本地验证器设计，在减少计算成本的同时提升泛化性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的MetaV方法需要大量多样化的模型集进行训练，这在现实场景中面临两个主要问题：1）公开可用模型稀缺，构建大规模模型集需要大量计算资源；2）减少模型数量会导致过拟合，而MetaV的纠缠设计进一步加剧了这一问题，损害了对未见模型的泛化能力

Method: LiteGuard提出两个关键技术：1）基于检查点的模型集增强策略，利用每个盗版模型和独立训练模型训练过程中的中间快照来丰富模型多样性，减少需要训练的实际模型数量；2）本地验证器架构，为每个指纹配备轻量级本地验证器，减少参数纠缠并缓解过拟合

Result: 在五个代表性任务上的广泛实验表明，LiteGuard在泛化性能和计算效率方面均持续优于MetaV

Conclusion: LiteGuard通过创新的模型增强和架构设计，解决了任务无关模型指纹框架在实际部署中的计算成本和泛化性能问题，为模型版权保护提供了更实用的解决方案

Abstract: Task-agnostic model fingerprinting has recently gained increasing attention due to its ability to provide a universal framework applicable across diverse model architectures and tasks. The current state-of-the-art method, MetaV, ensures generalization by jointly training a set of fingerprints and a neural-network-based global verifier using two large and diverse model sets: one composed of pirated models (i.e., the protected model and its variants) and the other comprising independently trained models. However, publicly available models are scarce in many real-world domains, and constructing such model sets requires intensive training and massive computational resources, posing a significant barrier to deployment. Reducing the number of models can alleviate the overhead, but increases the risk of overfitting, a problem further exacerbated by MetaV's entangled design, in which all fingerprints and the global verifier are jointly trained. This overfitting issue compromises the generalization capability for verifying unseen models.
  In this paper, we propose LiteGuard, an efficient task-agnostic fingerprinting framework that attains enhanced generalization while significantly lowering computational cost. Specifically, LiteGuard introduces two key innovations: (i) a checkpoint-based model set augmentation strategy that enriches model diversity by leveraging intermediate model snapshots captured during training of each pirated and independently trained model, thereby alleviating the need to train a large number of such models, and (ii) a local verifier architecture that pairs each fingerprint with a lightweight local verifier, thereby reducing parameter entanglement and mitigating overfitting. Extensive experiments across five representative tasks show that LiteGuard consistently outperforms MetaV in both generalization performance and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [92] [IrisFP: Adversarial-Example-based Model Fingerprinting with Enhanced Uniqueness and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24996)
*Ziye Geng,Guang Yang,Yihang Chen,Changqing Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IrisFP是一种基于对抗样本的模型指纹框架，通过利用多边界特性、多样本行为和指纹判别力评估来生成复合样本指纹，显著提高了指纹的唯一性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型指纹方法通常针对单个决策边界生成指纹，存在唯一性和鲁棒性不足的问题。需要开发一种能够同时提升指纹唯一性和鲁棒性的新方法，以更可靠地进行模型所有权验证。

Method: 1) 将指纹定位在所有决策边界的交点附近，而非单个边界；2) 构建复合样本指纹，每个指纹包含多个靠近多边界交点的样本；3) 使用基于两个参考模型集开发的统计可分性度量评估生成指纹的判别力，保留高判别力指纹并分配指纹特定阈值。

Result: 大量实验表明，IrisFP在所有权验证方面一致优于现有最先进方法，通过同时增强鲁棒性和唯一性实现了可靠的模型所有权验证。

Conclusion: IrisFP通过利用多边界特性、复合样本构造和判别力评估，成功解决了模型指纹的唯一性和鲁棒性平衡问题，为可靠的模型所有权验证提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose IrisFP, a novel adversarial-example-based model fingerprinting framework that enhances both uniqueness and robustness by leveraging multi-boundary characteristics, multi-sample behaviors, and fingerprint discriminative power assessment to generate composite-sample fingerprints. Three key innovations make IrisFP outstanding: 1) It positions fingerprints near the intersection of all decision boundaries - unlike prior methods that target a single boundary - thus increasing the prediction margin without placing fingerprints deep inside target class regions, enhancing both robustness and uniqueness; 2) It constructs composite-sample fingerprints, each comprising multiple samples close to the multi-boundary intersection, to exploit collective behavior patterns and further boost uniqueness; and 3) It assesses the discriminative power of generated fingerprints using statistical separability metrics developed based on two reference model sets, respectively, for pirated and independently-trained models, retains the fingerprints with high discriminative power, and assigns fingerprint-specific thresholds to such retained fingerprints. Extensive experiments show that IrisFP consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving reliable ownership verification by enhancing both robustness and uniqueness.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient ML-DSA Public Key Management Method with Identity for PKI and Its Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25043)
*Penghui Liu,Yi Niu,Xiaoxiong Zhong,Jiahui Wu,Weizhe Zhang,Kaiping Xue,Bin Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于后量子密码的身份基公钥管理框架IPK-pq，解决传统PKI在大规模证书存储、验证和传输方面的局限性，特别针对工业物联网和后量子密码应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工业物联网的快速发展，传统基于证书的PKI在大规模证书存储、验证和频繁传输方面面临挑战，后量子密码的广泛采用将进一步加剧这些问题。

Method: 提出IPK-pq框架，基于NIST ML-DSA和随机矩阵理论实现身份密钥生成协议，在复合公钥概念基础上通过增强的身份映射机制解决线性共谋问题，简化公钥真实性验证。

Result: 提供了IPK-pq的形式化安全证明，涵盖单个私钥组件和复合私钥；在RPKI场景中实现和评估，实验结果表明基于IPK-pq的RPKI系统在效率和可扩展性方面有显著改进。

Conclusion: IPK-pq验证了可行性和合理性，是下一代能够安全管理大规模路由信息的RPKI系统的有力候选方案，有效解决了传统PKI在后量子密码时代的局限性。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of the Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT), the boundaries and scale of the Internet are continuously expanding. Consequently, the limitations of traditional certificate-based Public Key Infrastructure (PKI) have become increasingly evident, particularly in scenarios requiring large-scale certificate storage, verification, and frequent transmission. These challenges are expected to be further amplified by the widespread adoption of post-quantum cryptography. In this paper, we propose a novel identity-based public key management framework for PKI based on post-quantum cryptography, termed \textit{IPK-pq}. This approach implements an identity key generation protocol leveraging NIST ML-DSA and random matrix theory. Building on the concept of the Composite Public Key (CPK), \textit{IPK-pq} addresses the linear collusion problem inherent in CPK through an enhanced identity mapping mechanism. Furthermore, it simplifies the verification of the declared public key's authenticity, effectively reducing the complexity associated with certificate-based key management. We also provide a formal security proof for \textit{IPK-pq}, covering both individual private key components and the composite private key. To validate our approach, formally, we directly implement and evaluate \textit{IPK-pq} within a typical PKI application scenario: Resource PKI (RPKI). Comparative experimental results demonstrate that an RPKI system based on \textit{IPK-pq} yields significant improvements in efficiency and scalability. These results validate the feasibility and rationality of \textit{IPK-pq}, positioning it as a strong candidate for next-generation RPKI systems capable of securely managing large-scale routing information.

</details>


### [94] [PIDP-Attack: Combining Prompt Injection with Database Poisoning Attacks on Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25164)
*Haozhen Wang,Haoyue Liu,Jionghao Zhu,Zhichao Wang,Yongxin Guo,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIDP-Attack是一种新型复合攻击方法，通过将提示注入与数据库投毒相结合，在无需了解用户具体查询的情况下有效操控RAG系统中LLM的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽然能增强LLM的外部知识获取能力，但仍面临对抗攻击的威胁，特别是数据投毒攻击。现有投毒攻击通常需要预先知道用户的具体查询，限制了其灵活性和实际应用性。

Method: 提出PIDP-Attack复合攻击方法：1) 在推理时向查询附加恶意字符（提示注入）；2) 向检索数据库中注入有限数量的投毒段落（数据库投毒）。这种组合使得攻击者无需了解用户实际查询即可操控LLM响应。

Result: 在三个基准数据集（Natural Questions、HotpotQA、MS-MARCO）和八个LLM上的实验评估表明，PIDP-Attack始终优于原始的PoisonedRAG方法。在开放域QA任务上，攻击成功率提高了4%到16%，同时保持高检索精度。

Conclusion: 复合攻击策略对于对抗RAG系统的安全漏洞是必要且高效的。PIDP-Attack通过结合提示注入和数据库投毒，实现了无需先验查询知识的有效攻击，揭示了RAG系统安全防护需要更全面的防御机制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across a wide range of applications. However, their practical deployment is often hindered by issues such as outdated knowledge and the tendency to generate hallucinations. To address these limitations, Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems have been introduced, enhancing LLMs with external, up-to-date knowledge sources. Despite their advantages, RAG systems remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, with data poisoning emerging as a prominent threat. Existing poisoning-based attacks typically require prior knowledge of the user's specific queries, limiting their flexibility and real-world applicability. In this work, we propose PIDP-Attack, a novel compound attack that integrates prompt injection with database poisoning in RAG. By appending malicious characters to queries at inference time and injecting a limited number of poisoned passages into the retrieval database, our method can effectively manipulate LLM response to arbitrary query without prior knowledge of the user's actual query. Experimental evaluations across three benchmark datasets (Natural Questions, HotpotQA, MS-MARCO) and eight LLMs demonstrate that PIDP-Attack consistently outperforms the original PoisonedRAG. Specifically, our method improves attack success rates by 4% to 16% on open-domain QA tasks while maintaining high retrieval precision, proving that the compound attack strategy is both necessary and highly effective.

</details>


### [95] [Usability of Passwordless Authentication in Wi-Fi Networks: A Comparative Study of Passkeys and Passwords in Captive Portals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25290)
*Martiño Rivera-Dourado,Rubén Pérez-Jove,Alejandro Pazos,Jose Vázquez-Naya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过对照实验比较了Wi-Fi热点中passkeys与密码在captive portal环境下的可用性，发现passkeys在登录时感知可用性更高但差异不显著，captive portal限制对用户体验有负面影响，并提出了改进设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Passkeys作为一种无密码认证机制已出现，但其在captive portal（强制门户）环境下的可用性尚未被研究。需要实证比较passkeys与密码在Wi-Fi热点captive portal中的可用性差异。

Method: 采用对照实验室实验，50名参与者，采用分割区设计，覆盖Android和Windows平台，使用实现FIDO2CAP协议的路由器。通过定量和定性方法收集数据，比较passkeys和密码的可用性。

Result: 1. Passkeys在登录时感知可用性高于密码，但差异不显著；2. 无论认证方法如何，captive portal限制都对用户体验产生负面影响并增加错误率；3. Passkeys在两个平台上配置相对容易，但平台特定问题带来显著可用性挑战。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了改进captive portal认证的设计建议：引入无用户名认证流程、改进captive portal检测机制、优化用户界面设计，以提升passkeys在captive portal环境下的可用性。

Abstract: Passkeys have recently emerged as a passwordless authentication mechanism, yet their usability in captive portals remains unexplored. This paper presents an empirical, comparative usability study of passkeys and passwords in a Wi-Fi hotspot using a captive portal. We conducted a controlled laboratory experiment with 50 participants following a split-plot design across Android and Windows platforms, using a router implementing the FIDO2CAP protocol. Our results show a tendency for passkeys to be perceived as more usable than passwords during login, although differences are not statistically significant. Independent of the authentication method, captive portal limitations negatively affected user experience and increased error rates. We further found that passkeys are generally easy to configure on both platforms, but platform-specific issues introduce notable usability challenges. Based on quantitative and qualitative findings, we derive design recommendations to improve captive portal authentication, including the introduction of usernameless authentication flows, improved captive portal detection mechanisms, and user interface design changes.

</details>


### [96] [Physical Backdoor Attack Against Deep Learning-Based Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25304)
*Younes Salmi,Hanna Bogucka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究针对基于深度学习的调制分类器的物理后门攻击，利用功率放大器非线性失真作为物理触发器，在训练时操纵RF信号幅度并改变标签，在推理时通过相同操纵激活后门实现误分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已成为RF信号分类应用的关键技术，但DL模型容易受到对抗性机器学习威胁。现有数字后门攻击在训练数据集中注入数字触发器，而本文研究物理后门攻击，利用物理设备特性创建更隐蔽的触发器。

Method: 提出一种物理后门攻击方法：1）利用功率放大器非线性失真创建物理触发器；2）在训练阶段操纵RF信号幅度并改变其标签为目标调制方案；3）训练出后门模型；4）在推理阶段，通过应用与训练时相同的操纵来激活后门。

Result: 攻击成功率很高，仅需少量操纵的RF信号即可在不同噪声水平下实现有效攻击。测试了多种防御技术的抵抗性，结果显示这些防御技术无法有效缓解该攻击。

Conclusion: 物理后门攻击对基于深度学习的调制分类器构成严重威胁，现有防御技术难以有效应对。该研究揭示了物理层攻击的隐蔽性和危险性，需要开发新的防御机制来保护RF信号分类系统。

Abstract: Deep Learning (DL) has become a key technology that assists radio frequency (RF) signal classification applications, such as modulation classification. However, the DL models are vulnerable to adversarial machine learning threats, such as data manipulation attacks. We study a physical backdoor (Trojan) attack that targets a DL-based modulation classifier. In contrast to digital backdoor attacks, where digital triggers are injected into the training dataset, we use power amplifier (PA) non-linear distortions to create physical triggers before the dataset is formed. During training, the adversary manipulates amplitudes of RF signals and changes their labels to a target modulation scheme, training a backdoored model. At inference, the adversary aims to keep the backdoor attack inactive such that the backdoored model maintains high accuracy on test signals. However, if they apply the same manipulation used during training on these test signals, the backdoor is activated, and the model misclassifies these signals. We demonstrate that our proposed attack achieves high attack success rates with few manipulated RD signals for different noise levels. Furthermore, we test the resilience of the proposed attack to multiple defense techniques, and the results show that these techniques fail to mitigate the attack.

</details>


### [97] [On the Vulnerability of Deep Automatic Modulation Classifiers to Explainable Backdoor Threats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25310)
*Younes Salmi,Hanna Bogucka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究针对无线通信中基于深度学习的自动调制分类系统的物理后门攻击，利用可解释AI指导触发器在信号最脆弱部分的放置，实现高成功率攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在无线通信应用中广泛使用，特别是自动调制分类，但这些模型容易受到对抗性机器学习威胁。现有研究大多关注计算机视觉领域的威胁，而无线通信领域的物理后门攻击研究不足，特别是针对传输前无线信号的攻击

Method: 使用可解释AI（XAI）识别信号中最脆弱的部分来指导触发器放置，结合类别原型和主成分分析生成触发器，针对传输前的无线信号实施物理后门攻击

Result: 该攻击对多种基于深度学习的AMC模型有效，在广泛的信噪比范围内都能实现高成功率，且只需要很小的投毒比例

Conclusion: 无线通信系统中的深度学习模型面临严重的物理后门攻击威胁，需要开发相应的防御机制来保护这些关键系统

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) has been widely studied for assisting applications of modern wireless communications. One of the applications is automatic modulation classification (AMC). However, DL models are found to be vulnerable to adversarial machine learning (AML) threats. One of the most persistent and stealthy threats is the backdoor (Trojan) attack. Nevertheless, most studied threats originate from other AI domains, such as computer vision (CV). Therefore, in this paper, a physical backdoor attack targeting the wireless signal before transmission is studied. The adversary is considered to be using explainable AI (XAI) to guide the placement of the trigger in the most vulnerable parts of the signal. Then, a class prototype combined with principal components is used to generate the trigger. The studied threat was found to be efficient in breaching multiple DL-based AMC models. The attack achieves high success rates for a wide range of SNR values and a small poisoning ratio.

</details>


### [98] [Multi-target Coverage-based Greybox Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25354)
*Masami Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MTCFuzz：针对操作系统与固件协同架构的多目标覆盖率导向灰盒模糊测试方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代系统软件架构通常由固件和操作系统通过定义良好的接口协同工作，但传统模糊测试主要针对单一软件组件，缺乏对协同执行架构的关注

Method: 采用QEMU作为虚拟化环境，在虚拟机内启动系统执行模糊测试，实现跨软件边界的代码覆盖率测量，利用各协同软件组件的代码覆盖率作为反馈

Result: 实现了名为MTCFuzz的多目标覆盖率导向灰盒模糊测试器，评估显示相比传统单目标模糊测试能更深入探索系统

Conclusion: 提出的多目标模糊测试方法能有效探索操作系统与固件协同架构，相比单目标方法具有更好的系统深度探索能力

Abstract: In recent years, fuzzing has been widely applied not only to application software but also to system software, including the Linux kernel and firmware, and has become a powerful technique for vulnerability discovery. Among these approaches, Coverage-based grey-box fuzzing, which utilizes runtime code coverage information, has become the dominant methodology. Conventional fuzzing techniques primarily target a single software component and have paid little attention to cooperative execution with other software. However, modern system software architectures commonly consist of firmware and an operating system that operate cooperatively through well-defined interfaces, such as OpenSBI in the RISC-V architecture and OP-TEE in the ARM architecture. In this study, we investigate fuzzing techniques for architectures in which an operating system and firmware operate cooperatively. In particular, we propose a fuzzing method that enables deeper exploration of the system by leveraging the code coverage of each cooperating software component as feedback, compared to conventional Single-target fuzzing. To observe the execution of the operating system and firmware in a unified manner, our method adopts QEMU as a virtualization environment and executes fuzzing by booting the system within a virtual machine. This enables the measurement of code coverage across software boundaries. Furthermore, we implemented the proposed method as a Multi-target Coverage-based Greybox Fuzzer called MTCFuzz and evaluated its effectiveness.

</details>


### [99] [ALPS: Automated Least-Privilege Enforcement for Securing Serverless Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25393)
*Changhee Shin,Bom Kim,Seungsoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ALPS：基于静态分析和LLM的自动化最小权限框架，用于保护无服务器计算环境安全


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器计算在AI工作负载中日益普及，但其基于函数的架构带来了严重的安全风险，包括过度权限分配和权限管理不善，需要自动化最小权限执行方案

Method: 采用无服务器定制的静态分析从函数代码中提取精确权限需求，使用微调的大型语言模型生成语言和供应商特定的安全策略，并进行实时监控以阻止未授权访问和适应变化

Result: 在AWS、Google Cloud和Azure的8,322个真实函数评估中，ALPS实现了94.8%的最小权限提取覆盖率，安全逻辑生成质量分别提升220%(BLEU)、124%(ChrF++)和100%(ROUGE-2)，性能开销最小

Conclusion: ALPS为保护无服务器工作负载提供了一个有效、实用且供应商无关的解决方案，能够自动化执行最小权限原则

Abstract: Serverless computing is increasingly adopted for AI-driven workloads due to its automatic scaling and pay-as-you-go model. However, its function-based architecture creates significant security risks, including excessive privilege allocation and poor permission management. In this paper, we present ALPS, an automated framework for enforcing least privilege in serverless environments. Our system employs serverless-tailored static analysis to extract precise permission requirements from function code and a fine-tuned Large Language Model (LLM) to generate language- and vendor-specific security policies. It also performs real-time monitoring to block unauthorized access and adapt to policy or code changes, supporting heterogeneous cloud providers and programming languages. In an evaluation of 8,322 real-world functions across AWS, Google Cloud, and Azure, ALPS achieved 94.8\% coverage for least-privilege extraction, improved security logic generation quality by 220\% (BLEU), 124\% (ChrF++) and 100\% (ROUGE-2), and added minimum performance overhead. These results demonstrate that ALPS provides an effective, practical, and vendor-agnostic solution for securing serverless workloads.

</details>


### [100] [Shape and Substance: Dual-Layer Side-Channel Attacks on Local Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25403)
*Eyal Hadad,Mordechai Guri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示动态高分辨率预处理（如AnyRes）在设备端视觉语言模型中引入了算法侧信道漏洞，攻击者可通过执行时间变化和末级缓存争用推断输入图像的几何特征和语义内容，威胁隐私安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端视觉语言模型（VLMs）虽承诺通过本地执行保护数据隐私，但动态高分辨率预处理架构的转变引入了固有的算法侧信道漏洞。研究者发现动态预处理会根据图像宽高比将图像分解为可变数量的补丁，创建与工作负载相关的输入，这为攻击者提供了可乘之机。

Method: 提出双层攻击框架：第一层，无特权攻击者利用标准无特权操作系统指标（执行时间变化）可靠地指纹识别输入图像的几何特征；第二层，通过分析末级缓存（LLC）争用，攻击者可解析相同几何形状内的语义歧义，区分视觉密集内容（如医学X光片）和稀疏内容（如文本文档）。评估了LLaVA-NeXT和Qwen2-VL等最先进模型。

Result: 结合执行时间信号和缓存争用信号，攻击者能够可靠地推断隐私敏感上下文。分析缓解此漏洞的安全工程权衡，揭示恒定工作填充带来的显著性能开销，并提出实用的边缘AI部署安全设计建议。

Conclusion: 动态高分辨率预处理在设备端视觉语言模型中引入了严重的隐私风险，攻击者可通过算法侧信道推断敏感信息。需要重新考虑边缘AI系统的安全设计，平衡性能与隐私保护，并提出实用的安全部署建议。

Abstract: On-device Vision-Language Models (VLMs) promise data privacy via local execution. However, we show that the architectural shift toward Dynamic High-Resolution preprocessing (e.g., AnyRes) introduces an inherent algorithmic side-channel. Unlike static models, dynamic preprocessing decomposes images into a variable number of patches based on their aspect ratio, creating workload-dependent inputs. We demonstrate a dual-layer attack framework against local VLMs. In Tier 1, an unprivileged attacker can exploit significant execution-time variations using standard unprivileged OS metrics to reliably fingerprint the input's geometry. In Tier 2, by profiling Last-Level Cache (LLC) contention, the attacker can resolve semantic ambiguity within identical geometries, distinguishing between visually dense (e.g., medical X-rays) and sparse (e.g., text documents) content. By evaluating state-of-the-art models such as LLaVA-NeXT and Qwen2-VL, we show that combining these signals enables reliable inference of privacy-sensitive contexts. Finally, we analyze the security engineering trade-offs of mitigating this vulnerability, reveal substantial performance overhead with constant-work padding, and propose practical design recommendations for secure Edge AI deployments.

</details>


### [101] [Unveiling the Resilience of LLM-Enhanced Search Engines against Black-Hat SEO Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25500)
*Pei Chen,Geng Hong,Xinyi Wu,Mengying Wu,Zixuan Zhu,Mingxuan Liu,Baojun Liu,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LLM增强搜索引擎对传统SEO攻击有99.78%的防御率，但对新型LLMSEO攻击存在漏洞，重写查询和分段文本攻击可使操纵率翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM增强搜索引擎在提升信息检索效率的同时，其面对黑帽SEO攻击的安全影响尚未被系统研究，需要评估这些新型AI搜索系统对传统和新型攻击的脆弱性。

Method: 构建SEO-Bench基准测试集（包含1000个真实黑帽SEO网站），评估10个代表性LLMSE产品（如ChatGPT、Gemini），提出并测试7种LLMSEO攻击策略，分析检索阶段的安全机制。

Result: LLMSEs能有效防御99.78%的传统SEO攻击，检索阶段是主要过滤层；但现有LLMSEs对LLMSEO攻击存在漏洞，重写查询填充和分段文本攻击可使操纵率相比基线翻倍。

Conclusion: LLM增强搜索引擎生态系统存在安全风险，虽然对传统SEO攻击有强防御，但对专门设计的LLMSEO攻击脆弱，需要更强大的安全机制来构建更具韧性的AI驱动搜索系统。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Language Model-enhanced Search Engines (LLMSEs) has revolutionized information retrieval by integrating web-scale search capabilities with AI-powered summarization. While these systems demonstrate improved efficiency over traditional search engines, their security implications against well-established black-hat Search Engine Optimization (SEO) attacks remain unexplored.
  In this paper, we present the first systematic study of SEO attacks targeting LLMSEs. Specifically, we examine ten representative LLMSE products (e.g., ChatGPT, Gemini) and construct SEO-Bench, a benchmark comprising 1,000 real-world black-hat SEO websites, to evaluate both open- and closed-source LLMSEs. Our measurements show that LLMSEs mitigate over 99.78% of traditional SEO attacks, with the phase of retrieval serving as the primary filter, intercepting the vast majority of malicious queries. We further propose and evaluate seven LLMSEO attack strategies, demonstrating that off-the-shelf LLMSEs are vulnerable to LLMSEO attacks, i.e., rewritten-query stuffing and segmented texts double the manipulation rate compared to the baseline. This work offers the first in-depth security analysis of the LLMSE ecosystem, providing practical insights for building more resilient AI-driven search systems. We have responsibly reported the identified issues to major vendors.

</details>


### [102] [TAAC: A gate into Trustable Audio Affective Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25570)
*Xintao Hu,Feng-Qi Cui*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出TAAC框架，通过对抗损失子空间分解实现可信音频情感计算，在保护用户身份信息的同时进行抑郁症检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频抑郁症诊断面临用户敏感身份信息泄露风险，现有方法难以清晰分离抑郁特征与敏感特征，需要安全加密方法和强大分类器

Method: 基于对抗损失子空间分解的TAAC框架，包含特征子空间分解器(DFSD)、灵活噪声加密器(FNE)和分阶段训练范式，用于特征分解、身份加密和性能提升

Result: 实验表明框架在抑郁症检测、身份信息保留和音频重建方面优于现有加密方法，在不同加密强度下保持稳定，具有保密性、准确性、可追溯性和可调节性

Conclusion: TAAC框架成功解决了音频抑郁症诊断中的隐私保护问题，实现了可信的音频情感计算环境

Abstract: With the emergence of AI techniques for depression diagnosis, the conflict between high demand and limited supply for depression screening has been significantly alleviated. Among various modal data, audio-based depression diagnosis has received increasing attention from both academia and industry since audio is the most common carrier of emotion transmission. Unfortunately, audio data also contains User-sensitive Identity Information (ID), which is extremely vulnerable and may be maliciously used during the smart diagnosis process. Among previous methods, the clarification between depression features and sensitive features has always serve as a barrier. It is also critical to the problem for introducing a safe encryption methodology that only encrypts the sensitive features and a powerful classifier that can correctly diagnose the depression. To track these challenges, by leveraging adversarial loss-based Subspace Decomposition, we propose a first practical framework \name presented for Trustable Audio Affective Computing, to perform automated depression detection through audio within a trustable environment. The key enablers of TAAC are Differentiating Features Subspace Decompositor (DFSD), Flexible Noise Encryptor (FNE) and Staged Training Paradigm, used for decomposition, ID encryption and performance enhancement, respectively. Extensive experiments with existing encryption methods demonstrate our framework's preeminent performance in depression detection, ID reservation and audio reconstruction. Meanwhile, the experiments across various setting demonstrates our model's stability under different encryption strengths. Thus proving our framework's excellence in Confidentiality, Accuracy, Traceability, and Adjustability.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [Sketch2Simulation: Automating Flowsheet Generation via Multi Agent Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24629)
*Abdullah Bahamdan,Emma Pajak,John D. Hedengren,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发了一个端到端多智能体大语言模型系统，能够将过程流程图直接转换为可执行的Aspen HYSYS流程模拟文件，实现了从草图到仿真的自动化工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程流程图转换为可执行仿真模型是过程系统工程中的主要瓶颈，需要大量人工努力和模拟器专业知识。现有的生成式AI方法在工程图解释和LLM辅助流程生成方面有所进展，但这些方法相互脱节：图理解方法通常止于提取图结构，而文本到仿真工作流假设结构化输入而非原始视觉工件。

Method: 提出了一个端到端多智能体大语言模型系统，将任务分解为三个协调层：图解析与解释、仿真模型合成和多级验证。专门智能体处理视觉解释、基于图的中间表示构建、HYSYS COM接口代码生成、执行和结构验证。

Result: 在四个化学工程案例研究（从简单脱盐过程到具有多个循环回路的工业芳香烃生产流程）中评估了框架。系统在所有情况下都生成了可执行的HYSYS模型，在两个较简单案例中实现了完全结构保真度，在更复杂案例中表现良好，连接一致性高于0.93，流一致性高于0.96。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了一个可行的端到端草图到仿真工作流，同时突出了在密集循环结构、隐式图语义和模拟器接口约束方面存在的挑战。

Abstract: Converting process sketches into executable simulation models remains a major bottleneck in process systems engineering, requiring substantial manual effort and simulator-specific expertise. Recent advances in generative AI have improved both engineering-diagram interpretation and LLM-assisted flowsheet generation, but these remain largely disconnected: diagram-understanding methods often stop at extracted graphs, while text-to-simulation workflows assume structured inputs rather than raw visual artifacts. To bridge this gap, we present an end-to-end multi-agent large language model system that converts process diagrams directly into executable Aspen HYSYS flowsheets. The framework decomposes the task into three coordinated layers: diagram parsing and interpretation, simulation model synthesis, and multi-level validation. Specialized agents handle visual interpretation, graph-based intermediate representation construction, code generation for the HYSYS COM interface, execution, and structural verification. We evaluate the framework on four chemical engineering case studies of increasing complexity, from a simple desalting process to an industrial aromatic production flowsheet with multiple recycle loops. The system produces executable HYSYS models in all cases, achieving complete structural fidelity on the two simpler cases and strong performance on the more complex ones, with connection consistency above 0.93 and stream consistency above 0.96. These results demonstrate a viable end-to-end sketch-to-simulation workflow while highlighting remaining challenges in dense recycle structures, implicit diagram semantics, and simulator-interface constraints.

</details>


### [104] [IndustriConnect: MCP Adapters and Mock-First Evaluation for AI-Assisted Industrial Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24703)
*Melwin Xavier,Melveena Jolly,Vaisakh M A,Midhun Xavier*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: INDUSTRICONNECT：一套MCP适配器原型，将工业协议（Modbus、MQTT/Sparkplug B、OPC UA）暴露为AI可发现的工具，支持本地模拟测试，并通过确定性基准测试验证正确性、并发性和错误处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI助手能够分解多步骤工作流，但无法原生支持工业协议（如Modbus、MQTT/Sparkplug B、OPC UA），因此需要一种方法将工业操作暴露为AI可发现和使用的工具，同时保持协议特定的连接性和安全控制。

Method: 开发INDUSTRICONNECT原型套件，包含基于Model Context Protocol（MCP）的适配器，使用通用响应封装和模拟优先工作流（可在本地测试适配器行为后再连接实际设备）。通过确定性基准测试评估适配器，涵盖正常、故障注入、压力和恢复场景。

Result: 基准测试包含870次运行（480次正常、210次故障注入、120次压力、60次恢复试验）和2820次工具调用，覆盖7种故障场景和12种压力场景。正常测试全部成功；故障测试确认了结构化错误处理（适配器级uint16范围验证）；压力测试识别了并发边界；所有三种协议均演示了端点重启后的同会话恢复。

Conclusion: INDUSTRICONNECT为AI辅助工业操作提供了协议适配解决方案，通过系统化测试验证了适配器正确性、并发行为和结构化错误处理能力，为AI与工业系统集成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI assistants can decompose multi-step workflows, but they do not natively speak industrial protocols such as Modbus, MQTT/Sparkplug B, or OPC UA, so this paper presents INDUSTRICONNECT, a prototype suite of Model Context Protocol (MCP) adapters that expose industrial operations as schema-discoverable AI tools while preserving protocol-specific connectivity and safety controls; the system uses a common response envelope and a mock-first workflow so adapter behavior can be exercised locally before connecting to plant equipment, and a deterministic benchmark covering normal, fault-injected, stress, and recovery scenarios evaluates the flagship adapters, comprising 870 runs (480 normal, 210 fault-injected, 120 stress, 60 recovery trials) and 2820 tool calls across 7 fault scenarios and 12 stress scenarios, where the normal suite achieved full success, the fault suite confirmed structured error handling with adapter-level uint16 range validation, the stress suite identified concurrency boundaries, and same-session recovery after endpoint restart is demonstrated for all three protocols, with results providing evidence spanning adapter correctness, concurrency behavior, and structured error handling for AI-assisted industrial operations.

</details>


### [105] [SlopCodeBench: Benchmarking How Coding Agents Degrade Over Long-Horizon Iterative Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24755)
*Gabriel Orlanski,Devjeet Roy,Alexander Yun,Changho Shin,Alex Gu,Albert Ge,Dyah Adila,Frederic Sala,Aws Albarghouthi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SlopCodeBench是一个语言无关的迭代编码基准测试，包含20个问题和93个检查点，用于评估智能体在演化规范下扩展自身代码的能力，发现当前智能体缺乏迭代软件开发所需的设计纪律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码基准测试主要评估单次解决方案，而软件开发本质上是迭代的。代码可能通过测试套件，但随着扩展变得越来越困难。最近的迭代基准测试试图缩小这一差距，但对智能体设计决策限制过严，无法真实衡量代码质量如何影响未来扩展。

Method: 引入SlopCodeBench基准测试，包含20个问题和93个检查点。智能体在演化规范下反复扩展自己的先前解决方案，这些规范强制做出架构决策但不规定内部结构。跟踪两个轨迹级质量信号：冗余度（冗余或重复代码的比例）和结构侵蚀（复杂性质量集中在高复杂度函数中的比例）。

Result: 没有智能体能解决任何问题的端到端解决方案（测试了11个模型）；最高检查点解决率为17.2%。质量持续下降：80%的轨迹中结构侵蚀增加，89.8%的轨迹中冗余度增加。与48个开源Python仓库相比，智能体代码冗余度高出2.2倍，结构侵蚀明显更严重。对20个仓库的跟踪显示，人类代码保持稳定，而智能体代码随每次迭代恶化。提示干预研究表明初始质量可以改善，但无法阻止退化。

Conclusion: 通过率基准测试系统性地低估了扩展鲁棒性，当前智能体缺乏迭代软件开发所需的设计纪律。需要更真实的基准测试来评估代码质量如何随时间演变，以及智能体在长期维护中的表现。

Abstract: Software development is iterative, yet agentic coding benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate single-shot solutions against complete specifications.
