Code can pass the test suite but become progressively harder to extend. Recent iterative benchmarks attempt to close this gap, but constrain the agent's design decisions too tightly to faithfully measure how code quality shapes future extensions. We introduce SlopCodeBench, a language-agnostic benchmark comprising 20 problems and 93 checkpoints, in which agents repeatedly extend their own prior solutions under evolving specifications that force architectural decisions without prescribing internal structure. We track two trajectory-level quality signals: verbosity, the fraction of redundant or duplicated code, and structural erosion, the share of complexity mass concentrated in high-complexity functions. No agent solves any problem end-to-end across 11 models; the highest checkpoint solve rate is 17.2%. Quality degrades steadily: erosion rises in 80% of trajectories and verbosity in 89.8%. Against 48 open-source Python repositories, agent code is 2.2x more verbose and markedly more eroded. Tracking 20 of those repositories over time shows that human code stays flat, while agent code deteriorates with each iteration. A prompt-intervention study shows that initial quality can be improved, but it does not halt degradation. These results demonstrate that pass-rate benchmarks systematically undermeasure extension robustness, and that current agents lack the design discipline iterative software development demands.

</details>


### [106] [Bridging the Gap Between Agility and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24773)
*Eduardo Miranda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MDAX是一种混合管理框架，结合敏捷开发的实证控制与基于里程碑的战略规划，实现方法无关的项目执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统敏捷开发在战略规划方面的不足，同时保留敏捷的灵活性，需要一种既能保持敏捷优势又能实现战略驱动的混合管理方法

Method: 保留敏捷开发的实证控制组件，但通过宏观战略里程碑计划来驱动产品待办事项的优先级排序，实现方法无关的执行机制

Result: MDAX框架允许组织在保持敏捷灵活性的同时，通过里程碑驱动的战略规划来指导项目执行，实现方法选择的自主性

Conclusion: MDAX提供了一种有效的混合管理框架，既保留了敏捷开发的实证控制优势，又通过里程碑驱动的战略规划解决了传统敏捷在长期规划方面的局限性

Abstract: Milestone Driven Agile Execution is a hybrid management framework where the empirical control component of agile development is retained but the prioritization of the backlog is done according to a macro or strategic (milestone) plan that drives the execution of the project.
  MDAX is method agnostic, in the sense that the development approach is not embedded in the execution mechanism but in the plan that drives it. This allows organizations using it to choose the development approach that suites them most,

</details>


### [107] [From Untestable to Testable: Metamorphic Testing in the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24774)
*Valerio Terragni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 讨论测试集成AI和LLM功能软件系统的挑战，提出使用蜕变测试解决LLM不可靠性和标注真值难以扩展的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和LLM功能在软件系统中的集成度越来越高，测试面临重大挑战：LLM虽然强大但不可靠，且用于测试的标注真值难以扩展

Method: 采用蜕变测试方法，通过将多个测试执行之间的关系转化为可执行的测试预言来解决测试预言问题

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体实验结果，但暗示蜕变测试能够有效应对LLM不可靠性和标注数据不足的测试挑战

Conclusion: 蜕变测试为解决集成AI/LLM系统测试问题提供了一种可行方法，通过关系约束而非绝对真值来建立测试预言

Abstract: This article discusses the challenges of testing software systems with increasingly integrated AI and LLM functionalities. LLMs are powerful but unreliable, and labeled ground truth for testing rarely scales. Metamorphic Testing solves this by turning relations among multiple test executions into executable test oracles.

</details>


### [108] [Learning From Developers: Towards Reliable Patch Validation at Scale for Linux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24825)
*Chih-En Lin,Attreyee Mukherjee,Ajay Rawat,Ruqi Zhang,Pedro Fonseca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FLINT是一个用于Linux内核补丁验证的系统框架，通过分析开发者历史讨论自动生成验证规则，帮助扩展补丁审查流程，检测传统工具难以发现的并发和维护性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内存管理子系统补丁审查过程主要依赖人工，尽管有各种自动化检查工具。随着提交量增加，开发者难以跟上审查需求，且严重依赖少数核心开发者。需要一种能扩展补丁审查流程的自动化解决方案。

Method: FLINT采用多阶段方法：1) 从过去10年开发者讨论中提取关键信息；2) 基于历史讨论构建规则分析；3) 使用无需训练或微调的LLM；4) 当需要审查补丁时，检索相关验证规则进行验证；5) 生成易于开发者理解和验证的参考报告。

Result: FLINT在Linux v6.18开发周期中检测到2个新问题，在先前版本中检测到7个问题。在并发错误检测上，FLINT比仅使用LLM的基线提高了21%和14%的真实覆盖率。FLINT的误报率为35%，低于基线。

Conclusion: FLINT框架能有效扩展Linux内核补丁审查流程，通过利用历史开发者讨论自动生成验证规则，显著提高审查效率，特别是对传统工具难以检测的并发和维护性问题，且误报率较低。

Abstract: Patch reviewing is critical for software development, especially in distributed open-source development, which highly depends on voluntary work, such as Linux. This paper studies the past 10 years of patch reviews of the Linux memory management subsystem to characterize the challenges involved in patch reviewing at scale. Our study reveals that the review process is still primarily reliant on human effort despite a wide-range of automatic checking tools. Although kernel developers strive to review all patch proposals, they struggle to keep up with the increasing volume of submissions and depend significantly on a few developers for these reviews.
  To help scale the patch review process, we introduce FLINT, a patch validation system framework that synthesizes insights from past discussions among developers and automatically analyzes patch proposals for compliance. FLINT employs a rule-based analysis informed by past discussions among developers and an LLM that does not require training or fine-tuning on new data, and can continuously improve with minimum human effort. FLINT uses a multi-stage approach to efficiently distill the essential information from past discussions. Later, when a patch proposal needs review, FLINT retrieves the relevant validation rules for validation and generates a reference-backed report that developers can easily interpret and validate. FLINT targets bugs that traditional tools find hard to detect, ranging from maintainability issues, e.g., design choices and naming conventions, to complex concurrency issues, e.g., deadlocks and data races. FLINT detected 2 new issues in Linux v6.18 development cycle and 7 issues in previous versions. FLINT achieves 21% and 14% of higher ground-truth coverage on concurrency bugs than the baseline with LLM only. Moreover, FLINT achieves a 35% false positive rate, which is lower than the baseline.

</details>


### [109] [Data-Oriented Modeling for Spacecraft Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24841)
*Nathan Strange*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将游戏行业的实体-组件-系统架构应用于基于模型的系统工程，通过数据导向方法降低航天器开发复杂性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管发射成本下降，航天器开发成本仍然高昂，主要因为传统MBSE工具采用面向对象编程范式，存在数据与逻辑紧耦合、可变状态、刚性类层次结构等问题，难以与专业分析工具集成

Method: 提出数据导向的MBSE方法，采用游戏行业的实体-组件-系统架构：将设计数据存储为不可变、格式无关的组件，通过模板和容器化工具在持续集成管道中运行无状态分析函数

Result: 开发原型系统VVERDAD，在示例星际任务概念上验证了该方法能够降低部署复杂性、简化测试，同时保留基于文档的系统工程的可追溯性优势

Conclusion: 数据导向原则能够有效解决传统MBSE工具的集成问题，为航天器系统工程提供更灵活、可测试和可追溯的开发框架

Abstract: Spacecraft development costs remain high despite falling launch costs, in part because Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) tools carry the complexity of the object-oriented programming paradigm: tightly coupled data and logic, mutable state, and rigid class hierarchies that resist integration with discipline-specific analysis tools. This paper presents a data-oriented approach to MBSE that adapts the Entity-Component-System (ECS) architecture from the video game industry. Design data is stored as immutable, format-agnostic components in a generic data system; stateless analysis functions operate on this data through templates and containerized tools within a continuous integration pipeline. A prototype implementation, VVERDAD (https://github.com/VisVivaSpace/vverdad-prototype), demonstrates the approach on an example interplanetary mission concept, showing how data-oriented principles can reduce deployment complexity, simplify testing, and preserve the traceability benefits of document-based systems engineering.

</details>


### [110] [Governance in Practice: How Open Source Projects Define and Document Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24879)
*Pedro Oliveira,Tayana Conte,Marco Gerosa,Igor Steinmacher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析开源软件项目中GOVERNANCE.md文件如何通过制度语法定义角色结构，发现角色漂移现象和少数核心成员职责过载问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件可持续性不仅依赖代码贡献，更需要治理结构来明确决策权、行动者和责任分配。目前缺乏关于项目如何通过书面文档正式编码角色和权限的系统性实证证据。

Method: 使用制度语法从GitHub仓库的GOVERNANCE.md及相关文档中提取和形式化角色定义，将每个角色分解为范围、特权、义务和生命周期规则，比较不同社区的治理结构。

Result: 研究发现：1) 开源项目使用稳定的角色头衔集；2) 相同头衔承担不同责任，不同标签描述相似功能（角色漂移）；3) 少数参与者有时积累技术、管理和社区职责，形成治理瓶颈。

Conclusion: 理解开源软件中的权威和责任分配有助于设计更清晰的角色、分配工作并减轻领导负担，从而支持更健康、可持续的社区发展。

Abstract: Open source software (OSS) sustainability depends not only on code contributions but also on governance structures that define who decides, who acts, and how responsibility is distributed. We lack systematic empirical evidence of how projects formally codify roles and authority in written artifacts. This paper investigates how OSS projects define and structure governance through their GOVERNANCE.md files and related documents. We analyze governance as an institutional infrastructure, a set of explicit rules that shape participation, decision rights, and community memory. We used Institutional Grammar to extract and formalize role definitions from repositories hosted on GitHub. We decompose each role into scope, privileges, obligations, and life-cycle rules to compare role structures across communities. Our results show that although OSS projects use a stable set of titles, identical titles carry different responsibilities, and different labels describe similar functions, which we call role drift. Still, we observed that a few actors sometimes accumulate technical, managerial, and community duties. %This creates the Maintainer Paradox: those who enable broad participation simultaneously become governance bottlenecks. By understanding authority and responsibilities in OSS, our findings inform researchers and practitioners on the importance of designing clearer roles, distributing work, and reducing leadership overload to support healthier and more sustainable communities.

</details>


### [111] [MobileDev-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating Language Models on Mobile Application Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24946)
*Moshood A. Fakorede,Krishna Upadhyay,A. B. Siddique,Umar Farooq*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MobileDev-Bench是一个包含384个真实移动应用问题解决任务的基准测试，涵盖Android Native、React Native和Flutter平台，用于评估LLM在移动开发中的修复能力，结果显示当前最先进模型成功率仅3.39%-5.21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注通用库或Web应用，而移动应用开发具有严格的平台约束、框架驱动的生命周期和复杂的平台API交互，这些特性尚未得到充分探索。需要专门针对移动开发场景的基准来评估LLM的实际修复能力。

Method: 从18个生产级移动应用中收集384个真实开发者报告的问题，每个任务包含问题描述和可执行的测试补丁。支持Android Native(Java/Kotlin)、React Native(TypeScript)和Flutter(Dart)三种平台。通过自动化验证模型生成的修复在移动构建环境中的正确性。

Result: 基准测试显示补丁复杂度高：平均每个修复修改12.5个文件和324.9行代码，35.7%的实例需要跨多种工件类型（如源代码和清单文件）的协调更改。评估四个最先进代码LLM（GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini Flash 2.5、Qwen3-Coder）的端到端解决率仅为3.39%-5.21%，远低于先前基准测试表现。

Conclusion: 移动应用修复任务对当前LLM构成重大挑战，多文件和多工件变更的故障定位是主要瓶颈。MobileDev-Bench揭示了移动开发场景的独特复杂性，为未来研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on automated software engineering tasks, yet existing benchmarks focus primarily on general-purpose libraries or web applications, leaving mobile application development largely unexplored despite its strict platform constraints, framework-driven lifecycles, and complex platform API interactions. We introduce MobileDev-Bench, a benchmark comprising 384 real-world issue-resolution tasks collected from 18 production mobile applications spanning Android Native (Java/Kotlin), React Native (TypeScript), and Flutter (Dart). Each task pairs an authentic developer-reported issue with executable test patches, enabling fully automated validation of model-generated fixes within mobile build environments. The benchmark exhibits substantial patch complexity: fixes modify 12.5 files and 324.9 lines on average, and 35.7% of instances require coordinated changes across multiple artifact types, such as source and manifest files. Evaluation of four state-of-the-art code-capable LLMs, GPT- 5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini Flash 2.5, and Qwen3-Coder, yields low end-to-end resolution rates of 3.39%-5.21%, revealing significant performance gaps compared to prior benchmarks. Further analysis reveals systematic failure modes, with fault localization across multi-file and multi-artifact changes emerging as the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [112] [Error Understanding in Program Code With LLM-DL for Multi-label Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25005)
*Md Faizul Ibne Amin,Yutaka Watanobe,Md. Mostafizer Rahman,Daniel M. Muepu,Md. Shahajada Mia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个多标签错误分类框架，利用微调的大型语言模型与深度学习架构结合，对Python代码错误进行多标签分类，CodeT5+_GRU模型表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程是计算机科学和软件工程的核心技能，但代码错误的识别和解决对新手和经验丰富的开发者都具有挑战性。虽然大型语言模型在自然语言任务中表现出色，但在编程错误多标签分类等特定领域复杂场景中的应用潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出多标签错误分类框架，集成微调的LLMs（CodeT5-base、GraphCodeBERT、CodeT5+、UniXcoder、RoBERTa、PLBART、CoTexT）与深度学习架构（GRU、LSTM、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-A），从真实学生编写的Python代码错误数据集中捕获句法和语义特征。使用Optuna进行超参数优化，评估32个模型变体。

Result: CodeT5+_GRU模型表现最佳：加权F1分数0.8243，平均准确率91.84%，精确匹配准确率53.78%，汉明损失0.0816，One-error 0.0708。结果证实了预训练语义编码器与高效循环解码器结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发智能、可扩展的自动代码反馈工具奠定了基础，在编程教育和更广泛的软件工程领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Programming is a core skill in computer science and software engineering (SE), yet identifying and resolving code errors remains challenging for both novice and experienced developers. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in natural language understanding and generation tasks, their potential in domain-specific, complex scenarios, such as multi-label classification (MLC) of programming errors, remains underexplored. Recognizing this less-explored area, this study proposes a multi-label error classification (MLEC) framework for source code that leverages fine-tuned LLMs, including CodeT5-base, GraphCodeBERT, CodeT5+, UniXcoder, RoBERTa, PLBART, and CoTexT. These LLMs are integrated with deep learning (DL) architectures such as GRU, LSTM, BiLSTM, and BiLSTM with an additive attention mechanism (BiLSTM-A) to capture both syntactic and semantic features from a real-world student-written Python code error dataset. Extensive experiments across 32 model variants, optimized using Optuna-based hyperparameter tuning, have been evaluated using comprehensive multi-label metrics, including average accuracy, macro and weighted precision, recall, F1-score, exact match accuracy, One-error, Hamming loss, Jaccard similarity, and ROC-AUC (micro, macro, and weighted). Results show that the CodeT5+\_GRU model achieved the strongest performance, with a weighted F1-score of 0.8243, average accuracy of 91.84\%, exact match accuracy of 53.78\%, Hamming loss of 0.0816, and One error of 0.0708. These findings confirm the effectiveness of combining pretrained semantic encoders with efficient recurrent decoders. This work lays the foundation for developing intelligent, scalable tools for automated code feedback, with potential applications in programming education (PE) and broader SE domains.

</details>


### [113] [Factors Influencing the Quality of AI-Generated Code: A Synthesis of Empirical Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25146)
*Vehid Geruslu,Zulfiyya Aliyeva,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 系统文献综述分析AI生成代码质量的影响因素，发现代码质量受人类因素、AI系统特性和人机交互动态共同影响，需要技术验证和开发流程整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助代码生成工具（如大语言模型）的快速普及，虽然这些工具承诺显著提高生产力，但学术界和工业界对AI生成代码的质量、可靠性和安全性日益关注。本研究旨在系统综合现有关于AI生成源代码质量影响因素的实证证据，并分析这些因素在不同评估背景下如何影响软件质量结果。

Method: 采用遵循既定指南的系统文献综述方法，辅以人工监督的AI辅助工作流程。通过结构化搜索和筛选过程，从主要数字图书馆中选取了24项主要研究。使用基于模式的定性证据综合方法进行数据提取和分析。

Result: 研究发现AI辅助开发中的代码质量受人类因素、AI系统特性和人机交互动态的组合影响。关键影响因素包括提示设计、任务规范和开发者专业知识。结果还显示不同研究在正确性、安全性、可维护性和复杂性等质量结果上存在差异，既有改进也有风险报告。

Conclusion: AI辅助代码生成代表了软件工程中的社会技术转变，实现高质量结果既取决于技术因素也取决于人类因素。尽管前景广阔，但AI生成的代码需要仔细验证并整合到开发工作流程中。

Abstract: Context: The rapid adoption of AI-assisted code generation tools, such as large language models (LLMs), is transforming software development practices. While these tools promise significant productivity gains, concerns regarding the quality, reliability, and security of AI-generated code are increasingly reported in both academia and industry. --Objective: This study aims to systematically synthesize existing empirical evidence on the factors influencing the quality of AI-generated source code and to analyze how these factors impact software quality outcomes across different evaluation contexts. --Method: We conducted a systematic literature review (SLR) following established guidelines, supported by an AI-assisted workflow with human oversight. A total of 24 primary studies were selected through a structured search and screening process across major digital libraries. Data were extracted and analyzed using qualitative, pattern-based evidence synthesis. --Results: The findings reveal that code quality in AI-assisted development is influenced by a combination of human factors, AI system characteristics, and human AI interaction dynamics. Key influencing factors include prompt design, task specification, and developer expertise. The results also show variability in quality outcomes such as correctness, security, maintainability, and complexity across studies, with both improvements and risks reported. --Conclusion: AI-assisted code generation represents a socio-technical shift in software engineering, where achieving high-quality outcomes depends on both technological and human factors. While promising, AI-generated code requires careful validation and integration into development workflows.

</details>


### [114] [LACY: Simulating Expert Mentoring for Software Onboarding with Code Tours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25391)
*Zeynep Begüm Kara,Aytekin İsmail,Ece Ateş,İzgi Nur Tamcı,Zehra İyigün,Selin Şirin Aslangül,Ömercan Devran,Baykal Mehmet Uçar,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Lacy是一个混合人机代码导览系统，通过专家指导的代码导览帮助新人快速理解复杂代码库，结合AI生成内容和专家审核，显著提升学习效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件组织面临新成员入职挑战：需要帮助新人理解复杂代码库、弥补文档不足、理解非自己编写的代码。专家指导虽然有效但成本高、重复性强且难以扩展

Method: 设计Lacy混合人机入职系统，结合AI生成内容和专家审核创建可重用的代码导览。系统包括语音转导览捕获、理解测验、播客和仪表板等功能。通过与Beko的一年行业合作，基于20多次会议、调查和访谈确定需求

Result: 在Beko生产环境部署Lacy，并在遗留金融系统(3万+行代码)进行对照研究。使用专家指导导览的学习者测验得分83% vs AI-only导览57%，更偏好导览而非传统自学，报告需要更少专家咨询。专家认为创建导览比现场指导负担更轻

Conclusion: Lacy成功解决了代码入职的可扩展性问题，Beko已将其用于组织入职。混合人机方法结合了AI效率和专家质量保证，显著提升学习效果并减少专家依赖

Abstract: Every software organization faces the onboarding challenge: helping newcomers navigate complex codebases, compensate for insufficient documentation, and comprehend code they did not author. Expert walkthroughs are among the most effective forms of support, yet they are expensive, repetitive, and do not scale. We present Lacy, a hybrid human-AI onboarding system that captures expert mentoring in reusable code tours-to our knowledge, the first hybrid approach combining AI-generated content with expert curation in code tours. Our design is grounded in requirements derived from 20+ meetings, surveys, and interviews across a year-long industry partnership with Beko. Supporting features include Voice-to-Tour capture, comprehension quizzes, podcasts, and a dashboard. We deployed Lacy on Beko's production environment and conducted a controlled study on a legacy finance system (30K+ LOC). Learners using expert-guided tours achieved 83% quiz scores versus 57% for AI-only tours, preferred tours over traditional self-study, and reported they would need fewer expert consultations. Experts found tour creation less burdensome than live walkthroughs. Beko has since adopted Lacy for organizational onboarding, and we release our code and study instruments as a replication package.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum Circuit Repair by Gate Prioritisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25587)
*Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Thomas Laurent,Paolo Arcaini,Shaukat Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QRep是一种自动修复量子电路故障的方法，通过迭代识别和修复故障，为每个门分配可疑度分数，并优先处理最可疑的门以提高修复效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修复有故障的量子电路具有挑战性，需要自动化解决方案。现有方法在处理大规模复杂电路时存在局限性。

Method: QRep采用迭代修复方法，为电路中的每个门分配可疑度分数，反映其故障可能性。通过在整个电路中统一应用补丁，并优先处理最可疑的门来缩小搜索空间，提高修复效率。

Result: 在40个真实和合成的故障电路上评估，QRep完全修复了70%的电路。对于剩余电路，实际故障门被排在前44%最可疑的门中。相比两个基线方法，QRep能够扩展到更大更复杂的电路（最多13个量子比特）。

Conclusion: QRep在故障定位方面表现出有效性，能够扩展到更大规模的量子电路，为量子电路自动修复提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Repairing faulty quantum circuits is challenging and requires automated solutions. We present QRep, an automated repair approach that iteratively identifies and repairs faults in a circuit. QRep uniformly applies patches across the circuit and assigns each gate a suspiciousness score, reflecting its likelihood of being faulty. It then narrows the search space by prioritising the most suspicious gates in subsequent iterations, increasing the repair efficiency. We evaluated QRep on 40 (real and synthetic) faulty circuits. QRep completely repaired 70% of them, and for the remaining circuits, the actual faulty gate was ranked within the top 44% most suspicious gates, demonstrating the effectiveness of QRep in fault localisation. Compared with two baseline approaches, QRep scales to larger and more complex circuits, up to 13 qubits.

</details>


### [116] [SHAPR: Operationalising Human-AI Collaborative Research Through Structured Knowledge Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25660)
*Ka Ching Chan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SHAPR框架将人类中心决策与AI辅助能力结合，通过结构化、可追溯的知识生成方法实现AI辅助研究实践，支持透明、可复现的研究工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作提出了SHAPR作为概念框架，但缺乏具体实施方法。本文旨在将其操作化为结构化、可追溯、能生成知识的AI辅助研究实践方法，解决AI辅助研究中透明度、可复现性和知识积累的挑战。

Method: 提出一组相互关联的模型：研究活动通过探索-构建-使用-评估-学习的迭代周期组织；工件通过开发和使用演进；经验证据转化为概念知识。核心是结构化知识单元(SKUs)，提供模块化、可重用的实践洞察表示。框架引入证据和可追溯性作为跨领域机制，连接人类决策、AI辅助开发和工件演进。

Result: SHAPR成为AI可执行的研究框架，其结构化流程和文档可由生成式AI系统解释以指导研究工作流。同时支持AI参与的连续性，允许研究人员在不同情境下平衡控制、学习和自动化。框架被概念化为集成研究系统，结合LLM工作空间、开发环境、云存储和版本控制。

Conclusion: SHAPR为在AI辅助环境中进行严谨、透明、可复现的研究提供了实践和理论基础，有助于发展可扩展且方法学健全的研究实践，促进知识积累和系统化改进。

Abstract: SHAPR (Solo Human-Centred and AI-Assisted Practice) is a framework for research software development that integrates human-centred decision-making with AI-assisted capabilities. While prior work introduced SHAPR as a conceptual framework, this paper focuses on its operationalisation as a structured, traceable, and knowledge-generating approach to AI-assisted research practice. We present a set of interconnected models describing how research activities are organised through iterative cycles (Explore-Build-Use-Evaluate-Learn), how artefacts evolve through development and use, and how empirical evidence is transformed into conceptual knowledge. Central to this process are Structured Knowledge Units (SKUs), which provide modular and reusable representations of insights derived from practice, supporting knowledge accumulation across cycles. The framework introduces evidence and traceability as a cross-cutting mechanism linking human decisions, AI-assisted development, and artefact evolution to enable transparency, reproducibility, and systematic refinement. SHAPR is also positioned as an AI-executable research framework, as its structured processes and documentation can be interpreted by generative AI systems to guide research workflows. Simultaneously, SHAPR supports a continuum of AI involvement, allowing researchers to balance control, learning, and automation across different contexts. Beyond individual workflows, SHAPR is conceptualised as an integrated research system combining LLM workspaces, development environments, cloud storage, and version control to support scalable, knowledge-centred research practices. Overall, SHAPR provides a practical and theoretically grounded foundation for conducting rigorous, transparent, and reproducible research in AI-assisted environments, contributing to the development of scalable and methodologically sound research practices.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Examining the Effect of Explanations of AI Privacy Redaction in AI-mediated Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24735)
*Roshni Kaushik,Maarten Sap,Koichi Onoue*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨在AI中介通信中，解释内容删除操作如何影响用户信任，发现提供解释能提升隐私保护感知，且解释效果受上下文和个人差异影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐私敏感领域，AI中介需要平衡透明度和隐私保护。当AI删除或保留信息时，用户如何感知这些干预措施，以及系统行为解释是否能改善信任，是本研究要解决的核心问题。

Method: 设计了一个经过验证的系统，从消息中删除敏感内容，并生成不同详细程度的解释来向接收者传达其决策。然后进行了180名参与者的用户研究，研究用户信任和偏好如何随删除内容量和解释详细程度而变化。

Result: 提供解释时，参与者认为系统在保护隐私方面更有效(p<0.05, Cohen's d≈0.3)。上下文因素有影响：当系统进行大量删除时，参与者更依赖解释并认为其更有帮助(p<0.05, Cohen's f≈0.2)。解释偏好还取决于年龄和AI基础熟悉度等个体差异。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在AI中介通信中平衡透明度和隐私的重要性和挑战，表明自适应、上下文感知的解释对于设计隐私感知、可信赖的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI-mediated communication is increasingly being utilized to help facilitate interactions; however, in privacy sensitive domains, an AI mediator has the additional challenge of considering how to preserve privacy. In these contexts, a mediator may redact or withhold information, raising questions about how users perceive these interventions and whether explanations of system behavior can improve trust. In this work, we investigate how explanations of redaction operations can affect user trust in AI-mediated communication. We devise a scenario where a validated system removes sensitive content from messages and generates explanations of varying detail to communicate its decisions to recipients. We then conduct a user study with $180$ participants that studies how user trust and preferences vary for cases with different amounts of redacted content and different levels of explanation detail. Our results show that participants believed our system was more effective at preserving privacy when explanations were provided ($p<0.05$, Cohen's $d \approx 0.3$). We also found that contextual factors had an impact; participants relied more on explanations and found them more helpful when the system performed extensive redactions ($p<0.05$, Cohen's $f \approx 0.2$). We also found that explanation preferences depended on individual differences as well, and factors such as age and baseline familiarity with AI affected user trust in our system. These findings highlight the importance and challenge of balancing transparency and privacy in AI-mediated communications and suggest that adaptive, context-aware explanations are essential for designing privacy-aware, trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [118] [SABER: Spatial Attention, Brain, Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24830)
*Tom Bullock,Emily Machniak,You-Jin Kim,Radha Kumaran,Justin Kasowski,Apurv Varshney,Julia Ram,Melissa M. Hernandez,Stina Johansson,Neil M. Dundon,Tobias Höllerer,Barry Giesbrecht*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了SABER框架，结合VR和EEG研究3D空间中运动物体的注意机制，验证了传统EEG指标在VR环境中的适用性，并实现了基于脑振荡的注意力位置重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对注意力的神经机制研究主要基于静态2D刺激，缺乏对3D空间中运动物体追踪的神经机制理解。需要开发新方法来研究真实世界中的动态注意力过程。

Method: 开发SABER框架，结合虚拟现实游戏Beat Saber的变体任务，让参与者用虚拟光剑击打静止和运动的彩色目标球，同时记录32名参与者的脑电图数据。使用计算建模方法从振荡脑活动中重建注意力位置。

Result: 1) 验证了传统用于2D静态刺激的单变量EEG指标可有效推广到包含静态和动态3D刺激的沉浸式VR环境；2) 成功从振荡脑活动中重建了静止和运动物体的注意力位置，证明了在3D空间中精确追踪注意力的可行性。

Conclusion: SABER框架验证成功，为未来研究真实世界中的注意力机制提供了基础，同时对开发更好的VR应用具有直接相关性。

Abstract: Tracking moving objects is a critical skill for many everyday tasks, such as crossing a busy street, driving a car or catching a ball. Attention is a key cognitive function that supports object tracking; however, our understanding of the brain mechanisms that support attention is almost exclusively based on evidence from tasks that present stable objects at fixed locations. Accounts of multiple object tracking are also limited because they are largely based on behavioral data alone and involve tracking objects in a 2D plane. Consequently, the neural mechanisms that enable moment-by-moment tracking of goal-relevant objects remain poorly understood. To address this knowledge gap, we developed SABER (Spatial Attention, Brain, Extended Reality), a new framework for studying the behavioral and neural dynamics of attention to objects moving in 3D. Participants (n=32) completed variants of a task inspired by the popular virtual reality (VR) game, Beat Saber, where they used virtual sabers to strike stationary and moving color-defined target spheres while we recorded electroencephalography (EEG). We first established that standard univariate EEG metrics which are typically used to study spatial attention to static objects presented on 2D screens, can generalize effectively to an immersive VR context involving both static and dynamic 3D stimuli. We then used a computational modeling approach to reconstruct moment-by-moment attention to the locations of stationary and moving objects from oscillatory brain activity, demonstrating the feasibility of precisely tracking attention in a 3D space. These results validate SABER, and provide a foundation for future research that is critical not only for understanding how attention works in the physical world, but is also directly relevant to the development of better VR applications.

</details>


### [119] [Gaze patterns predict preference and confidence in pairwise AI image evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24849)
*Nikolas Papadopoulos,Shreenithi Navaneethan,Sheng Bai,Ankur Samanta,Paul Sajda*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 眼动追踪可揭示AI生成图像成对评估中的偏好形成过程，眼动特征能预测选择结果和决策信心水平


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究偏好学习方法（如RLHF和DPO）所依赖的成对人类判断背后的认知过程，探索眼动追踪是否能揭示AI生成图像成对评估中的偏好形成

Method: 30名参与者完成1800次试验，记录其眼动数据，分析注视级联效应、注视特征与选择结果的关系，以及注视转换模式与决策信心的关联

Result: 复制了注视级联效应（决策前约1秒注视转向所选图像）；注视特征能预测二元选择（68%准确率）；注视转换能区分高信心和不确定决策（66%准确率）；低信心试验中图像切换频率更高

Conclusion: 眼动模式能预测成对图像评估中的选择和信心，表明眼动追踪提供了与偏好标注质量相关的隐式信号，有助于理解偏好判断的认知过程

Abstract: Preference learning methods, such as Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), rely on pairwise human judgments, yet little is known about the cognitive processes underlying these judgments. We investigate whether eye-tracking can reveal preference formation during pairwise AI-generated image evaluation. Thirty participants completed 1,800 trials while their gaze was recorded. We replicated the gaze cascade effect, with gaze shifting toward chosen images approximately one second before the decision. Cascade dynamics were consistent across confidence levels. Gaze features predicted binary choice (68% accuracy), with chosen images receiving more dwell time, fixations, and revisits. Gaze transitions distinguished high-confidence from uncertain decisions (66% accuracy), with low-confidence trials showing more image switches per second. These results show that gaze patterns predict both choice and confidence in pairwise image evaluations, suggesting that eye-tracking provides implicit signals relevant to the quality of preference annotations.

</details>


### [120] [Context-Mediated Domain Adaptation in Multi-Agent Sensemaking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24858)
*Anton Wolter,Leon Haag,Vaishali Dhanoa,Niklas Elmqvist*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种上下文中介的领域适应范式，通过用户对AI生成产物的编辑来捕获隐含的领域知识，并重塑多智能体推理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专家拥有难以通过明确规范表达的隐性知识。当专家修改AI生成的产物时，这些编辑揭示了传统基于提示的交互中保持潜在的领域理解。当前系统将这些修改视为终点修正，而非可以重塑后续推理的隐含规范。

Method: 提出上下文中介的领域适应范式，用户对系统生成产物的修改作为隐含领域规范，重塑LLM驱动的多智能体推理行为。通过Seedentia系统（基于Web的多智能体框架）实现生成产物与系统推理之间的双向语义链接。

Result: 从用户修改中提取了46个领域知识条目，证明了通过编辑模式捕获隐性专业知识的可行性。但有限样本量限制了关于系统性质量改进的结论。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了规范引导（模糊初始提示演变为精确领域规范）、隐性知识转移（通过逆向工程用户编辑）和上下文学习（基于观察到的修正模式调整智能体行为），展示了通过人类-AI协作捕获隐性专业知识的潜力。

Abstract: Domain experts possess tacit knowledge that they cannot easily articulate through explicit specifications. When experts modify AI-generated artifacts by correcting terminology, restructuring arguments, and adjusting emphasis, these edits reveal domain understanding that remains latent in traditional prompt-based interactions. Current systems treat such modifications as endpoint corrections rather than as implicit specifications that could reshape subsequent reasoning. We propose context-mediated domain adaptation, a paradigm where user modifications to system-generated artifacts serve as implicit domain specification that reshapes LLM-powered multi-agent reasoning behavior. Through our system Seedentia, a web-based multi-agent framework for sense-making, we demonstrate bidirectional semantic links between generated artifacts and system reasoning. Our approach enables specification bootstrapping where vague initial prompts evolve into precise domain specifications through iterative human-AI collaboration, implicit knowledge transfer through reverse-engineered user edits, and in-context learning where agent behavior adapts based on observed correction patterns. We present results from an evaluation with domain experts who generated and modified research questions from academic papers. Our system extracted 46 domain knowledge entries from user modifications, demonstrating the feasibility of capturing implicit expertise through edit patterns, though the limited sample size constrains conclusions about systematic quality improvements.

</details>


### [121] [More Than "Means to an End": Supporting Reasoning with Transparently Designed AI Data Science Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24877)
*Venkatesh Sivaraman,Patrick Vossler,Adam Perer,Julian Hong,Jean Feng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 生成式AI工具能帮助非专家完成复杂数据科学任务，但其端到端方法不支持用户评估替代方案和重构问题，这在高风险医疗领域尤为关键。论文通过两个医疗AI系统案例，发现成功的关键在于围绕精心设计的中间产物构建AI工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI工具虽然能帮助更多人处理数据，但其端到端方法存在局限性：不支持用户评估替代方法和重构问题，而这对于高风险医疗领域的开放式任务至关重要。需要探索如何设计AI系统以促进有效的数据科学思维。

Method: 通过反思两个为医疗环境设计的AI数据科学系统，分析它们如何作为思维工具发挥作用。重点研究这些系统如何围绕精心设计的中间产物（如可读查询语言、概念定义、输入输出示例）构建AI工作流。

Result: 研究发现，尽管AI过程的其他部分可能不透明，但这些中间产物帮助用户推理重要的分析选择、完善初始问题，并贡献他们的独特知识。成功的关键在于构建围绕有意设计的中间产物的AI工作流。

Conclusion: 论文邀请HCI社区思考何时以及如何设计中间产物以促进有效的数据科学思维。在医疗等高危领域，中间产物设计对于帮助用户理解AI过程、做出明智决策至关重要。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) tools can now help people perform complex data science tasks regardless of their expertise. While these tools have great potential to help more people work with data, their end-to-end approach does not support users in evaluating alternative approaches and reformulating problems, both critical to solving open-ended tasks in high-stakes domains. In this paper, we reflect on two AI data science systems designed for the medical setting and how they function as tools for thought. We find that success in these systems was driven by constructing AI workflows around intentionally-designed intermediate artifacts, such as readable query languages, concept definitions, or input-output examples. Despite opaqueness in other parts of the AI process, these intermediates helped users reason about important analytical choices, refine their initial questions, and contribute their unique knowledge. We invite the HCI community to consider when and how intermediate artifacts should be designed to promote effective data science thinking.

</details>


### [122] [Rethinking Health Agents: From Siloed AI to Collaborative Decision Mediators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24986)
*Ray-Yuan Chung,Xuhai Xu,Ari Pollack*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出将AI重新定位为医疗协作中的参与者而非独立助手，通过儿科慢性肾病案例展示孤立AI工具的局限性，并提出了促进多方共享理解的AI协作框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗AI系统大多以孤立方式运行，服务于个体用户而非医疗核心的多方协作关系，这种使用方式会加剧患者、照护者和临床医生之间的理解碎片化和目标错位。

Method: 通过临床验证的虚构儿科慢性肾病案例研究，分析依从性问题的根源；提出AI协作者的概念框架，强调揭示上下文信息、协调心智模型、搭建共享理解，同时保留人类决策权。

Result: 研究发现依从性问题源于碎片化的情境意识和错位的目标，而孤立使用通用AI工具无法解决这些协作缺口；提出的框架旨在促进多方医疗协作中的共享理解。

Conclusion: 应将AI重新构想为嵌入多方护理交互中的协作者，而非独立助手，通过设计支持情境意识、心智模型协调和共享理解构建的AI系统来改善医疗协作和决策质量。

Abstract: Large language model based health agents are increasingly used by health consumers and clinicians to interpret health information and guide health decisions. However, most AI systems in healthcare operate in siloed configurations, supporting individual users rather than the multi-stakeholder relationships central to healthcare. Such use can fragment understanding and exacerbate misalignment among patients, caregivers, and clinicians. We reframe AI not as a standalone assistant, but as a collaborator embedded within multi-party care interactions. Through a clinically validated fictional pediatric chronic kidney disease case study, we show that breakdowns in adherence stem from fragmented situational awareness and misaligned goals, and that siloed use of general-purpose AI tools does little to address these collaboration gaps. We propose a conceptual framework for designing AI collaborators that surface contextual information, reconcile mental models, and scaffold shared understanding while preserving human decision authority.

</details>


### [123] [Co-designing for the Triad: Design Considerations for Collaborative Decision-Making Technologies in Pediatric Chronic Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24993)
*Ray-Yuan Chung,Jaime Snyder,Zixuan Xu,Daeun Yoo,Athena C. Ortega,Wanda Pratt,Aaron Wightman,Ryan Hutson,Cozumel Pruette,Ari Pollack*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过协同设计工作坊探讨数字技术如何支持儿科慢性病护理中的协作决策，识别了情境意识各层面的障碍，并提出了支持持续决策实践、对齐心智模型、平衡照顾者支持与青少年自主性发展的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科慢性护理中患者、照顾者和医疗提供者之间的三元关系带来了独特挑战，不同决策群体的价值观差异、角色冲突和不对称的情境意识阻碍了协作，影响了健康结果，因此需要支持协作决策的数字技术。

Method: 研究者与6名慢性肾病青少年、6名照顾者和7名医疗提供者进行了协同设计工作坊，探索数字技术如何设计以支持协作决策。

Result: 研究发现情境意识各层面都存在障碍：个体认知和情感限制、心智模型错位、护理目标相关的冲突关系。这些障碍影响了协作决策的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究提出了支持持续决策实践、对齐心智模型、平衡照顾者支持与青少年自主性发展、揭示潜在护理挑战的设计启示，推进了促进共享理解和赋能家庭的协作决策技术设计。

Abstract: In pediatric chronic care, the triadic relationship among patients, caregivers, and healthcare providers introduces unique challenges for youth in managing their conditions. Diverging values, roles, and asymmetrical situational awareness across decision-maker groups often hinder collaboration and affect health outcomes, highlighting the need to support collaborative decision-making. We conducted co-design workshops with 6 youth with chronic kidney disease, 6 caregivers, and 7 healthcare providers to explore how digital technologies can be designed to support collaborative decision-making. Findings identify barriers across all levels of situational awareness, ranging from individual cognitive and emotional constraints, misaligned mental models, to relational conflicts regarding care goals. We propose design implications that support continuous decision-making practice, align mental models, balance caregiver support and youth autonomy development, and surface potential care challenges. This work advances the design of collaborative decision-making technologies that promote shared understanding and empower families in pediatric chronic care.

</details>


### [124] [Framing Data Choices: How Pre-Donation Exploration Design Influence Data Donation Behavior and Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24995)
*Zeya Chen,Zach Pino,Ruth Schmidt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了三种数据探索干预设计对数据捐赠参与率的影响，发现社会比较设计效果最佳（87.5%），自我聚焦设计次之（62.5%），集体聚焦设计最差（37.5%）且引发隐私担忧


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据捐赠作为一种新兴的用户中心数据收集方法，在参与者意愿与实际捐赠之间存在差距。虽然技术上和监管上被宣传为"捐赠者中心"，但如何呈现数据选择并干预个体行为的设计视角仍未充分探索。研究聚焦于捐赠前的数据探索阶段，这是确保充分和有意义的知情参与的关键环节。

Method: 通过一项真实世界的数据捐赠研究（N=24），评估了三种数据探索干预设计：自我聚焦设计、社会比较设计和集体聚焦设计。研究比较了不同设计框架对捐赠参与率的影响。

Result: 社会比较设计表现最佳（87.5%捐赠率），自我聚焦设计次之（62.5%），而集体聚焦设计效果最差（37.5%）且引发了"视角混淆"和隐私担忧。这表明选择框架显著影响捐赠参与。

Conclusion: 研究表明战略性的数据框架设计能够将数据捐赠视为行为挑战加以解决，揭示了设计在数据捐赠和参与式公共部门创新中的关键但未充分探索的作用。社会比较设计通过提供参照点增强了捐赠动机。

Abstract: Data donation, an emerging user-centric data collection method for public sector research, faces a gap between participant willingness and actual donation. This suggests a design absence in practice: while promoted as "donor-centered" with technical and regulational advances, a design perspective on how data choices are presented and intervene on individual behaviors remain underexplored. In this paper, we focus on pre-donation data exploration, a key stage for adequately and meaningful informed participation. Through a real-world data donation study (N=24), we evaluated three data exploration interventions (self-focused, social comparison, collective-only). Findings show choice framing impacts donation participation. The "social comparison" design (87.5%) outperformed the "self-focused view" (62.5%) while a "collective-only" frame (37.5%) backfired, causing "perspective confusion" and privacy concerns. This study demonstrates how strategic data framing addresses data donation as a behavioral challenge, revealing design's critical yet underexplored role in data donation for participatory public sector innovation.

</details>


### [125] [TopoPilot: Reliable Conversational Workflow Automation for Topological Data Analysis and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25063)
*Nathaniel Gorski,Shusen Liu,Bei Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TopoPilot是一个可靠且可扩展的智能体框架，用于自动化复杂科学可视化工作流，通过系统防护和验证机制确保可靠性，在评估中成功率超过99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能体系统在生成科学可视化时存在可靠性问题：可能执行无效操作、引入细微但重要的错误，或在输入不完整时未能请求缺失信息。这些问题在超出标准基准复杂度的真实工作流中被放大，确保自主可视化管道的可靠性仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: TopoPilot采用以可靠性为中心的双智能体架构：编排器智能体将用户提示转换为由原子后端操作组成的工作流，验证器智能体在执行前评估这些工作流，强制执行结构有效性和语义一致性。采用模块化架构隔离组件，并引入故障模式分类法，为每类故障实施针对性防护措施。

Result: 在模拟100个提示的1000轮多轮对话评估中，包括对抗性和不可行请求，TopoPilot实现了超过99%的成功率，而缺乏全面防护和检查的基线系统成功率低于50%。

Conclusion: TopoPilot通过系统防护和验证机制解决了自主可视化工作流中的可靠性挑战，其双智能体架构和模块化设计不仅适用于拓扑数据分析和可视化，还能推广到其他可视化领域，显著提高了复杂科学可视化工作流的自动化可靠性。

Abstract: Recent agentic systems demonstrate that large language models can generate scientific visualizations from natural language. However, reliability remains a major limitation: systems may execute invalid operations, introduce subtle but consequential errors, or fail to request missing information when inputs are underspecified. These issues are amplified in real-world workflows, which often exceed the complexity of standard benchmarks. Ensuring reliability in autonomous visualization pipelines therefore remains an open challenge. We present TopoPilot, a reliable and extensible agentic framework for automating complex scientific visualization workflows. TopoPilot incorporates systematic guardrails and verification mechanisms to ensure reliable operation. While we focus on topological data analysis and visualization as a primary use case, the framework is designed to generalize across visualization domains. TopoPilot adopts a reliability-centered two-agent architecture. An orchestrator agent translates user prompts into workflows composed of atomic backend actions, while a verifier agent evaluates these workflows prior to execution, enforcing structural validity and semantic consistency. This separation of interpretation and verification reduces code-generation errors and enforces correctness guarantees. A modular architecture further improves robustness by isolating components and enabling seamless integration of new descriptors and domain-specific workflows without modifying the core system. To systematically address reliability, we introduce a taxonomy of failure modes and implement targeted safeguards for each class. In evaluations simulating 1,000 multi-turn conversations across 100 prompts, including adversarial and infeasible requests, TopoPilot achieves a success rate exceeding 99%, compared to under 50% for baselines without comprehensive guardrails and checks.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Benchmarks: How Users Evaluate AI Chat Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25220)
*Moiz Sadiq Awan,Muhammad Haris Noor,Muhammad Salman Munaf*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 用户满意度调查显示，Claude、ChatGPT和DeepSeek三大平台满意度无显著差异，用户将AI聊天工具视为可互换工具而非粘性生态系统，各平台因不同优势吸引用户，市场呈现竞争多元化而非赢家通吃格局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI聊天平台评估主要依赖自动化基准测试，缺乏对用户满意度、采用动机和挫折感的系统性跨平台比较研究。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过一致工具比较不同平台的用户体验差异。

Method: 采用跨平台调查方法，对388名活跃AI聊天用户进行问卷调查，比较七个主要平台（ChatGPT、Claude、Gemini、DeepSeek、Grok、Mistral和Llama）的满意度、采用驱动因素、用例表现和定性挫折感。

Result: 1. 前三名平台（Claude、ChatGPT和DeepSeek）满意度评分在统计上无显著差异，尽管它们在资金、团队规模和基准测试表现上存在巨大差异；2. 超过80%用户使用两个或更多平台，切换成本可忽略不计，用户将工具视为可互换实用程序；3. 各平台因不同优势吸引用户：ChatGPT因界面、Claude因答案质量、DeepSeek因口碑、Grok因内容政策；4. 幻觉和内容过滤是所有平台最常见的挫折点。

Conclusion: AI聊天市场呈现竞争多元化而非赢家通吃格局，专业化而非通用主导地位维持市场竞争。用户将AI工具视为可互换实用程序而非粘性生态系统，这为仅靠基准测试无法描述的市场提供了早期实证基准。

Abstract: Automated benchmarks dominate the evaluation of large language models, yet no systematic study has compared user satisfaction, adoption motivations, and frustrations across competing platforms using a consistent instrument. We address this gap with a cross-platform survey of 388 active AI chat users, comparing satisfaction, adoption drivers, use case performance, and qualitative frustrations across seven major platforms: ChatGPT, Claude, Gemini, DeepSeek, Grok, Mistral, and Llama. Three broad findings emerge. First, the top three platforms (Claude, ChatGPT, and DeepSeek) receive statistically indistinguishable satisfaction ratings despite vast differences in funding, team size, and benchmark performance. Second, users treat these tools as interchangeable utilities rather than sticky ecosystems: over 80% use two or more platforms, and switching costs are negligible. Third, each platform attracts users for different reasons: ChatGPT for its interface, Claude for answer quality, DeepSeek through word-of-mouth, and Grok for its content policy, suggesting that specialization, not generalist dominance, sustains competition. Hallucination and content filtering remain the most common frustrations across all platforms. These findings offer an early empirical baseline for a market that benchmarks alone cannot characterize, and point toward competitive plurality rather than winner-take-all consolidation among engaged users.

</details>


### [127] [Understanding Newcomer Persistence in Social VR: A Case Study of VRChat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25223)
*Qijia Chen,Andrea Bellucci,Giulio Jacucci*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨社交VR平台中新用户如何克服初始障碍并成功融入社区，通过访谈24名活跃用户识别了UI、社交规范和感官过载等挑战，以及相应的适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交VR平台作为新型在线互动空间，其新用户成功融入的路径尚未得到充分研究。与已深入研究的2D在线环境不同，VR空间中新用户的整合策略和适应机制存在研究空白，需要探索如何帮助新用户克服初始障碍并实现留存。

Method: 研究采用定性方法，访谈了24名活跃的社交VR用户（包括正在适应挑战的新用户和已成功融入的老用户），并进行了反思性主题分析，以识别新用户面临的具体障碍和成功适应策略。

Result: 研究发现新用户面临三大主要障碍：不熟悉的用户界面、复杂的社交规范以及VR特有的感官过载。成功的新用户通过主动构建社交意义、管理VR晕动症等策略来适应环境。研究还发现社交动态对VR特定问题（如VR晕动症）的管理有重要影响。

Conclusion: 研究扩展了对新用户持久性理解，超越了传统2D环境，强调了社交VR中社交动态对适应过程的影响。成功的新用户通过主动构建社交意义来克服缺乏明确目标的问题。研究提出了设计建议，以支持这些成功的整合路径，帮助新用户更好地融入社交VR社区。

Abstract: Newcomers are crucial for the growth of online communities, yet their successful integration into these spaces requires overcoming significant initial hurdles. Social Virtual Reality (VR) platforms are novel avenues that offer unprecedented online interaction experiences. Unlike well-studied two-dimensional online environments, the pathways to successful newcomer integration in online VR spaces are underexplored. Our research addresses this gap by examining the strategies used by newcomers to navigate early challenges in social VR and how they adapt. By focusing on active participants (ranging from newcomers currently navigating these hurdles to veterans who have successfully integrated) we isolate the specific strategies necessary for retention. We interviewed 24 active social VR users and conducted a reflexive thematic analysis. While participants identified barriers such as unfamiliar user interfaces, social norms, and overwhelming sensory input, our analysis reveals the adaptation strategies required to overcome them. Our findings expand on understanding newcomer persistence beyond traditional 2D environments, emphasizing how social dynamics influence the management of VR-specific issues like VR sickness during onboarding. Additionally, we highlight how successful newcomers overcome the lack of clear objectives in social VR by proactively constructing social meaning. We propose design suggestions to scaffold these successful integration pathways.

</details>


### [128] [Does Explanation Correctness Matter? Linking Computational XAI Evaluation to Human Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25251)
*Gregor Baer,Chao Zhang,Isel Grau,Pieter Van Gorp*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过用户实验发现，可解释AI的功能性正确性指标（如解释正确率）与人类理解效果并非线性相关，70%正确率是理解效果显著下降的阈值点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法通常使用功能性指标（如正确性）来评估解释质量，并假设更高的正确性会产生更好的人类理解。但这种关联性尚未通过控制不同正确性水平的实验进行验证，需要研究功能性指标与人类理解效果之间的实际关系。

Method: 研究者进行了用户研究（N=200），在时间序列分类任务中操纵解释正确性为四个水平（100%、85%、70%、55%）。参与者无法依赖领域知识或视觉直觉，而是基于解释来预测AI的决策（前向模拟）。通过控制正确性水平，测量参与者对AI决策模式的理解程度。

Result: 1. 正确性影响理解，但并非每个水平都有差异：与完全正确的解释相比，性能在70%和55%正确性水平下显著下降，但低于70%后进一步下降不会产生额外损失。
2. 较低的正确性减少了学习决策模式的参与者比例，而非均匀降低所有参与者的性能。
3. 即使完全正确的解释也不能保证理解，只有部分参与者达到高准确率。
4. 探索性分析显示，自我报告评分仅当解释完全正确且参与者已学习模式时才与表现相关。

Conclusion: 功能性正确性的差异并不总是转化为人类理解效果的差异，70%正确率是理解效果显著下降的关键阈值。这强调了需要针对人类结果验证功能性指标的重要性，仅依赖功能性指标评估XAI方法可能无法准确反映其对人类理解的实际影响。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods are commonly evaluated with functional metrics such as correctness, which computationally estimate how accurately an explanation reflects the model's reasoning. Higher correctness is assumed to produce better human understanding, but this link has not been tested experimentally with controlled levels. We conducted a user study (N=200) that manipulated explanation correctness at four levels (100%, 85%, 70%, 55%) in a time series classification task where participants could not rely on domain knowledge or visual intuition and instead predicted the AI's decisions based on explanations (forward simulation). Correctness affected understanding, but not at every level: performance dropped at 70% and 55% correctness relative to fully correct explanations, while further degradation below 70% produced no additional loss. Rather than shifting performance uniformly, lower correctness decreased the proportion of participants who learned the decision pattern. At the same time, even fully correct explanations did not guarantee understanding, as only a subset of participants achieved high accuracy. Exploratory analyses showed that self-reported ratings correlated with demonstrated performance only when explanations were fully correct and participants had learned the pattern. These findings show that not all differences in functional correctness translate to differences in human understanding, underscoring the need to validate functional metrics against human outcomes.

</details>


### [129] [Visual or Textual: Effects of Explanation Format and Personal Characteristics on the Perception of Explanations in an Educational Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25624)
*Qurat Ul Ain,Mohamed Amine Chatti,Nasim Yazdian Varjani,Farah Kamal,Astrid Rosenthal-von der Pütten*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 视觉解释在推荐系统中比文本解释更有效，能提升用户感知控制、透明度、信任和满意度，且不受用户个人特征影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释对于提升推荐系统的透明度、信任和用户满意度至关重要，但不同解释格式（视觉vs文本）如何适用于具有不同个人特征的用户尚不明确

Method: 采用被试内用户研究（n=54），在教育推荐系统中比较视觉和文本解释，使用稳健混合效应模型分析大五人格特质、认知需求、决策风格、可视化熟悉度和技术专长等个人特征的调节作用

Result: 设计良好的视觉解释（简单、交互式、选择性、易于理解的可视化）能清晰直观地展示用户偏好与推荐之间的联系，对大多数用户都能提升感知控制、透明度、适当信任和满意度，且不受个人特征影响

Conclusion: 视觉解释在教育推荐系统中比文本解释更有效，研究还提出了一套支持教育推荐系统中解释有效设计的指南

Abstract: Explanations are central to improving transparency, trust, and user satisfaction in recommender systems (RS), yet it remains unclear how different explanation formats (visual vs. textual) are suited to users with different personal characteristics (PCs). To this end, we report a within-subject user study (n=54) comparing visual and textual explanations and examine how explanation format and PCs jointly influence perceived control, transparency, trust, and satisfaction in an educational recommender system (ERS). Using robust mixed-effects models, we analyze the moderating effects of a wide range of PCs, including Big Five traits, need for cognition, decision making style, visualization familiarity, and technical expertise. Our results show that a well-designed visual, simple, interactive, selective, easy to understand visualization that clearly and intuitively communicates how user preferences are linked to recommendations, fosters perceived control, transparency, appropriate trust, and satisfaction in the ERS for most users, independent of their PCs. Moreover, we derive a set of guidelines to support the effective design of explanations in ERSs.

</details>


### [130] [Clinician Perspectives on Type 1 Diabetes Guidelines and Glucose Data Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25631)
*Mohammed Basheikh,Rujiravee Kongdee,Hood Thabit,Bijan Parsia,Sarah Clinch,Simon Harper*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过问卷调查探讨医疗专业人员对1型糖尿病管理的看法，包括指南应用优先级和患者数据解读能力评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解医疗专业人员如何应用临床指南管理1型糖尿病，以及他们对患者使用血糖监测设备数据能力的看法

Method: 采用两部分在线问卷：第一部分调查临床医生对指南的优先排序和应用；第二部分评估医生对患者数据解读和治疗决策能力的认知；样本为英国19名糖尿病相关医疗专业人员

Result: 发现血糖管理在临床指南中被优先考虑，建议常根据患者个体需求调整；医生普遍认为血糖监测设备数据易于患者解读，且患者偶尔能基于数据做出正确治疗决策

Conclusion: 医疗专业人员重视血糖管理并个性化调整指南，同时认为患者具备一定数据解读能力，但治疗决策能力仍有提升空间

Abstract: This study explored healthcare professionals' perspectives on the management of Type 1 Diabetes Mellitus (T1DM) through a two-part questionnaire. The first part examined how clinicians prioritise and apply current clinical guidelines, including the relative importance assigned to different aspects of T1DM management. The second part investigated clinicians' perceptions of patients' ability to interpret data from the glucose monitoring devices and to make appropriate treatment decisions. An online questionnaire was completed by 19 healthcare professionals working in diabetes-related roles in the United Kingdom. The findings revealed that blood glucose management is prioritised within clinical guidance and that advice is frequently tailored to individual patient needs. Additionally, clinicians generally perceive that data presented in glucose monitoring devices is easy for patients to interpret and based on these data, they believe that patients occasionally make correct treatment decisions.
